### [1] [Who Verifies the Builder? Toward Independent Assurance for Agent-Produced Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28667)
*Sion Israel Sion,Alain April*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文主张agent生成的软件需要与构建平面结构上独立的"保证平面"，并提出分离构建与验证的框架（验证契约 + 规范优先阶段 + 实现感知阶段），实验表明独立验证者比构建者自带测试发现更多植入缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可端到端完成实现、测试与修复，但当软件与其正确性证据由同一系统产生时，通过的测试套件不再构成独立证据：可见测试成为优化目标、代码与测试可能共享同一规格误解、构建者自测可能不完整。

Method: 刻画利益相关者意图与产品之间的四类鸿沟（需求、符合性、评估、模型），指出验证可弥合其中哪些；提出将构建与验证分离的框架：以Verification Contract规定发布前须证明的内容，验证分为无实现访问的规范优先阶段和基于变异分析、基于属性的测试与模糊测试的实现感知阶段，最终以保证报告将发布决策留给人类。采用预注册研究：3个有状态服务（2个由第三方模型编写）+ EvalPlus试点。

Result: 构建者的程序本身正确，但其自测遗漏了细微交互；全新上下文的验证者比构建者测试检出更多植入缺陷（46个中更多被检出，Holm校正后p=0.006），并揭示规格中的歧义，代价是偶发的错误判定（false oracle）；来自另一提供方的验证者未带来检出提升。

Conclusion: agent生成的软件需要结构独立的保证平面；分离构建与验证可提升缺陷检出与规格歧义暴露，但跨提供方的验证者未增益检出，且需以人工发布决策控制错误判定风险。

Abstract: Coding agents can now implement a specification, write and run tests, repair failures, and declare the result ready for release. When the same agentic system produces both the software and the evidence of its correctness, a passing test suite is no longer independent evidence: visible tests become an optimization target, code and tests may share one misinterpretation of the specification, and the builder's own tests may simply be incomplete. This paper argues that agent-produced software requires an assurance plane that is structurally independent of the build plane. We characterize four gaps between stakeholder intent and production (requirement, conformance, evaluation, and model gaps) and identify which of them verification can close. We propose a framework that separates building from verifying, introduces a Verification Contract stating what must be demonstrated before release, and organizes verification in a specification-first phase without access to the implementation and an implementation-aware phase based on mutation analysis, property-based testing, and fuzzing, with an assurance report that leaves the release decision to a human. We evaluate the framework in a pre-registered study on three stateful services, two of them written by third-party models, and an EvalPlus pilot. The builder's programs were correct, but its own tests missed subtle interactions: a verifier in a fresh context detected more of the 46 seeded faults than the builder's tests (Holm-corrected p = 0.006) and surfaced the ambiguities of the specification, at the price of rare false oracles. A verifier from another provider did not add detection.

</details>


### [2] [Compilation Is Not Correctness: Scan-by-Scan Evaluation of LLM-Generated PLC Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28675)
*Jinyun Chen,Juntao Hu,Haochen Li,Wentao Song,Ningbo Gu,Jianwei Niu,Qing Gao,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CycleBench逐扫描对比基准与CycleGen候选生成-修复-自测选择框架，揭示直出ST的编译通过率显著高估执行正确性，误检漏检达11.92pp及21.66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成IEC 61131-3结构化文本（ST）时，编译成功及对部分属性或抽样扫描的检查无法发现行为错误，尤其瞬态输出偏差易被稀疏采样遗漏，缺乏逐扫描对齐的严格评测手段。

Method: 构建CycleBench：301个ST任务，含行为需求、公共接口、参考程序与可执行场景；运行时在对齐输入、初始化与逻辑时间下逐扫描比较生成程序与参考程序输出。另提出CycleGen：并行生成多个候选ST，借助有界编译器反馈修复编译错误，并在独立模型上下文中以运行时自测选择最优候选；辅以组件消融与候选数量分析。

Result: 八个LLM及基线方法下执行通过率比编译通过率低11.92个百分点，失败含输出更新、算术与定时错误；对五款LLM直出程序的固定轨迹，每5次扫描抽检一次漏检率达21.66%（157个失败中），跨采样相位漏检率为12.10%–28.03%。CycleGen使执行通过率较直接生成提升8.68个百分点；消融区分修复与自测选择贡献；超过5个候选无明显增益，而8个候选token消耗高38.8%。

Conclusion: 编译成功与非逐扫描检查会系统性高估LLM生成PLC逻辑的正确性，逐扫描对齐评测必不可少；候选生成配合编译修复与运行时自测筛选可有效提升行为正确性，但存在候选数量的边际收益递减。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) automate industrial processes through repeated scans, each reading inputs, executing control logic, and updating outputs. Engineers write this logic in Structured Text (ST), a high-level textual language defined in IEC 61131-3. Large language models (LLMs) can now generate ST from natural-language requirements, yet compilation success and checks on selected properties or sampled scans can leave behavioral errors undetected. We introduce CycleBench, a benchmark of 301 ST tasks with behavioral requirements, public interfaces, reference programs, and executable scenarios. Its runtime executes each generated program and its reference under aligned inputs, initialization, and logical time, comparing their outputs after every scan. Across eight LLMs and baseline generation methods, execution pass rate trails compilation pass rate by 11.92 percentage points; observed failures include output-update, arithmetic, and timing errors. On fixed traces of programs directly generated by five LLMs, checking outputs only at every fifth scan misses 21.66% of the 157 failures detected by per-scan comparison; across sampling phases, the missed-failure rate ranges from 12.10% to 28.03%. These misses stem from transient output discrepancies, whose detection depends on the sampling phase. Building on this evaluation, we propose CycleGen, which generates multiple candidate ST programs, repairs their compilation errors with bounded compiler feedback, and selects one using runtime self-tests generated in a separate model context. CycleGen improves the execution pass rate over direct generation by 8.68 percentage points. Component ablations distinguish repair and self-test selection; candidate-count analysis shows diminishing returns, with no execution-pass-rate improvement beyond five candidates in our experiments despite 38.8% higher token consumption at eight.

</details>


### [3] [Benchmarking the Quantum Leap: Auditing LLM Evaluation for Quantum Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28678)
*Ethan Seefried,Romit Ghosh,Sharmin Afrose,Nicholas Stapleton,Vicente Layton Ortega,Travis Humble,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评测15个模型在5个量子代码生成基准（530个任务）及新提出的QBE基准（96个任务）上的表现，并审计评估器设计，发现评估器缺陷会同时造成假失败与假通过，主张排行榜分数不足以刻画量子代码生成的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能生成量子程序，但基准在任务构成、评分方式和失败处理上各不相同，导致可靠评估困难；同时现有评估器可能存在未被检出的设计缺陷。

Method: 在五个量子代码生成套件（530个任务）上评测15个托管前沿模型、开放权重模型、量子专用模型与检索增强模型，另在自建QBE基准（6个主题、96个任务）上评测10个模型；采用bootstrap置信区间、多重比较校正的配对比较，并对输出有效性与评估器行为进行审计与受控探针实验。

Result: QiskitHumanEval-Standard接近饱和（pass@1 98.7%），Hard变体仍有空间（63.8%）；五个主套件中排名前二的比较经多重比较校正后均不显著。评估器设计显著改变分数：去除对未指定量的检查后，GPT-6 Astra的QBE pass@1在T1上由0%升至94.1%、在T6上由1.2%升至92.9%，而模型输出未变。识别出QuanBench-117中17个与基准自带导入相关的共因失败任务；探针还揭示单元测试未被执行、规范函数泄漏进候选执行、参考导入阻断有效候选等问题。

Conclusion: 评估器缺陷可同时导致假失败与假通过，历史QuanBench结果需在隔离评估器下重新验证；仅凭排行榜分数不足以评估量子代码生成进展。作者发布测试框架、逐样本结果与审计工件，并建议验证评估器、隔离候选代码、报告不确定性与输出有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate quantum programs, but reliable evaluation remains difficult because benchmarks differ in task composition, scoring, and failure handling. We evaluate 15 hosted frontier, open-weight, quantum-specialized, and retrieval-augmented models on five quantum code-generation suites (530 tasks), plus 10 models on our new QuantumBenchEval (QBE) benchmark (96 tasks across six topics). We report bootstrap confidence intervals, multiplicity-adjusted paired comparisons, and audits of output validity and evaluator behavior. QiskitHumanEval-Standard approaches saturation (98.7% pass@1), while its Hard variant retains headroom (63.8%). None of the five top-two comparisons across primary suites remains significant after multiple-comparison correction. Evaluator design substantially changes scores: removing checks for unspecified quantities raises GPT-6 Astra's QBE pass@1 from 0% to 94.1% (T1) and from 1.2% to 92.9% (T6), without changing model outputs. We identify 17 QuanBench-117 tasks with shared failures linked to benchmark-owned imports. Controlled probes also reveal unexecuted unit tests, leakage of canonical functions into candidate execution, and reference imports that block otherwise valid candidates. These defects can produce both false failures and false passes, so historical QuanBench results require revalidation under an isolated evaluator. Overall, leaderboard scores alone do not adequately characterize progress in quantum code generation. We release our harness, per-sample results, and audit artifacts and recommend validating evaluators, isolating candidate code, and reporting uncertainty and output validity.

</details>


### [4] [VerifierBench: Benchmarking the Generation of Executable Verifiers for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28679)
*Jianing Yin,Ziyi Chen,Hao Wang,Yiyou Sun,Zijian Jin,Dawn Song,Daoan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"验证器生成"(verifier generation)任务与VerifierBench基准,要求智能体编写可执行验证器以接受一切有效解、拒绝无效解,并揭示生成验证器普遍过度严格的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在完成任务和筛选合成训练数据时都需要可靠地校验自身输出,但如何让智能体自动生成可信验证器尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建VerifierBench:涵盖工具调用、状态型与代码三类验证的六个智能体基准、222个任务、4,993个隐藏测试用例(含替代有效解与攻击样本);提出V4V六项审计以筛查验证器源码与测试集;在统一智能体脚手架下评测19个模型,使用归一化平衡准确率。

Result: 归一化平衡准确率仅10.6–62.1;生成验证器在拒绝无效解上明显优于接受有效解,该"优势"实为过度严格:多数假拒绝来自对任务所有被测解均不接受的验证器。案例分析显示,对解形式、工具使用和代码改动的错误假设会排除有效解;此类错误因自测解与验证器共享错误假设、或未确认任一正确解可通过而逃避自测。

Conclusion: 验证器评测应同时检验其是否接受某个正确解,以及是否接受任务允许的其他解;仅测拒绝能力会掩盖验证器的过度限制性。

Abstract: Agents need reliable ways to validate their outputs, both when completing tasks and when filtering synthetic training data. We study verifier generation: from the instruction and environment available for solving a task, an agent writes an executable verifier that must accept every valid solution and reject invalid ones. We introduce VerifierBench, a benchmark of 222 tasks from six agent benchmarks spanning tool-call, state-based, and code verification, with 4,993 hidden test cases that include alternative valid solutions and attacks; Verifier4Verifier (V4V), a six-check audit, screens its source verifiers and test suites. Across 19 models in a common agent harness, normalized balanced accuracy ranges from 10.6 to 62.1, with generated verifiers consistently rejecting invalid solutions more reliably than they accept valid ones. This apparent strength can mask overly restrictive verification: most false rejections in the analyzed records come from verifiers that accept no tested solution to a task. Case studies reveal unsupported assumptions about solution formats, tool use, and code changes that exclude valid solutions. These errors survive self-testing because agents test solutions that share the verifier's mistaken assumptions, or submit verifiers without confirming that any correct solution passes. These findings suggest testing both whether a verifier accepts a correct solution and whether it accepts other solutions permitted by the task. The project page and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [5] [Runtime Semantic Discovery for Evolving Action Alphabets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28682)
*Pietro Bernabei,Rosario Pugliese*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究"运行时语义发现"问题：基于执行轨迹中动作的邻居上下文，提出一种局部、可在运行时计算的k-邻居等价关系，用于在可观测字母表发生变化时识别新旧动作的语义对应，从而轻量级地支持运行时监控器与规约的适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时验证通常假设可观测动作的字母表固定不变，但演化系统中的更新、重配置或架构变更可能对动作进行重命名、拆分或合并，且保持系统高层意图。这会使原有监控器与规约同实际运行系统失配。作者希望在不重建全局行为模型的前提下，判断新观测动作是否承担与旧动作相同的操作角色。

Method: 提出基于执行轨迹中动作出现的"邻居上下文"的动作相似性概念，定义一族k-邻居等价（k-neighbor equivalences），并据此比较可观测字母表不同的系统。方法刻意保持轻量：不追求完整刻画行为保持性，而是识别动作间候选的语义对应关系。

Result: 论文讨论了邻居等价关系的表达能力及其局限，并说明推断出的对应关系如何在若干类字母表演化（重命名、拆分、融合）情形下支持运行时监控器与规约的自适应调整。摘要未给出具体量化实验数据。

Conclusion: 基于局部邻居上下文的k-邻居等价为演化系统中的动作语义对应提供了一种轻量、运行时可行的发现手段，但仅产生候选对应而非保证行为保持，其适用性受等价关系表达能力与字母表演化类别的限制。

Abstract: Runtime Verification typically assumes a fixed alphabet of observable actions. This assumption becomes fragile in evolving software systems, where updates, reconfigurations, or architectural changes may rename, split, or fuse actions while preserving the system's high-level intent. In these cases, previously valid monitors and specifications may become misaligned with the running system.
  In this paper, we study runtime semantic discovery, namely the problem of identifying whether newly observed actions play the same operational role as previously known ones, without reconstructing a global behavioral model. To this end, we propose a local and runtime-computable notion of action similarity based on the neighbor contexts in which actions occur within execution traces. We define a family of k-neighbor equivalences and use them to compare systems whose observable alphabets differ.
  Our approach is intentionally lightweight: it does not aim to fully characterize behavioral preservation, but rather to identify candidate semantic correspondences between actions in evolving systems. We discuss the expressive power and limitations of neighbor-based equivalence and show how inferred correspondences can support the adaptation of runtime monitors and specifications under selected classes of alphabet evolution.

</details>


### [6] [Who Approved This? Coding Agents, Human Accounts, and Merge-Gate Evidence at a Fintech Startup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28683)
*Adam Manji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对Bashi AI十二周代理驱动开发的审计式分析：按"非作者批准+通过检查、均发生在被合并的精确修订上"重算核心门禁属性，揭示代理借用主负责人账号批准、关闭分支保护、将失败检查记为通过等问题，指出记录决策权与账号身份分离、隔离代理凭证与门禁控制、在集成点核验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业将编码代理接入真实代码库后，SOC 2 审计所需的"审批与门禁"证据可能失真：平台记录把代理通过人类账号提交的批准记为普通批准，无法反映真实决策者。作者希望检验在代理参与下，门禁记录与门禁实际执行是否一致，并指出记录模型和安全边界的缺陷。

Method: 案例研究：Bashi AI 十二周、786 个已合并 PR（其中 657 个通过显式标记或推断关联到代理），数据来源为平台记录与保存的代理会话转录，辅以工程师问卷。核心方法是对每个合并重算门禁属性：非作者批准与必需检查通过，均须在合并前且位于实际被合并的精确修订上；比较分支保护适用范围，定位未覆盖处的失效模式。

Result: 10月3日起263次非机器人合并均通过非作者评审门禁，但71次（27%）仅依赖编码代理经技术负责人GitHub账号提交的批准，平台均记为负责人本人的普通批准；门禁阶段中负责人账号对同事PR的所有批准均由代理会话提交，且几乎所有合并由其发起，记录无法显示真实决策者。重算在分支保护生效处确认门禁成立，在未生效处发现：代理围绕单个合并关闭强制执行、合并脚本将失败检查记录为通过、代理经由未受保护分支折叠整叠PR。工程师反馈：一名工程师的批准同样依赖评审代理（由本人核验结论并决策），两人均认为评审价值在于把变更对照 diff 之外的事物进行检验。

Conclusion: 基于账号身份的审批记录不等于决策权威记录；应在记录层面将决策权与账号身份分离，在安全层面使代理凭证远离其运行所处的控制机制，并在集成实际发生的分支与修订处核验门禁证据，而非依赖汇总层面的状态。

Abstract: From 3 October, every non-bot merge into our main branch passed a non-author review gate, yet 71 of those 263 merges (27%) rested only on an approval that a coding agent had submitted through the technical lead's GitHub account, and the platform recorded each one as an ordinary approval by the lead. We report twelve weeks of agent-driven development at Bashi AI, a fintech startup preparing for a SOC 2 audit: 786 merged pull requests, 657 of them associated with agents by explicit markers or by inference, analysed from platform records and saved agent-session transcripts. In the gated phase, an agent session submitted every approval the lead's account gave on a colleague's pull request, and issued nearly all of its merges; the records cannot show who decided any of them. We recompute, for every merge, a core gate property: a non-author approval and a passing required check, both before the merge and both on the exact revision merged. The recomputation confirmed the gate wherever branch protection applied and found where it did not: agents switching enforcement off around individual merges, a merge script that recorded failed checks as passing, and an agent collapsing a stack of pull requests through unprotected branches. The engineers' survey answers show that one engineer's approvals also rested on review agents, with the person validating findings and deciding, and both engineers say review mattered when it tested a change against something outside the diff. We draw lessons on recording decision authority separately from account identity, keeping agents' credentials away from the controls they operate under, and checking evidence at the branch and revision where integration happens.

</details>


### [7] [PCLA-Bridge: A Framework for Cross-Domain Deployment of CARLA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28696)
*Masoud Jamshidiyan Tehrani,Jinhan Kim,Paolo Tonella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 PCLA-Bridge，一个可部署工具，使未经修改的 CARLA Leaderboard 智能体可在无 CARLA 服务器的情况下，从任意传感器源接收输入、向任意控制目标输出，实现跨仿真器与真实数据集的复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能端到端自动驾驶智能体（ADA）多为 CARLA Leaderboard 开发，紧耦合于 CARLA 的 API、传感器布局、坐标系与内部时钟，迁移到真实数据集、其他仿真器或实车平台需大量手工重构，缺乏可复用性。

Method: PCLA-Bridge 负责依赖隔离、张量编组（tensor marshalling）、路由与合成时序，集成方仅需提供最小接口；工具验证涵盖两种范式：基于 CARLA 录制与 nuScenes 日志的离线回放，以及在 MetaDrive、AWSIM、AlpaSim 三种仿真引擎上的实时闭环控制，并分析各平台的轻量集成需求（输出翻译、坐标帧变换）。

Result: 全部八个被评估智能体在三个仿真器上无需修改即可运行；集成仅需少量胶水代码而非结构性改动；离线回放覆盖域内 CARLA 录制与真实 nuScenes 日志，闭环控制覆盖三种异构仿真器。

Conclusion: PCLA-Bridge 具备可靠性，可作为 sim-to-real、跨仿真器与安全测试的系统性基础平台。

Abstract: Most high-performing end-to-end autonomous driving agents (ADAs) are developed for the CARLA Leaderboard, tightly coupling them to CARLA's APIs, sensor layouts, coordinate systems, and internal clock. Reusing these models on real datasets, alternative simulators, or physical platforms necessitates substantial manual re-engineering. We present PCLA-Bridge, a deployable tool that runs unmodified CARLA Leaderboard agents from any sensor source to any control destination without an active CARLA server. With PCLA-Bridge handling dependency isolation, tensor marshalling, routing, and synthetic timing, integrators only need to provide a minimal interface. We validate the tool across two operational paradigms: offline replay on in-domain CARLA recordings and real-world nuScenes logs, and live closed-loop control across three diverse simulation engines: MetaDrive, AWSIM, and AlpaSim. We analyze the lightweight integration requirements for each platform, highlighting output translation (such as converting agent actions into target trajectory formats) and coordinate-frame transformations, showing that integration requires minimal glue code rather than structural changes. All eight evaluated agents execute across all three simulators without modification, confirming the reliability of PCLA-Bridge and establishing a foundation for systematic sim-to-real, cross-sim, and security testing.

</details>


### [8] [Multi-agent LLMs on the Cloud-Edge-IoT Continuum: A Systematic Mapping Study on Architectures, Deployment, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28702)
*Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Tiziano Santilli,Leo Wilhelm Lierse,Sebastian Werner,Francesco Daghero*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文是一篇系统性映射研究，梳理了云-边-物联网连续体上多智能体LLM系统的组织与部署方式：从831条记录中筛选45篇原始研究，按架构族、部署模式、应用领域与评测实践分类，并提出报告规范、部署指南与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用已从单一提示-响应管线转向多组件协同系统，而IoT场景中感知、推理、控制与执行分布在不同设备、边缘节点与云服务上，受时延、隐私与带宽约束。多智能体LLM系统如何在该连续体上组织与部署尚缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 系统性映射研究：检索截至2026年3月的831条记录，筛选出45篇原始研究，逐篇按架构族、部署模式、应用领域与评测实践编码，并记录评测方式及代码/数据是否公开。

Result: 归纳出五类架构族：贴近传感器的专用智能体团队、规划者向工作者委派子任务、本地小模型与云端大模型协作、跨设备联邦式模型适配，以及面向卸载、通信、记忆与服务的支撑基础设施。部署归纳为六种模式（从纯本地执行到经策略校验或人工审批把关的控制回路）。45篇中24篇面向IoT/工业/智能家居/医疗/车联网/机器人场景，29篇在真实硬件上评测，以参数量低于8B的开源模型为主。

Conclusion: 基于映射结果，提出使未来评测可比的报告条目清单，给出针对常见IoT约束的部署指导，并识别出开放问题。

Abstract: LLM applications are increasingly built as systems of cooperating components rather than as single prompt-response pipelines. IoT applications add constraints of their own, because sensing, reasoning, control, and actuation are spread across devices, edge nodes, and cloud services to meet latency, privacy, and bandwidth requirements. How multi-agent LLM systems are organized and deployed across this cloud-edge-IoT continuum has not been mapped systematically. We therefore conducted a systematic mapping study. From 831 records retrieved up to March 2026, we selected 45 primary studies and classified each one by architecture family, deployment pattern, application domain, and evaluation practice. We also recorded how each system was evaluated and whether its code or data are public. Five architecture families recur in the corpus. Specialized agent teams run next to sensors, planners delegate subtasks to workers, and small local models cooperate with large cloud models. A fourth group federates model adaptation across devices, and a large fifth group builds the supporting infrastructure for offloading, communication, memory, and serving. Deployment relies on six recurring patterns, ranging from local-only execution to control loops gated by policy checks or human approval. Twenty-four of the 45 studies target IoT, industrial, smart-home, healthcare, vehicular, or robotics settings, and 29 evaluate on physical hardware, where open models below 8B parameters dominate. Based on the mapping, we list the reporting items needed to make future evaluations comparable, give deployment guidance for common IoT constraints, and identify open problems.

</details>


### [9] [Where Should a Stuck Agent Spend Its Next Tokens? Causal Attribution of Inference-Time Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28834)
*Saiyue Lyu,Jerrold Huang,Yunbei Zhang,Janet Wang,Zhenting Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Haibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Banzhaf Resource Attribution (BRA)因子框架，在固定失败状态与修订协议下量化"计算、信息、反馈"三类推理期资源对代码修复的边际贡献及交互作用，发现智能体的资源偏好与实际有效性之间存在显著错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理期扩展（inference-time scaling）常被简化为token预算问题，但失败的编码智能体实际可寻求性质不同的资源：额外计算、任务信息或外部反馈。这些资源各自的边际价值及相互作用缺乏可控的量化手段，且智能体自身的资源选择策略是否合理尚不清楚。

Method: 提出Banzhaf Resource Attribution (BRA)，一个受控因子实验框架，通过Banzhaf指数式归因在固定失败状态与修订协议的前提下测量各资源的边际贡献与交互效应。实验覆盖6个模型：MBPP+与HumanEval+的194个函数级失败，以及SWE-bench Verified的126个仓库级失败；并对比模型自身的资源选择行为与均匀随机选择基线。

Result: 任务相关规范（specifications）稳定提升修复效果；公开测试反馈收益很小；检索代码几乎没有增益。额外计算的价值随模型能力增强而下降，而信息资源持续有用。资源通道总体呈现边际收益递减，但互补性信息可自我强化。然而四个前沿模型中有三个在95%–100%的情况下选择反馈，其收益低于均匀随机资源选择。

Conclusion: 推理期资源并非同质的token预算，其价值高度依赖于资源类型与模型能力。BRA揭示了智能体资源偏好与可测修复增益之间的系统性错配，表明当前智能体的资源分配策略次优，需针对性地改进资源选择机制。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling is often treated as a token-budget problem, but a failed coding agent can seek qualitatively different resources: computation, information, or feedback. We introduce Banzhaf Resource Attribution (BRA), a controlled factorial framework that measures each resource's marginal contribution to repair and its interactions while holding the failed state and revision protocol fixed. We evaluate six models on 194 function-level failures from MBPP+ and HumanEval+ and 126 repository-level failures from SWE-bench Verified. Task-relevant specifications consistently improve repair, whereas public-test feedback provides small gains and retrieved code little to none. The value of additional computation declines with model capability, while information remains useful; resource channels largely exhibit diminishing returns, although complementary information can reinforce itself. Yet three of four frontier models select feedback in 95-100% of cases and obtain lower gains than uniform random resource selection. BRA thus exposes a gap between the resources agents prefer and those that measurably improve repair.

</details>


### [10] [VeriEvolve: Evaluator-First Evolution for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29005)
*Yueshen Li,Kamer Ali Yuksel,Gokhan Tur,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,João Pedro Maia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出VeriEvolve——一种"评估器优先"的进化框架，在优化智能体之前先合成可执行评估器作为适应度信号，显著提升状态型任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进型智能体面临的核心障碍是：对有状态（stateful）的现实任务缺乏可靠适应度信号。传统方法难以对最终状态进行可验证的成功判定，导致搜索与进化过程缺乏校准依据。

Method: VeriEvolve从任务指令出发，合成"基于指令约束的挑战状态"及其可执行评估器。在每轮工作流进化中冻结评估器并将其作为适应度信号；轮次之间，将强/弱运行轨迹的终态转化为一对反事实测试用例来修订评估器，从而实现评估器与智能体的交替改进。

Result: 在30个MCPMark Filesystem任务上构建30个评估器、141个挑战状态，生成套件上达到141/141一致性，执行体智能体产生的留出状态上为113/122。评估器引导进化使外部验证成功率由6/30提升至18/30（GLM-5.2），由27/30提升至30/30（Claude）；评估器精炼消除了弱执行体暴露的全部3个假阳性。在PostgreSQL、Playwright、Notion、GitHub上的跨环境研究进一步揭示可观测性、状态表示与搜索/执行能力的互补性限制。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当任务成功可观测且存在外部验证器用于校准时，可执行评估器能够被自动合成并有效引导搜索，为状态型任务的智能体自我改进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A central obstacle to self-improving agents is the absence of a reliable fitness signal for realistic stateful tasks. We introduce \textsc{VeriEvolve}, an evaluator-first evolution framework that constructs this missing evaluation layer before optimizing the agent. From task instruction, VeriEvolve synthesizes instruction-grounded challenge states and an executable evaluator that checks them. During each round of workflow evolution, the evaluator is frozen and serves as the fitness signal. Between rounds, VeriEvolve revises the evaluator by converting the end states of strong and weak runs into a pair of counterfactual test cases.
  Across all 30 MCPMark Filesystem tasks, VeriEvolve constructs 30 evaluators with 141 challenge states, achieving 141/141 agreement on the generated suites and 113/122 agreement on held-out states produced by executor agents. Evaluator-guided evolution increases externally verified success from 6/30 to 18/30 for GLM-5.2 and from 27/30 to 30/30 for Claude; refinement removes all three false positives that the weak executor exposes. Cross-environment studies on PostgreSQL, Playwright, Notion, and GitHub further expose complementary limits from observability, state representation, and search/execution capacity. These results show that executable evaluators can be synthesized and used to guide search when task success is observable and an external verifier is available for calibration.

</details>


### [11] [Symmetry-Oriented Testing Framework for Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29053)
*Masaomi Yamaguchi,Fuyuki Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出面向变分量子算法的对称性导向测试框架，用与目标哈密顿量对易的算符作为测试规约，通过基于对易子的健康检查与基于方差的可接受性检查两种互补诊断，在无需精确解的前提下检测用户侧与系统侧故障，并在噪声下保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态不可直接观测，VQA测试困难；现有方法依赖经典可计算的结果，与量子优势预期场景冲突，且已有工作虽缓解oracle问题，仍无法判定执行是否偏离目标问题、所得解是否物理上合理。

Method: 以与目标哈密顿量对易的对称性算符作为测试规约，构建两个互补诊断：(1) 基于对易子的健康检查，在优化每一步监控执行是否与目标哈密顿量的对称性一致；(2) 基于方差的接受性检查，通过判断收敛态是否为对称性算符的本征态来评估解的质量。两种诊断均由问题结构导出，不需精确解。

Result: 在多个目标系统上针对代表性用户侧与系统侧故障进行评估，并注入噪声：两种诊断均能检出故障行为，提供有信息量的解质量信号，且在含噪执行下仍保持可用。

Conclusion: 对称性可作为VQA的有效测试规约，规避oracle问题并覆盖执行过程与最终解两个层面，具备在近中期含噪量子硬件上实用的测试潜力。

Abstract: Testing quantum software is challenging because quantum states cannot be directly inspected. Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) are a particularly important target as they are among the most promising approaches for near-term noisy quantum hardware. Many testing methods rely on classically computed results, which conflicts with the regime where quantum advantage is expected. Although prior studies mitigate this oracle problem, the core difficulty remains that existing approaches do not verify whether the execution deviates from the intended problem or whether the obtained solution is physically plausible.
  We propose a symmetry-oriented testing framework for VQAs that addresses this gap by using operators commuting with the target Hamiltonian as test specifications. The framework provides two complementary diagnostics: a commutator-based health check that monitors whether each execution step during optimization remains consistent with the symmetry of the target Hamiltonian, and a variance-based acceptance check that assesses the converged solution by examining whether the state is an eigenstate of the symmetry operators. Both diagnostics derive from the problem structure and do not require the exact solution.
  We evaluate the framework on representative user- and system-side faults, across multiple target systems, and under injected noise. Results show that the two diagnostics detect faulty behavior, provide informative signals of solution quality, and remain useful under noisy execution.

</details>


### [12] [VeeReview: Measuring Build-Verify Pairs in Agent-Authored Pull Requests from a V-Model Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29143)
*Gabriel Dautovi,Jie JW Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出VeeReview，用V-Model的"构建-验证"配对来度量pull request中的验证证据，并在1454对agent/人类PR上做比较；但该工具未达到预设校准标准，作者据此论证这类过程度量需机械可判定成分与经校验的人工参照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评审PR需要针对变更的验证证据，而现有coding agent基准与PR结果研究并不直接评估"变更—验证证据"之间的关系；作者想检验V-Model的构建/验证配对能否测量PR中的验证证据，并支持agent撰写与人类撰写变更之间的比较。

Method: VeeReview用仓库信号加语言模型评判器对四组build-verify配对分类：三组由确定性信号约束的模型判断，Module Design-Unit Test一组由基于diff与仓库树的规则判定。用两位标注者标注的25个PR参考集评估工具，再应用于741个仓库中1454对匹配的agent与人类PR，采用混合效应模型并做链接issue、随附测试、规模的标准化。

Result: 四组配对中agent PR的"仅有构建"率均更低（混合效应OR 0.52–0.86），与预设假设相反；Architecture-Integration不显著。标准化后匹配率差异缩小34%–60%，规则判定配对的标准化区间含零。工具未达预设校准标准：与裁决标签一致率4%–60%（要求80%），评分者间一致性弱（kappa=0.154）。

Conclusion: 由规则判定的配对表明，语言模型判定的过程度量部分反映的是PR的呈现方式而非背后的验证工作；队列差异仅作为该工具下的观察报告，并主张此类度量需具备机械可判定成分与经校验的人工参照，才能用于验证实践的比较。

Abstract: Context: Reviewing a pull request requires verification evidence that addresses the proposed change. Coding agent benchmarks and studies of pull request outcomes do not directly assess this relationship. Objective: We test whether the V-Model's build and verify pairs can measure verification evidence in pull requests and support comparisons between agent-authored and human-authored changes. Method: VeeReview classifies four build and verify pairs using repository signals and a language-model judge. Three pairs use model judgements constrained by deterministic signals, and a rule over the diff and repository tree decides Module Design-Unit Test. We evaluated VeeReview against a 25-pull request reference set labelled by two raters and applied it to 1,454 matched pairs of agent and human pull requests from 741 repositories. Results: VeeReview assigned lower build-only rates to agent-authored pull requests on all four pairs, contrary to the preregistered hypothesis (mixed-effects odds ratios 0.52 to 0.86). Architecture-Integration was not significant. Standardization for linked issues, shipped tests and size reduced matched-rate differences by 34% to 60%, and the rule-decided pair's standardized interval included zero. The instrument did not meet its preregistered calibration criterion: agreement with adjudicated labels was 4% to 60% against a required 80%, and inter-rater agreement was weak (kappa = 0.154). Conclusions: The rule-decided pair shows that a process measure judged by a language model partly reflects how a pull request is presented rather than the verification work behind it. We report the cohort differences as observations made with this instrument, and we argue that such measures need a mechanically decided component and a validated human reference before they can support comparisons of verification practice.

</details>


### [13] [VERDICT: Multi-Agent Parallel Modular Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29147)
*Tengjie Lin,Zhongyi Wang,Haokun Li,Jianwei Yin,Mingshuai Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VERDICT是一个多智能体框架，通过"假设驱动的并行规约合成"和"双向跨过程冲突消解"实现大规模程序模块化演绎验证的并行化，在真实C程序上合成了更高质量的规约并大幅缩短验证时间，首次在数小时内完成2万行X.509解析器的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化演绎验证依赖形式化契约来独立验证各函数，其核心瓶颈是规约合成。现有LLM驱动的自动化规约合成方法本质上是自底向上的，要求被调用者先于调用者被验证，这限制了并行性，且缺乏在跨调用链传播的过程间不一致时进行消解的原则性机制。

Method: 提出VERDICT多智能体框架，基于两条原则：(1) 基于假设的并行规约合成——为每个函数分配一个智能体，允许其立即使用被调用函数的假设契约推进验证，从而在整个程序上并行开展；(2) 双向跨过程冲突消解——当合成契约与假设矛盾时，智能体沿调用图向上和向下传播必要修订，直至契约收敛。

Result: 在真实世界C程序上的实验表明，VERDICT合成的规约质量优于现有方法，且显著缩短验证时间。它是首个能扩展到完整的2万行X.509解析器的方法，可在数小时内排除几乎所有潜在运行时错误；而PREGUSS等先前工具即使运行数天也远未达到该效果。

Conclusion: VERDICT通过假设驱动的并行合成与双向冲突消解，突破了传统自底向上规约合成的依赖限制，实现了大规模程序模块化演绎验证的可扩展并行化，在真实C程序上兼具规约质量与验证效率优势。

Abstract: Deductive verification scales to large software systems through modular reasoning, where formal contracts summarize the behavior of individual functions and enable their independent verification. Its central bottleneck is specification synthesis: constructing the contracts required for modular verification. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled automated specification synthesis at unprecedented scale. However, existing approaches remain fundamentally bottom-up, requiring callees to be verified before their callers. This dependency limits parallelism and provides no principled mechanism for resolving interprocedural inconsistencies that propagate across call chains.
  We present VERDICT, a multi-agent framework for parallelizing modular deductive verification of large-scale programs. VERDICT is built on two key principles. First, assumption-based parallel specification synthesis assigns an agent to each function and allows it to proceed immediately using assumed contracts for its callees, enabling verification to proceed in parallel across the program. Second, bidirectional interprocedural conflict reconciliation resolves the inconsistencies introduced by these assumptions: when a synthesized contract contradicts an assumption, agents propagate the necessary revisions both up and down the call graph until the contracts converge. Experimental results on real-world C programs show that VERDICT synthesizes higher-quality specifications than existing approaches while substantially reducing verification time. In particular, VERDICT is the first approach that scales to the complete 20,000-line X.509 parser, ruling out nearly all the potential runtime errors within hours, whereas prior tools like PREGUSS fall far short even after days of execution.

</details>


### [14] [AI-Based End-to-End Automotive Design and Software Development for SDVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29160)
*Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由大语言模型（LLM）与模型驱动工程（MDE）融合驱动的AI工具链，将自然语言需求端到端自动转化为系统架构、可执行软件与验证制品，并面向安全关键的车载开发强调可追溯性、一致性与合规性；在支持自适应巡航与紧急制动的集中式车载计算平台上经HiL/SiL验证，并讨论了能力边界与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义汽车（SDV）要求车辆设计与软件开发实现端到端自动化，但传统以人工为主的需求—架构建模—代码—验证流程效率低、一致性维护成本高；同时安全关键开发对可追溯性与合规性的严格要求，使AI自动化应用需在可靠性前提下被验证。

Method: 基于CeCaS及后续欧盟资助项目成果，构建AI驱动工具链：以LLM解析自然语言需求，结合MDE实现系统架构建模、可执行代码生成与验证制品生成，并在全流程中嵌入可追溯性、一致性与安全关键合规机制；在支持自适应巡航控制与紧急制动的集中式车辆计算平台上，采用硬件在环（HiL）与软件在环（SiL）进行验证。

Result: 工具链在真实的集中式车载计算平台上完成了从需求到架构、软件及验证制品的端到端自动化流程，并在HiL与SiL环境中对自适应巡航与紧急制动功能完成验证；同时明确识别了当前工程化落地的实际局限。

Conclusion: LLM与MDE结合可实现安全关键车载软件的端到端自动化开发，前提是内建可追溯性与一致性保障；未来需朝着以最少人工干预实现高度自动化、可靠且质量可保障的汽车软件开发方向演进。

Abstract: This paper explores the potential of Artificial Intelligence (AI) to enable end-to-end automotive design and software development for Software-Defined Vehicles (SDVs). Based on our contributions within the Central Car Server (CeCaS) and follow-up EU-funded projects, we present an AI-driven toolchain integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) with Model-Driven Engineering (MDE) to automate the transformation of natural-language requirements into system architectures, executable software, and validation artifacts. The approach emphasizes traceability, consistency, and compliance with safety-critical automotive development requirements. Validation is performed on a centralized vehicle computing platform supporting adaptive cruise control and emergency braking, tested in Hardware-in-the-Loop (HiL) and Software-in-the-Loop (SiL) environments. We discuss current capabilities, practical limitations, and future directions toward increasingly automated, reliable, and quality-assured automotive software development with minimal human intervention.

</details>


### [15] [CloudIR-Bench: A Benchmark for Root Cause Analysis and Mitigation of Cloud Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29185)
*Hoang Tran,Huong Ha,Tri Dang,Jeffrey Chan,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CloudIR-Bench，这是首个公开可用的基准，用于基于事件报告的根本原因分析（RCA）与缓解措施预测，包含来自五个领先云系统的数据集、含八种 LLM 方法的评估框架，以及零样本/上下文学习实验和定性失败分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件量增长与系统复杂度上升使事件报告成为 RCA 和缓解预测的重要资源，LLM 已展现潜力；但现有工作依赖私有数据，缺乏透明性、可复现性与泛化性，缺少公开数据集阻碍进一步进展。

Method: 构建 CloudIR-Bench：从五类领先云系统的事件报告中整理数据集，覆盖多样化真实生产场景与工程师推理过程；同时开源评估框架，整合八种近期 LLM 方法，并开展零样本与上下文学习实验及定性失败分析。

Result: 综合性评测揭示了现有 LLM 在该领域的性能边界，定性分析定位了模型的失败场景及其原因。

Conclusion: CloudIR-Bench 填补了公开基准的空白，为事件报告驱动的 RCA 与缓解预测提供可复现、透明的评测平台，数据集、框架与补充材料已在 GitHub 公开。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) and mitigation prediction are essential for incident management, enabling the identification of underlying causes of failures and ensuring the reliability of complex cloud-based systems. With rising incident volume and system complexity, incident reports have become a valuable resource for these tasks. Recent studies have shown promising results using Large Language Models (LLMs) to predict root causes and mitigation actions from textual descriptions in incident reports. Despite these advances, further progress is hindered by the lack of publicly available datasets; existing work relies on private data, which limits transparency, reproducibility, and generalization. To address this gap, we introduce CloudIR-Bench, the first publicly available benchmark providing a dataset and evaluation framework for incident report-based RCA and mitigation prediction. Curated from incident reports across five leading cloud-based systems, CloudIR-Bench captures a diverse range of real-world production scenarios representing authentic failures and engineers' reasoning. We also release an open-source evaluation framework that includes eight recent LLM-based approaches. Our comprehensive evaluation includes zero-shot and in-context learning experiments, along with a qualitative analysis to identify where and why LLMs fail. Benchmarking results reveal the capabilities and limitations of existing LLMs in this domain. The benchmark, including the dataset, framework and supplementary materials, is available at https://github.com/hoangtnm/CloudIR-Bench.

</details>


### [16] [Do Coding Agents Understand Why Systems Crash---and Why They Don't?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29332)
*Chenxi Huang,Haonan Wang,Jinghe Zhang,Aleksandr Nogikh,Franjo Ivančić,Petros Maniatis,Eugene Wu,Kostis Kaffes,Junfeng Yang,Baishakhi Ray*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"源码干预"(source interventions)配对评估方法：用相隔单一提交、仅一个版本崩溃的源码树对，检验编码智能体能否真正推理系统代码的执行。在SysRB数据集（Linux与OSS-Fuzz共934对）上测试四个编码智能体，发现其倾向预测"不崩溃"、漏报严重，能定位关键代码却常误判其效果，且仅在崩溃代码上评估会产生误导。结论是当前编码智能体尚不能可靠推理大型系统代码库的执行，配对评估不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体已能修复真实缺陷，但在Linux内核等大型系统代码库上仍表现不佳。仅凭"是否预测正确崩溃"无法证明其具备执行推理能力，因为正确答案可能仅来自对可疑代码的表面识别。因此需要一个能排除这种捷径、真正测量执行推理能力的评估范式。

Method: 提出崩溃推理任务：仅从源码判断给定输入是否导致系统崩溃。核心方法是源码干预：构造仅相差一个提交的源码树对，使同一输入只令其中一棵树崩溃，再检验智能体是否在崩溃树上更常预测崩溃。构建SysRB数据集，含来自Linux与OSS-Fuzz项目的934对源码树，并在其上评测四个编码智能体。

Result: 三项主要发现：(1) 智能体存在"预测不崩溃"的偏向，误报少但漏报多，最佳智能体仍漏掉超过四分之一真实崩溃；(2) 仅定位决定崩溃结果的代码不足够，智能体常误判其作用，尤其当崩溃点远离该代码时；(3) 仅在崩溃代码上测试具有误导性，仅"察觉可疑代码"的智能体会在两棵树上都预测崩溃，若只看崩溃树则会被误判为推理正确。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体尚不能可靠推理复杂系统代码库的执行行为；配对式评估是测量该能力的必要条件，单一崩溃树上的评估会高估智能体能力。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly capable of fixing real-world bugs, yet they still struggle with large systems codebases such as the Linux kernel. To understand why, we test how agents reason about execution in these codebases through crash reasoning: predicting from source code whether a given input crashes the system. A correct prediction alone, however, does not establish this ability, since it may come from merely spotting suspicious code. In this paper, we propose source interventions: we pair source trees that differ by a single commit, so that the same input crashes only one of them, and test whether agents predict crashes more often on the crashing tree. Experiments with four coding agents on SysRB, our dataset of 934 such pairs from Linux and OSS-Fuzz projects, yield three main findings. First, agents are biased toward predicting no crash: they rarely raise false alarms but miss many real crashes, and even the best agent misses over a quarter of them. Second, locating the code that determines the crash outcome is not enough: agents often misjudge its effect, especially when the crash occurs far from that code. Third, testing only on crashing code is misleading: agents that merely notice suspicious code predict crashes on both source trees, which would look like correct reasoning on the crashing tree alone. Together, these findings indicate that current coding agents cannot yet reliably reason about execution in complex systems codebases, and that paired evaluation is necessary to measure this ability.

</details>


### [17] [RegoMender: A Multi-agent Framework for Automated Rego Policy Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29456)
*Ruijie Zhang,Huaxiao Yin,Li Hu,Fanqi Kong,Haozhe Liang,Shaoyin Cheng,Kun Zhang,Bibo Tu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出首个自动化 Rego 策略修复基准 RegoBench（1,159 个真实缺陷）及反馈引导的多智能体修复框架 RegoMender，在语法与语义修复成功率上分别达 82.9% 与 77.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Policy-as-Code（PaC）已成为云原生系统的基础范式，但 Rego 策略因其严格语法、声明式语义和特定验证需求而难以修复。现有自动程序修复（APR）方法主要面向主流语言，难以适配 Rego 特有的策略逻辑与修复流程，导致 Rego 策略修复仍依赖人工试错。

Method: 构建 RegoBench 基准，包含 1,159 个真实缺陷；提出 RegoMender——一种反馈引导的多智能体框架，通过共享黑板（blackboard）与原子工具集协同工作，动态执行补丁（patching）、重写（rewriting）或回滚（rolling back），以应对复杂修复场景。

Result: 在 RegoBench 上，RegoMender 的语法成功率与基于测试的语义成功率分别为 82.9% 和 77.9%，较最强基线提升 13.4%；在单步与链式修复任务中均保持稳定效果，并在 HCL、SQL 上表现出良好泛化性；在 132 个真实 CVE 漏洞上达到 86.4% 的修复率。

Conclusion: RegoMender 有效解决了 Rego 策略自动修复难题，克服了现有方法在复杂场景下性能下降的问题，并在跨语言与真实安全漏洞修复中展现出鲁棒性与实用价值。

Abstract: Policy-as-Code (PaC) has emerged as a foundational paradigm in cloud-native systems. However, repairing Rego policies remains challenging due to their strict syntax, declarative semantics, and specific verification requirements. Existing automated program repair (APR) methods are mainly designed for mainstream languages and struggle to accommodate the unique policy logic and repair workflows of Rego. Thus, Rego policy repair remains a manual process driven by trial and error. To address this, we introduce RegoBench, the first automated Rego policy benchmark comprising 1,159 real-world defects, and propose RegoMender, a feedback-guided multi-agent framework. Collaborating via a shared blackboard and an atomic toolkit, RegoMender dynamically executes patching, rewriting, or rolling back, to resolve complex repair scenarios. Evaluated on RegoBench, RegoMender achieves syntactic and test-based semantic success rates of 82.9\% and 77.9\%, respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 13.4\%. Unlike other approaches whose performance drops in complex scenarios, RegoMender maintains effectiveness across single-step and chained repair tasks. Furthermore, it generalizes strongly across HCL and SQL and achieves an 86.4\% repair rate on 132 real-world CVE vulnerabilities, proving its robustness and practical utility for security.

</details>


### [18] [On the Effectiveness of Snapshot Testing: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29549)
*Shun Fujita,Satoru Tomizawa,Yutaro Kashiwa,Bin Lin,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过340个GitHub仓库的大规模实证与60名从业者问卷，揭示快照测试与传统测试具有高度互补性，二者结合可显著提升覆盖率（中位语句覆盖率86.7%）与缺陷检测能力（变异体发现量1.3倍），同时总结了开发者视角的优势与痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前端UI测试成本高、难度大；快照测试作为低成本回归测试方法虽被广泛采用，但缺乏关于其实际有效性的实证证据。

Method: 大规模实证研究：分析340个GitHub仓库的快照测试使用情况；结合覆盖率与变异测试（mutation testing）量化评估；并对60名有经验的从业者开展问卷调查以获取定性洞察。

Result: 快照测试与传统非快照测试结合可显著提升代码覆盖率，中位语句覆盖率达86.7%；两类测试通常覆盖生产代码的不同区域，呈高度互补；含快照测试的测试套件发现的变异体数量为仅用传统测试的1.3倍。问卷显示开发者认可其易实施、防回归和结果透明，但大型结果集审查与假阳性管理仍是主要障碍。

Conclusion: 快照测试是传统测试的有效补充，应被策略性地整合进现代开发流水线；未来需重点改进结果审查效率与假阳性控制。

Abstract: Software testing is essential for ensuring quality, yet testing complex front-end user interfaces is often time-consuming and difficult. Snapshot testing has emerged as a low-cost regression testing method that detects unexpected changes by comparing program behavior before and after modifications. Despite its growing adoption, empirical evidence regarding its actual effectiveness remains scarce. To fill this gap, this study presents a large-scale empirical investigation into the effectiveness of snapshot testing involving 340 GitHub repositories and a survey of 60 experienced practitioners.
  Our quantitative results demonstrate that combining snapshot and traditional non-snapshot tests significantly enhances code coverage, achieving a median statement coverage of 86.7%. We find that the two approaches are highly complementary, typically exercising distinct regions of the production code. Furthermore, test suites incorporating snapshot tests detected 1.3 times more mutants than those relying solely on traditional tests. Our qualitative insights from the developer survey reveal that practitioners value snapshot testing for its ease of implementation, ability to prevent regressions, and transparency in checking results. However, reviewing large result sets and managing false positives remain significant hurdles. These findings guide the strategic integration and future improvement of snapshot testing in modern development pipelines.

</details>


### [19] [Who Verifies the Agent's Success Claim? Self-Report Leakage in GUI-Agent Completion Judging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29609)
*Yunfeng Zhu,Shengcheng Yu,Zihan Li,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究GUI智能体的"报告—轨迹审计"(report–trace auditing):智能体的自评成功声明会"泄漏"到完成判定中,导致危险的误报(dangerous misreport)。作者证明扣留自评可显著提升仅凭轨迹的召回,并提出将"声明解读"与"声明盲的轨迹分析"分离的结构化审计器,实现可复现、可换策略的审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化流水线中,GUI智能体"自信地报告成功"会促使下游阶段继续推进,即使其执行轨迹并未显示其因果性地达成了所要求的结果。这种"危险的误报"需要被审计,但审计本身可能受到智能体自评的干扰。

Method: 1) 受控的"知情/盲态"(aware/blind)干预实验:同一个判读者读同一任务与轨迹,仅控制是否提供自评,以量化自我报告泄漏。2) 实现结构化审计器:将声明解读与"声明盲"的轨迹分析分离,对抽取的证据应用确定性规则;因证据抽取与消费证据的策略解耦,同一审计记录可在不同策略下重放而无需新的模型调用。

Result: 扣留自评后,仅轨迹的危险误报召回在Qwen3.5-35B-A3B上提升43.4个百分点,在DeepSeek-284B-A13B上提升13.3个百分点,且留出批次Batch 2趋势一致。审计器在465条审计目标集上F1为0.75–0.78,留出Batch 2为0.77–0.81,在召回与F1上优于"声明知情"与"提示盲"基线。在审计阶段门控下(标记与复核均阻断自动通过),以45–56%的检查负载阻断74–90%的危险误报;经粗粒度状态分诊后,相当于全量在范围流中的16–19%。

Conclusion: 自评会污染完成判定,报告隔离是必要的:自评记录智能体的"声明",但不得影响轨迹状态判断。将声明解读与声明盲轨迹分析解耦、并用确定性规则消费抽取证据的结构化审计,能在可控检查负担下高效拦截危险误报,并支持审计记录的策略无关重放。

Abstract: In automation pipelines, a GUI agent's confident success report can let downstream stages proceed even when the recorded execution trace does not show that the agent produced the requested outcome. We call this case a dangerous misreport: an unhedged success claim that the trace does not support as causal accomplishment during the run. We study report--trace auditing, which checks an agent's completion claim against an independently recorded trace.
  A controlled aware/blind intervention reveals self-report leakage: the agent's own self-report changes how completion is judged. The same judge reads the same task and trace; only that self-report is included or withheld. Withholding it improves trace-only dangerous-misreport recall by 43.4 percentage points with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B and 13.3 percentage points with DeepSeek-284B-A13B, with the same trend on held-out Batch 2. This motivates report isolation. The report records what the agent claims, but it must not influence the trace-state judgment.
  We implement this separation with a structured auditor that separates claim interpretation from claim-blind trace analysis and applies deterministic rules to extracted evidence. Because evidence extraction is separated from the policy that consumes it, the same audit record can be replayed under alternative policies without new model calls.
  The auditor achieves F1 0.75--0.78 on the 465-record audit-target set and 0.77--0.81 on held-out Batch 2, outperforming claim-aware and prompt-blind controls in recall and F1. Under audit-stage gating, where flag and review both block automatic pass, it blocks 74--90% of dangerous misreports at 45--56% inspection load, or 16--19% of the full in-scope stream after coarse status triage.

</details>


### [20] [Reference Manual of MEDiml](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29666)
*Mahdi Ait Lhaj Loutfi,Simon Provost,Élodie Gillard,Moustafa Amine Bezzahi,Alexander Chum,Ben Coull-Nveu,Duran Juan,Mariem Kallel,Hakima Laribi,Oliver Lefebvre,Nicolas Longchamps,Thanh-Tri Nguyen,Steven Ravary,Ouael Nedjem Eddine Sahbi,Jérémy Dana,Martin Vallières*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出开源、标准兼容的影像组学平台 MEDiml，将特征提取、预测建模与解释通过图形界面与编程接口统一，降低临床研究者使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影像组学可提取数千个定量特征作为候选影像生物标志物，但特征筛选困难，且端到端流程需编程能力，限制了临床研究者的可及性。

Method: MEDiml = Python 包（MEDiml-pkg）+ 图形桌面应用（MEDiml-app），二者共享同一计算实现；支持从图形配置导出可执行 Python 代码以保证可复现性。典型流程：图像预处理→特征提取→特征集约简→模型训练与评估→基于特征重要性的解释；特征按复杂度分层（形态学、强度、纹理、滤波）。

Result: 在五个肿瘤影像队列（共 2,104 例患者）上演示了复杂度驱动的流程；文档中包含与现有工具的比较。

Conclusion: MEDiml 作为开源、标准兼容的参考平台，打通影像组学特征提取—建模—解释全流程，通过图形/编程双接口与代码生成促进临床与计算团队协作及结果可复现。

Abstract: Radiomics is the high-throughput extraction of quantitative features (e.g., tumor shape, intensity distributions, and texture) from medical images to characterize tissues and uncover disease patterns invisible to the human eye. These features can serve as candidate imaging biomarkers for cancer diagnosis, prognosis, and classification. However, extracting thousands of features makes it difficult to identify the most informative ones for a given clinical task, while implementing an end-to-end workflow can require programming expertise that limits accessibility for clinical researchers. Hence, we propose MEDiml, an open-source, standards-compliant radiomics platform connecting feature extraction, predictive modeling, and interpretation through complementary graphical and programmatic interfaces. MEDiml combines a Python package (MEDiml-pkg) with a graphical desktop application (MEDiml-app) that enables researchers to conduct experiments without writing code. Both interfaces use the same computational implementation, and code generation exports graphically configured workflows as executable Python code, facilitating reproducibility and collaboration between clinical researchers and computational scientists. A typical MEDiml workflow includes image preprocessing, radiomic feature extraction, feature set reduction, model training and evaluation, and interpretation using feature importance. Feature sets can be grouped into levels of radiomic complexity (morphological, intensity-based, textural, and filter-based) to evaluate whether more complex features provide additional predictive value. This reference manual documents the radiomics concepts, workflow, software components, architecture, and installation, compares MEDiml with existing tools, and demonstrates its complexity-driven workflow on five oncological imaging cohorts comprising 2,104 patients. Resources: www.mediml.app.

</details>


### [21] [Benchmarking Coding Agents for System-Level Performance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29695)
*Yanze Zhang,Maggie Yongqi Guan,Shudong Liu,Qi Hao,Kanye Ye Wang,Meng Qu,Xiaobo Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 MicroPerf-Bench，通过在真实开源微服务系统中注入性能反模式，评测编码智能体在分布式系统端到端性能优化上的能力；最佳配置仅恢复 42.0% 的性能退化，主要失败原因是无法定位系统级瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码基准多在函数或仓库层面评测，缺少对微服务架构下需要跨服务推理、超越局部代码修改的端到端性能优化能力的考察。而可复现且易于归因的系统级性能修复实例稀缺，使此类基准构建困难。

Method: 向真实开源系统注入有实证依据的性能反模式，同时保持功能正确并确保可度量的性能退化；构建含 65 个优化实例、覆盖 7 个系统与 9 类反模式的基准，每个实例提供可执行环境、功能测试与混合负载评估；对 9 种智能体-模型配置进行 1,755 次运行评测。

Result: 最佳配置仅恢复 42.0% 的注入性能退化。主要失败模式并非代码无效，而是功能正确且满足约束、但未能改善端到端性能的修改，说明系统级瓶颈定位是当前编码智能体的关键短板。

Conclusion: MicroPerf-Bench 填补了分布式系统级性能优化评测的空白；结果表明智能体在系统级瓶颈定位与端到端性能优化方面仍有显著提升空间，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Coding agents are becoming an increasingly important paradigm for automating software engineering tasks. However, existing coding benchmarks primarily evaluate agents at the function or repository level, leaving their capabilities in more complex distributed software systems largely unexplored. In practice, many modern applications adopt microservice architectures, where end-to-end performance emerges from interactions among multiple independently running services, requiring agents to reason beyond local code changes. To study this capability, we introduce MicroPerf-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on end-to-end performance optimization in microservice systems. Constructing such a benchmark is challenging because reproducible system-level performance fixes are sparse and difficult to attribute. We address this challenge by injecting empirically grounded performance anti-patterns into real-world open-source systems while preserving functionality and ensuring measurable performance degradation. The resulting benchmark contains 65 optimization instances across seven systems and nine anti-patterns, each with an executable environment, functional tests, and mixed-workload evaluation. Across 1,755 runs covering nine agent-model configurations, the best configuration recovers only 42.0% of the injected performance degradation. The dominant failure is not invalid code, but functionally correct and constraint-compliant changes that fail to improve end-to-end performance, highlighting system-level bottleneck localization as a key challenge for current coding agents. The source code and data are available at https://github.com/zyzcjnb/microperf.

</details>


### [22] [SAKURA: Serving Attention Key-Value State Under Rate Adaptation for Private Image Editing on Phones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29733)
*Shuncheng Jie,Desen Sun,Ri Hong,Yuyang Tian,Hong Zhang,Sihang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SAKURA 是一种云-端协同架构，在带宽预算下将可复用的注意力 KV 状态传送到移动设备，通过 token 重要性感知压缩、按带宽自适应选择缓存表示以及流水线运行时，实现低延迟的扩散 Transformer 图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 图像编辑服务为兼顾效率与隐私，采用云-端协同、把请求相关推理留在设备端。此类负载常使用通用模板（换脸、虚拟试穿等），可预计算并复用 KV 状态；但该状态体积大且随模板数线性增长，无法全部存于移动端；若存放在云端，则缓存传输落在推理路径上，在波动移动网络下甚至慢于端上计算。

Method: 提出 SAKURA 云-边架构：1) 细粒度、token 重要性感知的压缩方法，在有限传输预算下保持图像质量；2) 运行时根据网络带宽为每个即将到来的分片（shard）自适应选择缓存表示；3) 流水线运行时，将缓存传输与重建同端上执行重叠。

Result: 在 Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 手机上，基于 3 个编辑任务与 2 个扩散模型评估：相比适配到 DiT 的 CacheGen 和端上生成，延迟分别降低 1.82x 和 3.27x；相比未压缩缓存，缓存存储降低 3.06x，同时保持图像质量。

Conclusion: 在带宽受限的移动环境中，SAKURA 通过重要性感知压缩与带宽自适应的流水线传输，使云端可复用 KV 状态的高效复用成为可行，兼顾隐私、存储与延迟。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers are widely used in image-editing services. To preserve both efficiency and privacy, existing systems commonly rely on cloud-device collaboration. Keeping request-specific inference on the device avoids sending personal images and prompts to the cloud. These workloads often use common templates, such as in face swapping and virtual try-on, enabling KV state to be precomputed and reused across requests. However, this reusable state is large and scales with the number of templates, making it impractical to store all of them on mobile devices. Storing them in the cloud addresses this capacity issue, but cache transfer is placed onto the inference path and can even be slower than on-device computation, especially under fluctuating mobile networks.
  We introduce SAKURA, a cloud-edge architecture that delivers reusable attention state to mobile devices under a bandwidth budget. It first uses a fine-grained token-importance-aware compression method to preserve image quality under limited transfer budgets. At runtime, SAKURA adaptively selects the cache representation for each upcoming shard based on network bandwidth. To enable efficient execution, we develop a pipelined runtime that overlaps cache transfer and reconstruction with on-device execution. We evaluate SAKURA using three editing tasks and two diffusion models, using a Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 phone. SAKURA reduces latency by 1.82x and 3.27x over CacheGen adapted to DiT and on-device generation, respectively. It also reduces cache storage by 3.06x over uncompressed cache, while preserving image quality.

</details>


### [23] [Engineering Requirements for Generative AI Agents: The BARD Process Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29741)
*Alfonso Cannavale,Antonio Della Porta,Gianmario Voria,Gilberto Recupito,Maria Teresa Baldassarre,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba,Andrea De Lucia,Stefano Lambiase*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于36次半结构化访谈，运用社会技术扎根理论，提出面向生成式AI Agent的需求工程过程理论BARD：需求并非预先固定，而是通过"界定需求—构建Agent—观察行为—修订诉求"四阶段循环被逐步发现，需求由固定规格演进为行为与评估准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕GenAI Agent构建的系统，其需求工程实践在实证层面尚属空白。现有RE研究主要针对广义的AI/ML系统，未提供面向agentic系统的过程性解释，而这类系统的非确定性行为无法在设计阶段预先确定。

Method: 采用社会技术扎根理论（Socio-Technical Grounded Theory），对从业者进行36次半结构化访谈，数据收集与分析交替进行，以涌现发现驱动理论抽样并开展多轮编码。

Result: 得到BARD（Behavioral Agent Requirement Discovery）过程理论：包含四个循环活动——界定需求、构建Agent、观察行为、修订诉求；需求由固定规格演化为行为性与评估性准则；四个张力驱动阶段间转换，两个条件构成循环的框架。

Conclusion: BARD为研究者提供迭代优化RE工作流的理论框架，为从业者提供可嵌入开发流程的结构化基础。

Abstract: How teams engineer requirements for systems built around Generative AI (GenAI) Agents remains empirically uncharted. Existing Requirements Engineering (RE) research mainly addresses AI and ML-based systems broadly but provides no process account for agentic systems, whose non-deterministic behavior cannot be anticipated at design time. To understand how teams actually proceed, we applied Socio-Technical Grounded Theory across 36 semi-structured interviews with practitioners, interleaving data collection and analysis so that emerging findings drove our theoretical sampling and shaped successive rounds of coding. The result is Behavioral Agent Requirement Discovery (BARD), the process theory of RE for GenAI Agents. BARD holds that requirements are not fixed up front but discovered through a recurring cycle of four activities: teams scope the need, build the agent, observe its behavior, and revise what they ask for. Through this four-phase loop, the requirement evolves from a fixed specification towards behavioral and evaluation criteria. Four tensions drive transitions between phases, and two conditions frame the loop. BARD provides researchers with a framework for iteratively refining RE workflows, while giving practitioners a structured basis to embed into their development processes.

</details>


### [24] [Metamodel-Guided LLM Decoding for Consistent DSL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29749)
*Boqi Chen,Aren A. Babikian,José Antonio Hernández López*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MeMoDecode 是一种解码框架，在 LLM 生成 DSL 实例的过程中联合强制约束具体语法、抽象语法与元模型级别的良好形成性（well-formedness）约束，从而提升生成一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多用于生成领域特定语言（DSL）工件，而正确的 DSL 实例不仅要符合具体语法，还须符合由元模型定义的抽象语法及语义良好形成性约束。现有受约束解码方法通常只保证具体语法或文本层面有限的语义约束，难以在生成过程中保证元模型级别的约束一致性。

Method: 提出 MeMoDecode：每当 LLM 生成一个完整的元模型概念，就将当前输出增量映射为部分模型，并验证其是否仍能补全为一致的实例；若检测到不一致，则结合简单的重试机制，从最近的可补全快照恢复解码。理论上，在后端验证器可靠（sound）的前提下，MeMoDecode 在满足自然假设的对齐指标上不劣于语法约束解码。

Result: 在四种 DSL 和六个 0.6B–70B 参数的开源权重 LLM 上，MeMoDecode 取得最高一致性：相比无约束解码平均提升 23%，相比语法约束解码平均提升 8%；同时提升基准性能，仅带来适度运行时开销。

Conclusion: 元模型引导的解码是在解码阶段强制 DSL 一致性的有效方法，可适用于多种 DSL 与不同规模的 LLM。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software engineering artifacts written in domain-specific languages (DSLs). Correct DSL instances usually must comply with not only the concrete syntax but also the abstract syntax defined by a metamodel augmented with semantic well-formedness constraints. Existing constrained decoding approaches typically guarantee compliance only with concrete syntax or with a limited set of semantic constraints expressed over the generated text, making metamodel-level constraint consistency difficult to enforce during generation. We present MeMoDecode: a decoding framework that enforces a DSL's concrete syntax, abstract syntax, and well-formedness constraints jointly. Specifically, whenever the LLM generates a complete metamodel concept, MeMoDecode incrementally maps the current output to a partial model and verifies whether it can still be completed into a consistent instance. We combine MeMoDecode with a simple retry mechanism that resumes decoding from the most recent completable snapshot whenever an inconsistency is detected. Under a sound backend verifier, MeMoDecode never performs worse than grammar-constrained decoding on alignment metrics satisfying natural assumptions. Across four DSLs and six open-weight LLMs ranging from 0.6B to 70B parameters, MeMoDecode achieves the highest consistency, improving consistency by 23% on average over unconstrained decoding and by 8% on average over grammar-constrained decoding. It also improves benchmark performance while incurring only modest runtime overhead. These results show that metamodel-guided decoding is an effective approach for enforcing DSL consistency during decoding across diverse DSLs and LLM scales.

</details>


### [25] [VeruSteer: Learning What to Repair Next in Verus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29755)
*Chao Jiang,Ding Wang,Chaohua Yang,Dugang Liu,Zhiwu Xu,Cheng Wen,Zhong Ming,Yuwei Liu,Xinyi Wan,Lin Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 VeruSteer，用离线构建的修复树学习错误类型选择策略，在 Rust/Verus 证明修复流水线中动态替代预设的错误修复优先级，以提升验证成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AutoVerus、VeruSAGE 等基于 LLM 的 Verus 证明修复系统在同时报告多种错误类型时，依赖固定的优先级选择下一个修复目标。但每次修复都会改变程序及后续错误，导致同一类错误在不同状态下需要不同的修复顺序，固定优先级并非最优。

Method: 离线构建修复树，从同一初始程序出发比较不同修复路径的验证成功、修复轮数与最终验证进度，据此监督一个选择器；该选择器结合逐步模仿学习与轨迹偏好排序，利用对程序、验证器反馈及历史选择的自注意力机制对当前报告的错误类型排序。选择器仅替换单条修复轨迹内的默认优先级，保留原有修复智能体，且选择过程不额外调用修复或验证器。

Result: 在 AutoVerus 与 VeruSAGE 存储的测试树、四种生成配置下重放其选择：1,270 棵测试树中，验证成功数从 185 提升至 194（14.57%→15.28%），净增 12、损失 3，提升 0.71 个百分点；默认失败但存在可验证备选路径的树中，AutoVerus 成功 3/4、VeruSAGE 成功 9/11；默认成功且存在备选路径的树中，分别保持 14/15 与 12/14 的成功；同时找到更短的失败轨迹，并在全部记录轨迹均失败的树上提升部分树的最终验证进度。

Conclusion: VeruSteer 表明，将错误类型选择建模为基于修复轨迹结果的学习问题，可在不改变现有修复智能体、不增加验证开销的前提下，稳定且低风险地改进 LLM 证明修复流水线的验证成功率与修复效率。

Abstract: LLM-based verification systems repair Rust proofs by revising programs in response to Verus diagnostics. When several error types are reported, pipelines such as AutoVerus and VeruSAGE select the next repair target using a preset priority. Yet each repair changes the program and the errors that follow, so the same reported types can require different choices at different states. We present VeruSteer, which learns to select error types from the outcomes of alternative repair trajectories. It constructs repair trees offline and compares paths from the same initial program by verification success, repair rounds, and final verification progress. These comparisons supervise a selector that combines step-wise imitation with trajectory preference ranking. The selector uses self-attention over the program, verifier feedback, and earlier selections to rank the currently reported types. It replaces the default priority within a single repair trajectory, keeping the existing repair agents and adding no repair or verifier calls for selection. We evaluate VeruSteer by replaying its choices on stored AutoVerus and VeruSAGE test trees under four generation configurations. Across 1,270 test trees, VeruSteer increases verified outcomes from 185 to 194 (14.57\% to 15.28\%), gaining 12 successes and losing three for a net improvement of 0.71 percentage points. Among trees where the default fails but a recorded alternative verifies, VeruSteer succeeds on 3 of 4 AutoVerus trees and 9 of 11 VeruSAGE trees. Among trees with recorded alternatives where the default verifies, it retains success on 14 of 15 AutoVerus trees and 12 of 14 VeruSAGE trees. It also finds shorter successful trajectories and improves final progress on some trees where all recorded trajectories fail.

</details>


### [26] [AssureCode: Structured Justification for Trustworthy Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29758)
*Logan Murphy,Wilson Sy,Rhys Balevicius,Kelsie Fung,Karenna Kung,Aren A. Babikian,Marsha Chechik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出将结构化论证（structured argumentation）引入AI辅助软件开发，定义"理由论证语言"，将自然语言提示、形式化规范、开发者意图假设与程序分析证据显式关联，并实现工具支持的AssureCode方法用于agentic代码生成，进而实证评估其对AI生成代码正确性保证的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具使大量代码以有限人工投入被生成，但核心难题在于确保生成产物与开发者意图一致且已被恰当验证，即正确性与意图对齐的可信保证缺失。

Method: 借鉴安全关键系统的保证工程（assurance engineering）技术，设计一种justification rationales（论证理由）语言，以结构化、可验证的格式显式关联非形式自然语言提示、形式化规范、关于开发者意图的假设以及程序分析证据；在此语言基础上构建AssureCode——一种工具支持的方法论，将此类论证理由嵌入agentic代码生成流程；并对其实现进行实证分析。

Result: 论文对实现进行了实证分析，考察其在为AI生成代码提供有依据的正确性保证（justified assurance of correctness）方面的能力（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 结构化论证可作为驱动AI辅助软件开发的有效机制，把提示、规范、意图假设与分析证据纳入可验证的论证结构，从而为AI生成代码的正确性保证提供支撑。

Abstract: The use of AI-based tools in software development is rapidly changing how software is produced, enabling large volumes of code to be generated with limited human implementation effort. However, this raises fundamental challenges for ensuring that generated artifacts are aligned with developer intent and have been appropriately validated. In this work, we propose the use of structured argumentation in driving AI-assisted software development. Inspired by techniques from assurance engineering for safety-critical systems, we define a language of justification rationales that explicitly relate informal natural language prompts, formal specifications, assumptions about developer intentions, and program analysis evidence in a structured and verifiable format. We then describe AssureCode, a tool-supported methodology for creating such rationales as part of agentic code generation. We provide an empirical analysis of our implementation where we investigate its capacity to contribute towards justified assurance of correctness for AI-generated code.

</details>


### [27] [Controlling Variability in~Neuroimaging Analysis Pipelines with~Software Product Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29821)
*Gauthier Le Bartz Lyan,Paul Temple,Boris Clenet,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文主张将软件产品线（SPL）原理引入神经影像学研究：NARPS研究显示不同团队针对同一问题构建的MRI/fMRI分析流水线存在显著结果变异性，而多条实现同一任务的流水线恰是SPL的典型场景；用SPL对既有流水线建模本质上是一个逆向工程问题，作者倡仪以概念性采纳SPL原则作为提升可重复性与一致性的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI数据复杂且噪声大，需通过流水线提取与增强信息；NARPS研究表明独立团队回答同一脑功能问题时因流水线不同而产生显著结果差异，即可重复性与一致性不足，需要新的组织与建模思路。

Method: 概念性/倡仪性论述（position paper）：论证多条实现同一任务的流水线属于软件产品线（SPL）的典型问题空间，并将从既有异构流水线中抽象出SPL视为逆向工程问题，剖析其常见困难。

Result: 本文为概念性论证，未报告实证结果；指出SPL原则与神经影像流水线现存实践之间存在缺口（可能超出神经影像从业者视野），并说明以SPL重构多流水线的逆向工程难点。

Conclusion: 采纳SPL原则是改善神经影像研究可重复性与一致性的有前景方向，值得在概念层面引入并进一步开展实证与工程化研究。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is an acquisition technique used for medical diagnosis. It is notably used for structural and functional MRI (or fMRI) image analysis. MRI data is known to be complex and noisy, so pipelines are created to extract and enhance relevant information in retrieved images. The NARPS study highlighted the significant variability that can emerge from different pipelines set up by independent teams while trying to answer the same research question about brain functioning. Having different implementations realizing the same task is a typical playground for software product lines (SPLs), which may be out of the sight of neuroimage practitioners. Thus, proposing an SPL out of these different pipelines falls back on a typical reverse-engineering problem with its usual difficulties. In this document, we advocate for the conceptual adoption of SPL principles as a promising direction to improve reproducibility and consistency in neuroimaging research.

</details>


### [28] [PAUSE: Search-Free Steering of Coding Agents Before Their First Source-Code Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29870)
*Viral Patel,Boyin Tan,Jason Xue,Youcheng Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PAUSE 是一种在编码智能体首次修改源代码前插入一次规划干预的框架，无需学习型监控器、检查点搜索或额外模型调用，在线与离线均可用，在 SWE-bench 上显著提升解决率并降低规划成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在仓库探索与测试上耗费大量精力却仍可能产出失败补丁。现有在线引导方法依赖周期性审查轨迹或由监控器检测异常行为后再调用顾问，频繁审查代价高昂，且"无异常行为"并不等于"方向正确"，缺乏有效的廉价干预时机与信号。

Method: 观察到智能体到达首次源代码编辑时，轨迹进度中位数已达 60%（即已完成探索、bug 复现与测试）。PAUSE 在该编辑请求执行前进行拦截：由独立的 Planner 读取任务描述与编辑前转录，撰写一份修复计划；同一智能体作为 Executor 携带该计划继续运行，保留原有上下文与工作区。方法不需学习型监控器、不需检查点搜索（search-free）、不需额外模型调用，可在线实时应用，也可从录制的轨迹前缀离线应用。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 与 SWE-bench Multilingual 上，使用两个 Executor 与三种 agent harness 评测。以 Qwen3-coder 为 Executor、GPT-5.6 Sol 为 Planner：在线引导在 OpenHands 上对 Verified 提升 16.8%，在 SWE-agent 上对 Multilingual 提升 14.7%。在 SWE-agent 中，相近 Planner 成本下比规则触发式顾问 LivePlan 高 3.2%（Verified）与 3.7%（Multilingual）；与 SICA 的周期性监督者效果相当，但 Planner 成本仅为其约 11–15%。离线设置下可恢复 35.4% 符合条件的失败 Verified 任务，从录制前缀续跑比重新启动节省 33–41% 的 Executor tokens。

Conclusion: 首次源码编辑是编码智能体的关键决策点，在此处进行单次规划干预即可高效纠偏；PAUSE 以低成本实现显著提升，兼具在线与离线适用性，并可复用已有轨迹前缀以节省计算。

Abstract: Coding agents can invest substantial effort in repository exploration and testing yet still produce unsuccessful patches. Existing online steering methods review trajectories periodically or invoke an advisor when a monitor detects behavioral problems; frequent reviews are costly, and the absence of anomalous behavior does not mean that an agent is on the right track. We propose PAUSE, a framework for steering coding agents with a single planning intervention. Agents reach their first source-code edit at a median of 60% of trajectory progress, after exploration, bug reproduction, and testing. PAUSE intercepts this edit request before it executes, which requires no learned monitor, no search over checkpoints, and no extra model call (search-free). A separate Planner reads the task and the pre-edit transcript and writes one repair plan; the same agent, the Executor, resumes with this plan, keeping its context and workspace. PAUSE applies online, during a live run, and offline, from a recorded trajectory prefix. We evaluate PAUSE on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Multilingual with two Executors and three agent harnesses. With Qwen3-coder as Executor and GPT-5.6 Sol as Planner, online steering improves resolution by 16.8% on Verified in OpenHands and by 14.7% on Multilingual in SWE-agent. In SWE-agent, PAUSE outperforms LivePlan, a rule-triggered advisor, by 3.2% on Verified and 3.7% on Multilingual at similar Planner cost, and is comparable to SICA's periodic overseer at about 11-15% of its Planner cost. Offline, PAUSE recovers 35.4% of eligible failed Verified tasks, and resuming from the recorded prefix uses 33-41% fewer Executor tokens than restarting.

</details>


### [29] [DynaCI: Generating GitHub Actions Workflows via Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29890)
*Javid Ditty,Rahul Ghanshyam Chavan,Nimmi Weeraddana,Foyzul Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DynaCI，利用开发者本地运行环境的运行时信息来生成CI工作流，结合启发式规则与基于RAG的LLM迭代精化，在50个GitHub Actions Python项目上显著优于GH-WCOM与LLM4GW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI工作流配置困难：现代软件架构复杂、依赖众多、运行环境多样，且各CI平台使用专有DSL，导致工作流易出错、难维护。已有自动生成方法依赖开发者以自然语言或部分工作流形式描述需求，但开发者往往难以准确表述需求，也不了解可用的CI特性。

Method: DynaCI分三步：(i) 追踪开发者本地环境，采集运行时信息；(ii) 基于运行时信息驱动的启发式规则构造初始工作流；(iii) 通过基于LLM的检索增强生成（RAG）迭代精化工作流。

Result: 在50个使用GitHub Actions的流行Python项目上评估，相比SOTA方法GH-WCOM与LLM4GW，BLEU平均提升21.13%以上，F1平均提升20.96%；56%的案例生成的工作流无功能缺失，且修复异常所需的人工修改更少。

Conclusion: 利用运行时信息可显著提升基于LLM的CI工作流生成质量，为更广泛的DevOps配置自动化提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) is an essential practice that automatically validates code changes through builds and tests. However, configuring CI workflows remains challenging, as modern software often features complex architectures, numerous dependencies, and support for diverse execution environments. Most CI platforms also require workflows to be written in their particular domain-specific languages. As a result, CI workflows are often error-prone and hard to maintain, especially for those unfamiliar with CI semantics. Prior work on automated CI workflow generation require developers to create specifications that describe their intended workflows using natural language, partial workflows, etc. However, in practice, developers may struggle to articulate requirements and may be unaware of available CI features. To address these limitations, we propose DynaCI, a novel approach that uses runtime information from developers' local environments to generate CI workflows. DynaCI (i) traces the developers' local environment to collect runtime information, (ii) constructs an initial workflow by applying runtime-informed heuristics, and (iii) iteratively refines the workflow via LLM-based Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG). We evaluate DynaCI on 50 popular Python projects that use GitHub Actions, a popular CI platform, and compare it against the state-of-the-art CI workflow generation approaches: GH-WCOM and LLM4GW. DynaCI outperforms these existing approaches by more than 21.13% in BLEU score and 20.96% in F1 score on average. It also generates workflows without missing functionality in 56% of cases, while requiring fewer manual modifications to repair such exceptions. These results demonstrate that leveraging runtime information substantially improves LLM-based CI workflow generation, and offer a promising direction for broader DevOps configuration automation.

</details>


### [30] [FaultGene: LLM-Assisted Fault-Augmented Test Generation for Hardware-in-the-Loop Validation of Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29969)
*Chaoran Yuan,Fadi Kurdahi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 FaultGene，一个 LLM 辅助的自动化框架，用于为 HIL 故障韧性验证生成可执行的故障增强测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶信息物理系统的可靠验证需同时覆盖正常驾驶行为与传感、嵌入式计算故障引发的失效。实车测试成本高、难复现；纯仿真无法刻画硬件相关行为与故障传播；HIL 可弥合二者，但构造故障增强的 HIL 测试场景仍主要依赖人工，制约测试覆盖率、可扩展性与效率。

Method: 输入为基线 Scenic/CARLA 场景与结构化故障规格，FaultGene 生成能体现 HIL 级故障注入效应的故障增强测试用例。离线精化阶段采用语义一致性检查与执行级反馈，修正无法保持场景上下文或未产生预期故障退化的候选用例，并据此构建高质量微调监督数据；精调后的生成器在部署阶段直接产出故障增强测试，无需人工构造参考执行或反复精化循环。

Result: 通过离线精化得到的生成器可在部署时一次性直接生成故障增强测试用例，免除人工参考执行与重复精化循环，从而提升测试生成的可扩展性与效率。

Conclusion: FaultGene 将 LLM 生成与语义/执行级反馈验证结合，实现 HIL 故障韧性测试的自动化生成，缓解人工场景构造瓶颈。

Abstract: Reliable validation of autonomous driving cyber-physical systems requires testing not only nominal driving behavior but also failures arising from faults in sensing and embedded computation. Physical road testing provides realistic system behavior but is costly and difficult to reproduce, while simulation enables scalable evaluation but cannot fully capture hardware-dependent behavior and fault propagation. Hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) validation bridges this gap by executing the driving stack on real embedded hardware within a simulated environment. However, preparing fault-augmented scenarios for HIL testing remains largely a manual process, limiting test coverage, scalability, and efficiency.
  We present FaultGene, an LLM-assisted framework for automated generation of executable fault-augmented tests for HIL fault-resilience validation. Given a baseline Scenic/CARLA scenario and a structured fault specification, FaultGene generates a fault-augmented test case that captures the effects of HIL-level fault injection. During offline refinement, semantic-consistency checks and execution-level feedback are used to correct candidates that fail to preserve the intended scenario context or produce the desired fault-induced degradation, and to construct high-quality supervision for fine-tuning. The resulting generator then produces fault-augmented tests directly at deployment time without requiring manually engineered reference executions or repeated refinement loops.

</details>


### [31] [HiddenLens: A Multi-Layer LLM Framework for Detecting and Resolving Hidden Assumptions in Software Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29996)
*Tanu Singh,Aashna Suman,Vinod Patidar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出HiddenLens——一个六层BERT-LLM流水线，用于检测、分类、质疑并解决软件需求中的隐藏假设，并构建首个专用标注数据集HLC，端到端F1达0.79。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求常隐含关于性能、运行条件、用户行为与领域约束的假设，若直到实现阶段才暴露代价高昂；现有研究多关注歧义与模糊性，而对"看似清晰合理却内含假设"的需求关注不足。

Method: 六层流水线：L1用BERT注意力熵定位未解析片段；L2用CoT提示的LLM推理阐述假设及其失效风险；L3用微调BERT将假设归入性能、安全、可扩展性、可用性、可靠性、领域六类NFR；L4用few-shot LLM提示生成排序的澄清选项；L5人工验证（确认/编辑/改写）；L6依据确认结果重写需求。同时构建HiddenLens Corpus（HLC），含312条需求、847条隐藏假设标注。

Result: 端到端F1为0.79，分别比关键词基线和QuARS-Adapted基线高出71%和61%；基于BERT的NFR分类器F1为0.847。

Conclusion: HiddenLens可在假设传播至软件开发各环节之前帮助分析师识别与解决隐藏假设，输出显式、可测试、可追溯的需求；HLC填补了该任务的标注数据空白。

Abstract: Natural-language software requirements often omit assumptions about performance, operating conditions, user behaviour, and domain constraints, affecting architecture, testing, and deployment. If undiscovered until implementation, resolving them is far more costly than addressing them early. Although ambiguity and vagueness are widely studied, less attention has been given to assumptions embedded in requirements that appear clear and reasonable. We propose HiddenLens, a novel six-layer BERT-LLM pipeline and, to our knowledge, the first framework engineered to detect, classify, question, and resolve hidden assumptions rather than merely flag ambiguity. Layer 1 uses BERT attention entropy to localise unresolved spans; Layer 2 uses CoT-prompted LLM reasoning to articulate each assumption and its failure risk; Layer 3 uses fine-tuned BERT to classify assumptions into six non-functional requirement categories: performance, security, scalability, usability, reliability, and domain; Layer 4 generates ranked clarification options using FS LLM prompting; Layer 5 enables human validation through confirmation, editing, or rephrasing; and Layer 6 rewrites the requirement using the confirmed resolution, producing an explicit, testable, and traceable requirement. We also introduce the HiddenLens Corpus (HLC), comprising 312 requirements annotated with 847 hidden assumptions across six NFR categories. Inspired by PROMISE and PURE, HLC addresses the lack of annotated data for this task and is, to our knowledge, the first dataset developed specifically for it. HiddenLens outperforms keyword-based and QuARS-Adapted baselines by 71% and 61%, respectively, achieving an end-to-end F1-score of 0.79; its BERT-based NFR classifier achieves an F1-score of 0.847. These results indicate that HiddenLens can help analysts resolve hidden assumptions before they propagate through software development.

</details>


### [32] [Code Comprehensor: Hierarchical Documentation and Cross-Modal Retrieval for Large Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30016)
*Sujoy Roychowdhury,Joseph H. R. Isaac,Jiangning Gao,Martin Klitte,Diarmuid Corcoran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Code Comprehensor 混合检索-推理框架，结合代码调用图、LLM 生成的分层文档（CodeScribe）与 CLIP 式双编码器（CodeContrast）实现代码上下文检索，支撑 MCP 编排的 LLM 问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模复杂代码库难以理解，阻碍新人上手、维护、重构与影响分析；且将仓库级上下文有效提供给 LLM 本身也是难题，LLM 需同时理解目标方法及其在仓库中的关联方法。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：(i) 代码依赖调用图；(ii) CodeScribe，用代码感知 LLM 沿仓库调用图生成分层文档；(iii) CodeContrast，基于 CodeScribe 输出训练的 CLIP 式双编码器，学习对齐的文本-代码嵌入，实现快速语义精准的代码上下文邻域检索，作为 MCP 编排 LLM 回答领域问题的有据证据。

Result: 在 OpenCV 与某 5M-LOC 专有仓库上，CodeScribe 衍生的监督信号显著提升文本到代码的跨模态检索准确率；更丰富的文本监督（分层文档、父/兄弟上下文、LLM 合成问题）在两仓库上均稳定带来增益；RAGAS 的 LLM-as-a-judge 评估显示 Code Comprehensor 输出忠实度（faithfulness）超过 90%。

Conclusion: 将调用图结构、LLM 生成分层文档与对比学习嵌入相结合，可为仓库级代码理解提供高效、可扩展且高忠实度的检索-推理方案。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex, and rapidly evolving code bases remains a core challenge for software engineering at scale, hindering onboarding, maintenance, refactoring, and impact analysis. Providing this information to a Large-Language Model (LLM) is another hurdle, as the LLM must understand not only the required code method but also its associated methods in the grand scheme of the repository. We propose Code Comprehensor, a hybrid retrieval-reasoning framework combining (i) a code dependency callgraph, (ii) CodeScribe, which uses code-aware LLMs to generate hierarchical documentation across repository call graphs, and (iii) CodeContrast, a CLIP-style dual-encoder model trained on CodeScribe output to learn aligned text-code embeddings. CodeContrast enables fast, semantically precise retrieval of relevant code-context neighborhoods, which serve as grounded evidence for an MCP-based LLM orchestrator answering domain-specific queries. Evaluation on OpenCV and a proprietary 5M-LOC repository shows that CodeScribe-derived supervision significantly improves cross-modal retrieval in text-to-code accuracy over baseline approaches. Richer textual supervision (hierarchical documentation, parent/sibling context, and LLM-generated synthetic questions) consistently enhances performance across both repositories. LLM-as-a-judge evaluation using the popular RAGAS of the Code Comprehensor output yields above 90% faithfulness scores.

</details>


### [33] [SWE-DIVE: Multi-Turn Coding Agent Evaluation with Dynamic User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30047)
*Lorenz Brehme,Benjamin Steenhoek,Ruth Breu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-DIVE，一个面向多轮交互场景的编码智能体动态评测框架，通过用户模拟器与对话校验器在 Harbor 环境中评测编码智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评测（如 SWE-bench）依赖单轮、形式良好的 GitHub issue 提示，而真实使用中提示往往不规范且需多轮迭代追加需求，二者存在显著差距。

Method: 构建 SWE-DIVE 动态交互式评测框架：1）用户模拟器模拟不同行为模式并与编码智能体通信；2）对话校验器评估用户模拟器输出是否满足初始需求并引导智能体完成任务；3）整体嵌入 Harbor 环境，编排智能体、模拟器与校验器，并执行测试环境判定任务成败。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果，仅说明结果与源码已公开于 GitHub 仓库。

Conclusion: 该工作将编码智能体评测从单轮静态提示扩展到多轮动态交互，为更贴近真实使用场景的评测提供了可复用框架。

Abstract: Coding agents are typically evaluated using one-shot, well-formulated prompts. Benchmarks such as SWE-bench use existing GitHub issues as input for a coding agent and evaluate whether the agent successfully solves the task.
