However, in real-world use cases, prompts are often not as well-formulated or structured as existing GitHub issues. Additionally, a coding agent session is not limited to a single prompt; it typically involves a multi-turn interaction in which the user iteratively adds new tasks and requirements that the agent must fulfill.
  To benchmark these abilities, we developed SWE-DIVE, a dynamic, interactive validation and evaluation framework for benchmarking coding agents in a multi-turn environment. To achieve this, we introduce a user simulator that can simulate different behavioral patterns and communicate with the coding agent. We also introduce a conversation validator that evaluates the user simulator's outputs to determine whether they fulfill the initial requirements and guide the agent toward successfully solving the task. The entire framework is embedded in a Harbor environment, which orchestrates the coding agent, user simulator, and conversation validator, and executes the test environment to determine whether the agent successfully solves the task. Our results and source code are available in our GitHub repository (https://github.com/lorenzbrehme/swe-dive_usersim).

</details>


### [34] [Validating Autoformalized Specifications via Contrapuntal Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30059)
*Yihan Dai,Xuanlin Liu,Haoxiang Jia,Tianchi Li,Xin Zhang,Sergey Mechtaev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"对位语言"(contrapuntal languages)：两个任务特定形式语言之间不存在保结构翻译，令LLM独立形式化自然语言意图，仅当求解器证明两者等价时接受规范，从而在无人工oracle条件下通过表征多样性解耦模型相关性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式规范可使AI生成代码可机械验证，但规范需由LLM从自然语言意图自动形式化得到，而自动形式化的规范本身不可信。核心难题是在不依赖人工审查等昂贵oracle的前提下验证规范是否真正刻画用户意图。常见的多输出投票策略因模型错误高度相关而失效——一致性可能反映共享错误而非正确性。

Method: 关键洞察：通过表征多样性使错误去相关。构造"对位语言"——每对任务特定形式语言均能表达目标规范，但二者之间不存在保结构翻译。LLM在每种语言中独立形式化同一意图，仅当求解器证明两种形式化等价时接受该规范。为构造此类语言，提出合成算法：采样种子规范，复用其片段作为基础规范语言之上的语法糖。在一阶逻辑规范上实现，并在HumanEval+与LiveCodeBench上以GPT-5.4 mini和Qwen3.6-Flash（含/不含思考模式）评测。

Result: 相较最强基线，被接受规范中正确（既完备又可靠）的比例在HumanEval+上提升12.0%，在LiveCodeBench上提升42.9%。被判定为完备的接受规范在HumanEval+上拒绝98.6%的错误程序变体，LiveCodeBench上拒绝96.2%，而最强基线分别为92.8%与78.3%。

Conclusion: 表征多样性（对位语言对+等价性证明）是无oracle规范验证的有效手段，显著提升接受规范的正确率与对错误程序的判别力，且语言对可由种子规范片段自动合成，具备可移植性。

Abstract: Formal specifications can make AI-generated code mechanically checkable. Because user intent is typically expressed in natural language rather than formal logic, specifications must first be derived from it, e.g., via LLM-based autoformalization. Yet autoformalized specifications are themselves untrusted, so a key challenge is validating whether they capture user intent without relying on a costly oracle such as human review. In this oracle-free setting, a natural strategy is to vote among model outputs, but because model errors are often correlated, agreement among outputs may reflect shared mistakes rather than correctness. Our key insight is that these errors can be decorrelated through representational diversity. We achieve this diversity with contrapuntal languages: pairs of task-specific formal languages, each designed to express the intended specification, yet admitting no structure-preserving translation between them. An LLM formalizes the intent independently in each language, and we accept a specification only if a solver proves the two formalizations equivalent. To construct such languages, we present a synthesis algorithm that reuses fragments of sampled seed specifications as syntactic sugar over a base specification language. We implement the approach for first-order logic specifications and evaluate it on HumanEval+ and LiveCodeBench using GPT-5.4 mini and Qwen3.6-Flash with and without thinking. Over the strongest baseline, our method increases the proportion of accepted specifications that are correct, i.e., complete and sound, by 12.0% on HumanEval+ and 42.9% on LiveCodeBench. The accepted specifications judged complete reject 98.6% of buggy program variants on HumanEval+ and 96.2% on LiveCodeBench, compared with 92.8% and 78.3% for the strongest baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [35] [A Small Model Asks Better: Fine-Tuning Query Clarification for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28686)
*Lihong He,Lucian Popa,Marina Danilevsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ClariEdge数据集（约3900例），用于多轮RAG场景下的查询澄清识别与澄清请求生成，并证明轻量LoRA微调模型可达96.6%分类准确率，优于多款大模型的提示基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统面对歧义用户查询时需要澄清。歧义来源包括：查询本身表述不良、在对话上下文中含义不明，以及查询本身规范但对照检索文档存在多个相关解释。现有工作缺少同时覆盖这些情况、并能区分"真歧义"与"可由上下文消解的表面歧义"的数据与方法。

Method: 构建ClariEdge数据集，约3900个示例，包含歧义查询、无需澄清的负例，并强调难负例（hard negatives）的作用；任务定义为二分类判定是否需澄清＋生成澄清请求；采用LoRA轻量微调模型，与多个更大模型的提示基线做对比。

Result: LoRA微调模型取得96.6%分类准确率，在分类与澄清生成质量上均优于多个大模型的提示基线；难负例是区分真歧义与表面歧义的关键；同一LoRA在检索前与检索后两种场景下均表现优异。

Conclusion: 该LoRA模型可作为RAG流程中答案生成前的双向检查点（检索前与检索后各设一次）；数据集已开源。

Abstract: Query clarification is essential for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems that face ambiguous user queries. We consider multi-turn grounded conversational scenarios where a user query is either ill-formed or ambiguous in the context of the prior dialog or, more interestingly, it may be well-formed but when posed against retrieved documents there are multiple relevant interpretations. The task is to recognize both such cases and then generate an appropriate clarification request to the user. We present ClariEdge, a new dataset of approximately 3,900 examples that include such ambiguous queries along with negative examples that do not require clarification. We also show that a lightweight fine-tuned LoRA achieves 96.6% classification accuracy and outperforms prompted baselines of many larger models on both classification and quality of clarification generation. Our study shows that hard negatives are an essential ingredient to separate out queries that are truly ambiguous from queries that may look ambiguous but are not (i.e., can be resolved from the context). The same LoRA achieves high performance in both pre-retrieval and post-retrieval scenarios; thus, it can be used in two places in a RAG pipeline as a check before answer generation. The ClariEdge dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/ClariEdge.

</details>


### [36] [Scaling in Games: Continued Pre-Training For Embodied Agents In Diverse Virtual Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28698)
*Kuan Zhang,Yukun Chen,Zhihao Yang,Yue Su,Xiangnan Wu,Zirong Chen,Run Luo,Jinkun Hou,Tao Tan,Yinhe Zheng,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控研究探究虚拟世界中的持续预训练（CPT），沿数据量、多样性与模型规模三轴展开120组实验，揭示数据/多样性/规模的差异化作用、观察到"早期接口相—后期世界相"两阶段现象并拟合经验缩放律，指出CPT的局限在于模仿学习上限与远域鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理环境中"数据—多样性—模型容量"的缩放收益源于共享物理规律，但在视觉风格、动力学、控制方式和目标各异的虚拟世界中，这些收益能否延续尚不清楚，亟需受控研究。

Method: 在3D虚拟世界中进行持续预训练（CPT）的受控缩放研究：120组训练配置，覆盖4个模型规模（0.8B–27B）、6档数据预算（50–1,600小时）、5种数据混合（从仅Minecraft到多世界混合语料）；以三级验证损失与从语料内世界到未见世界的玩法基准评估性能与泛化。

Result: (1) 数据量提升语料内表现；多样性提升向相关世界的迁移，但对远域世界收益有限；模型规模提升数据效率，但在单世界训练下对远域泛化更差。(2) 存在"早期接口相"（跨世界广泛收益）与"后期世界相"（收益收窄至语料内及相关世界），并拟合出关联验证损失与数据量、多样性、模型规模的经验缩放律。(3) 模型在3D世界中与Minecraft专家模型及前沿模型具竞争力，在实时设置下表现最佳。

Conclusion: 模仿学习的固有限制与远域世界的持续差距表明，构建通用具身智能体需要超越持续预训练的更丰富训练阶段（多阶段训练流程）。

Abstract: Scaling data, diversity and model capacity has improved embodied learning in physical environments, where diverse experiences remain grounded in shared physical laws. Whether these gains extend to virtual worlds with different visual styles, dynamics, controls, and objectives remains unclear. We investigate this question through a controlled study of continued pre-training (CPT) across three axes: data volume, diversity, and model size. Our 120 training configurations span four model sizes from 0.8B to 27B, six data budgets from 50 to 1,600 hours, and five mixtures ranging from Minecraft-only data to diverse multi-world corpora. We assess performance and generalization using three-level validation losses and gameplay benchmarks from in-corpus worlds to unseen worlds. Our results show: (1) more data improves in-corpus performance, diversity improves transfer to related worlds with limited gains in distant worlds, and model size improves data efficiency but generalizes worse to distant worlds under single-world training; (2) we observe an early interface phase with broad cross-world benefits and a later world phase with gains narrowed to in-corpus and related worlds, and fit an empirical scaling law relating validation loss to data volume, diversity and model size; and (3) our models are competitive with Minecraft specialists and frontier models in 3D worlds, and perform best in real-time settings. However, the limits of imitation and persistent gaps in distant worlds suggest that building generalist embodied agents requires richer training stages beyond continued pre-training.

</details>


### [37] [SpecEvo: Speculative Evolution with Large Language Models for Cost-Efficient Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28706)
*Ha Minh Hieu,Tongyao Zhu,Do Xuan Long,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecEvo多模型框架，通过轻量模型并行探索与证据固化、前沿模型稀疏战略干预的"推测-固化"工作流，实现低成本高效率的LLM驱动进化式科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的进化式搜索成本高、速度慢：对所有推理统一施加昂贵模型，且未充分利用失败、冗余与饱和试验中的证据。

Method: 多LLM协调框架：轻量模型承担并行Speculators（生成与改进候选程序）及Advisor（将成功、失败与饱和方向蒸馏为持续指导）；在稀疏检查点，由前沿模型Navigator审视进化种群，依据搜索停滞情况通过合成、精细改进或重构进行干预。

Result: 在覆盖四个科学与算法发现领域的176个任务上，匹配或超越前沿模型基线，API成本降低最多80%；在方程发现上评估效率较成本高效基线提升最多1.5倍；较最强CO-Bench基线提升3.8%。

Conclusion: 轨迹条件化的模型协调是低成本高效科学发现的有效路径。

Abstract: LLM-driven evolutionary search is a promising paradigm for scientific discovery, but existing methods remain costly and slow, often applying expensive reasoning uniformly while underusing evidence from failed, redundant, and saturated trials. We introduce Speculative Evolution (SpecEvo), a multi-LLM framework that coordinates lightweight and frontier models through continual consolidation of search evidence. Lightweight models power both parallel Speculators, which generate and refine candidate programs, and an Advisor, which distills accumulated successes, failures, and saturated directions into persistent guidance for subsequent exploration. At sparse checkpoints, a frontier-model Navigator inspects the evolving population and intervenes through synthesis, surgical refinement, or reframing according to search stagnation. This speculate-then-consolidate workflow delegates routine exploration and evidence consolidation to inexpensive models while reserving frontier reasoning for strategic interventions whose benefits extend across many subsequent low-cost evaluations. Across 176 tasks spanning four scientific and algorithmic discovery domains, SpecEvo matches or outperforms frontier-model baselines while reducing API cost by up to 80%, achieves up to 1.5x greater evaluation efficiency than cost-efficient baselines on equation discovery, and improves over the strongest CO-Bench baseline by 3.8%. These results support trajectory-conditioned model coordination as an effective approach to cost-efficient scientific discovery.

</details>


### [38] [Automating ML for Science: Can Frontier Agents Climb Scientific Hills in the Wild?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28763)
*Ming Zhong,Stacy Li,Nicholas Carlini,Matthew Jagielski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agent4Sci 长程测试台（20 个基准、11 个领域），衡量前沿编码智能体能否在真实条件下超越科学 ML 已发表最优模型，并诊断其短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体常被宣传为端到端自动科研者，而科学 ML 是严苛检验：提升科学模型通常需要超越常规 ML 工程的领域洞察。现有工作多以排行榜排名为主，缺少对"智能体能做出何种进展、在何处失效"的原位诊断。

Method: 构建 Agent4Sci 长程测试台，覆盖 11 个领域 20 个科学 ML 基准，考察在野条件下对已发表最优结果的改进能力；采用轨迹分析而非仅排行榜排序；并设计轻量插件式技能（研究构思、实验管理、定时检查清单）进行干预实验。

Result: 所有基准上前沿智能体均刷新已发表最优，Claude Opus 平均相对提升 10.4%，无运行被判学术不端；但增益几乎全部来自 ML 工程技巧而非研究级洞察。轨迹分析揭示三类反复出现的局限：新颖性误校准、资源利用不足、以及迅速退化为局部工程式搜索。加入插件技能后 Claude Opus 由 10.4% 升至 20.1%，Codex 由 4.4% 升至 8.1%，干预更具多样性与领域相关性。

Conclusion: Agent4Sci 为社区提供追踪并推动前沿智能体从 ML 工程走向领域洞察的评测手段，代码与数据已开源（GitHub / Hugging Face）。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents are increasingly framed as end-to-end automated researchers, and ML for science offers a demanding test of that promise: improving a scientific model often requires domain insight beyond routine ML engineering. We introduce Agent4Sci, a long-horizon testbed for measuring how far today's agents can improve the published best models on scientific ML benchmarks, spanning 20 benchmarks across 11 domains. Rather than ranking agents on a leaderboard, Agent4Sci asks what kinds of progress agents can make on these tasks under in-the-wild conditions, and where they fall short. Empirically, today's frontier agents advance the published best on every benchmark, with Claude Opus reaching 10.4% mean relative improvement and no run flagged for academic misconduct. These gains, however, come almost entirely from a bag of ML-engineering tricks rather than research-level insight. Trajectory analysis surfaces three recurring limitations in this setting: novelty miscalibration, resource under-utilization, and rapid collapse into local engineering sweeps. Lightweight plug-in skills for research ideation, experiment management, and scheduled checklists raise Claude Opus from 10.4% to 20.1% and Codex from 4.4% to 8.1%, while yielding more diverse, domain-relevant interventions. Agent4Sci gives the community a way to track and push frontier agents from ML engineering toward domain insight on real-world ML-for-science problems. Code and data are available on GitHub (https://github.com/maszhongming/agent4sci) and Hugging Face (https://huggingface.co/datasets/MingZhong/agent4sci).

</details>


### [39] [Forging More Tasks into Better Harnesses: Agentic Harness Evolution via Successive Scale Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28815)
*Jingzhe Liu,Jiliang Tang,Tong Zhao,Neil Shah,Clark Mingxuan Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示agentic harness进化中"任务池扩大反而性能下降"的悖论，并将其归因于证据选择次优，提出SSR框架自适应搜索最优证据组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: agentic harness evolution利用任务执行经验作为进化证据来优化指令、工具与控制流。直觉上更多进化任务应带来更好性能，但作者初步研究发现现有方法在任务池扩大时性能反而下降，分析表明根源在于优化过程中的证据选择次优——不同证据组合导致显著不同的进化结果。

Method: 提出Successive Scale Racing（SSR），在每轮进化中自适应搜索最优证据组合：依据任务质量与覆盖度构建多样化证据组合，并在小算力预算下对候选harness进行高效"竞赛"（racing）筛选。

Result: 在三个不同基准的所有六种配置上取得SOTA。任务池从40增至200时，SSR通过率从64.3%稳步提升至68.3%，而所有基线均下降；racing机制在减少60%验证执行的同时识别出近最优候选。

Conclusion: 证据选择是harness进化随任务池扩展的核心瓶颈，SSR通过质量/覆盖度驱动的组合构建与高效racing，将规模扩展转化为性能增益，并显著降低验证成本。

Abstract: Agentic harness evolution optimizes an agent's instructions, tools, and control flow by using its task execution experiences as evolving evidence. While more evolution tasks should intuitively improve performance, our preliminary study reveals a paradoxical failure mode: the performance of existing methods degrades as the task pool expands. Our further analysis demonstrates that this degradation stems from suboptimal evidence selection during optimization, as different evidence combinations lead to substantially different evolution outcomes. To address this issue, we propose Successive Scale Racing (SSR), a framework that adaptively searches for optimal evidence combinations in each evolution round. SSR constructs diverse evidence combinations based on the corresponding task quality and coverage, and efficiently races the resulting candidate harnesses under a small compute budget. Empirically, across three distinct benchmarks, SSR achieves state-of-the-art results in all six configurations. Notably, as the task pool grows from 40 to 200, SSR steadily improves the pass rate from 64.3% to 68.3%, while the baselines' performances all drop. Furthermore, its racing mechanism identifies near-optimal candidates while reducing validation executions by 60%.

</details>


### [40] [Decision language models as affordable and rapid alternatives to LLM judges of clinical generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28826)
*Rotem Gershon,Seffi Cohen,Dean Ariel,Havatzelet Bilavsky-Yarden,Amiel Sberro,Shai Yitzhaki,Osnat Tausky,Naama Golan,Maayan Rabinovitch,Ariel Avraham Hasidim,Itay Arianu,Maya Dagan,Ran Balicer,Noa Dagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文比较了决策语言模型 Jev 与前沿及开源 LLM 评判器在四类临床 AI 输出评估任务中的表现：Jev 的一致性可匹配或超过多数 LLM 评判器，且成本低 73–294 倍、耗时少 7–57 倍，有望支撑大规模临床 AI 评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床 AI 输出的自动化评判是必要的，但大规模使用 LLM 作为评判器成本高、速度慢，因此需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出/评估 Jev——一种返回结构化判断的决策语言模型，并在四类使用场景中与前沿及开源权重 LLM 评判器对比：118 条儿科问答的医生序数质量评分、HealthBench 二元标准、MedHELM 开放式任务（与三 LLM 陪审团一致性比较）、HealthBench Professional（与参考评判器 GPT-5.4 标准级一致性）。一致性指标为 Gwet's AC2 与期望得分。

Result: 儿科数据上 Jev 的 AC2 为 0.50（期望得分 0.53），匹配或超过 12 个 LLM 评判器中的 7 个，包括 Claude Opus 5.5（0.49）与 GPT-6（0.38）；HealthBench 二元标准上匹配或超过 6 个中的 4 个；MedHELM 上与三 LLM 陪审团的一致性接近或超过陪审员内部一致性；HealthBench Professional 上与 GPT-5.4 在 91% 标准上一致（其他评判器 91–93%）。前沿评判器成本为 Jev 的 73–294 倍、耗时为 7–57 倍。

Conclusion: 基于决策式结构化输出的 Jev 在保持与医生及基准评判器相当一致性的同时显著降低成本与延迟，可能为规模化临床 AI 评估提供高效方案。

Abstract: Automated judges of clinical artificial intelligence (AI) outputs are necessary, but large language model (LLM) judges are costly and slow at scale. We compared Jev, a decision language model that returns structured judgments, with frontier and open-weight LLM judges in four use cases. On 118 pediatric answers, Jev's agreement with physicians' ordinal quality ratings (Gwet's AC2 0.50; expected score 0.53) matched or exceeded that of seven of 12 LLM judges, including frontier models, Claude Opus 5.5 (0.49) and GPT-6 (0.38). On physician-graded binary HealthBench criteria, Jev matched or exceeded four of six LLM judges. On MedHELM's open-ended tasks, Jev's agreement with a three-LLM jury approached or exceeded the jurors' internal agreement. On HealthBench Professional, Jev matched the benchmark's reference judge, GPT-5.4, on 91% of criteria (other judges, 91-93%). Frontier judges cost 73-294 times more than Jev and took 7-57 times longer. Jev may enable efficient clinical AI evaluation at scale.

</details>


### [41] [Sketch2Sim: an end-to-end modular framework for hand-drawn circuit analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28836)
*Mohammad Kawsar,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Md Rakib Hossain,Farzana Akter,AKM Bahalul Haque,Rejwan Bin Sulaiman,Mohammed Amin Almaiah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个草图到仿真系统，将手绘电路图转换为SPICE兼容网表并自动求解节点电压与电流，服务于电路教学与工程设计评审。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手绘电路图难以直接仿真，缺少从原始图像到可执行网表的自动化流程；同时希望提升电路课程的教学体验并加速工程设计的评审与原型验证。

Method: 构建含1000张手绘RLC电路的数据集并标注元件、文本区域与字符；采用三级流水线：(i) 双YOLO检测器分别检测元件与文本区域；(ii) CRNN+CTC结合OCR的领域感知符号值提取；(iii) 图像处理（阈值化、骨架化、DBSCAN聚类）自动识别节点并与元件映射，生成网表并求解电路。

Result: 模块级定性与定量消融实验及端到端测试表明，系统在不同书写风格、光照条件与背景杂乱下均具有稳健性能。

Conclusion: 该系统可作为交互式学习辅助与实用工具，通过完整UI实现电路草图的快速数字化，用于学习、设计、原型制作与故障排查。

Abstract: This study proposes a sketch-to-simulation system to solve handwritten circuit diagrams. The system converts raw images into a Simulation Program with Integrated Circuit Emphasis (SPICE)-compatible netlist and automatically calculates the circuit's nodal voltages and currents. Another objective is to propose a holistic framework that benefits students and educators by enhancing the teaching and learning experience of electric circuits and expediting engineering design reviews. This article presents a multistage pipeline that converts raw photographs of handwritten electric circuits into executable SPICE-like netlists, providing a solution. A dataset of 1,000 hand-drawn Resistor-Inductor-Capacitor (RLC) circuits was curated and annotated for components, text regions, and character recognition. The proposed system decomposes a circuit diagram into three stages: (i) dual You Only Look Once (YOLO)-based detectors for components and text regions, (ii) a Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network (CRNN) with Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) and Optical Character Recognition (OCR)-based domain-aware symbol value extraction, and (iii) image processing with thresholding, skeletonization, and Density-Based Spatial Clustering of Applications with Noise (DBSCAN) to automatically identify nodes and map them with corresponding components. The proposed system produces the netlist and solves the circuit. The qualitative and quantitative ablation studies on modular stages, as well as end-to-end tests, demonstrate robust performance across various handwriting styles, lighting conditions, and clutter.This study serves as an interactive learning aid and practical tool for professionals, providing a comprehensive user interface (UI), enabling rapid digitization of circuit sketches for study, design, prototyping, or troubleshooting.

</details>


### [42] [Speedrunning Relational Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28855)
*Gabriel Wedson Mendonça de Souza Veloso,Luan Borges Teodoro Reis Sena,Denis Oliveira Correa,Francisco Galuppo Azevedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 modded-nanoRT，首个面向关系型 Transformer（Relational Transformers）的 speedrun 排行榜，通过"去中心化搜索、固定训练运行"的方式降低关系型预训练的准入门槛；并以校准方式选定留出数据库，配合 typed trilinear attention、Muon 与 SIGReg，在单张 H200 上 305.63 秒达标，较参考实现快 8.11 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型预训练的进展受限于"谁能负担得起试错"，关系型预训练尤甚：各实验室在私有协议下各自开发配方、结果难以累积。Speedrun 排行榜通过去中心化搜索（问题固定、技术栈开放、按达标墙钟时间排名、单卡即可保持纪录）来消除这一门槛，但此前尚无面向关系型 Transformer 的此类榜单。

Method: 1) 构建 modded-nanoRT 排行榜：固定任务、开放栈、以达到质量目标的墙钟时间为排名指标、单 GPU 可复现；2) 留出数据库的选择决定基准能否分辨改进，故通过校准进行固定——对六个候选库按"45 分钟学习曲线将改进与种子噪声区分开的锐度"排序；3) 初始纪录配方包含 typed trilinear attention（以外键遍历为条件来调制注意力分数与 value 的关系型算子）、Muon 优化器与 SIGReg。

Result: 在单张 NVIDIA H200 上以 305.63 秒达到质量目标，比参考实现快 8.11 倍；在开发数据库之外的 15 组对比中赢下 13 组。

Conclusion: 作者开源基准、冻结产物与纪录运行，并公开代码（https://github.com/kunumi/nanoRT），为关系型 Transformer 预训练建立可累积、可复现的社区化 speedrun 评测范式。

Abstract: Progress on foundation model pretraining is gated by who can afford a trial, and relational pretraining is no exception. Recipes are developed inside individual labs, reported under private protocols, and do not accumulate. Speedrun leaderboards remove that barrier by decentralizing the search rather than the training run. The problem is fixed, the stack is open, entries are ranked by wall-clock time to a quality target, and one GPU is enough to hold the record. We introduce modded-nanoRT, the first such leaderboard for Relational Transformers. Which database to hold out decides whether the benchmark can resolve improvements at all, so we fix it by calibration, ranking six candidates by how sharply a 45-minute learning curve separates improvement from seed noise. We open the leaderboard with typed trilinear attention, a relational operator that conditions attention scores and values on the foreign key traversed, together with Muon and SIGReg. The recipe reaches the target in 305.63 seconds on one NVIDIA H200, 8.11x faster than the reference implementation, and wins 13 of 15 pairs outside the development database. We will release the benchmark, its frozen artifacts, and the record runs. Our code is available at https://github.com/kunumi/nanoRT.

</details>


### [43] [To Manipulate or Not: Opinion Dynamics in LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28875)
*Mohammad Siahkamari,Phuoc Dat Nguyen,Stephen MacNeil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控的LLM–LLM辩论框架，让两个语言模型角色围绕气候变化归因话题多轮对话，其中一方被赋予立场、部分条件下被明确指示去说服，另一方无立场；在四种有序模型组合（GPT-6 Luna 与 Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite）下同时追踪显式报告立场与逐轮对话内行为。结果显示同模型互动平均缩小意见差距、跨模型互动则扩大，且"说服"指令并不能可靠提升影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说服研究多只测量对话交换中的单侧，而LLM日益参与双向塑造的长期对话，缺乏对双方意见动态的受控观测手段；同时，显式自报立场与对话内实际行为是否一致、角色指派与指令是否真能带来影响，均不明确。

Method: 构建受控的LLM–LLM辩论框架：两模型角色就争议话题（气候变化归因）重复讨论，一角被赋予既定立场并在某些条件下收到明确说服指令，另一角不设定立场；采用四种有序模型组合（GPT-6 Luna、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite），并行测量两类指标——角色显式报告的立场，以及独立的逐轮对话行为度量。

Result: 意见动态随模型组合显著不同：同模型互动平均缩小意见差距（但仅对两个模型之一明显），跨模型互动则扩大差距；显式报告与对话内行为刻画的是意见动态的不同侧面；笼统的说服指令未能可靠增加影响力。

Conclusion: 对话性影响不仅取决于指派角色与初始立场，还取决于参与的模型本身；这对在长程LLM互动中如何测量意见变化具有方法学启示，提示需区分自报立场与行为指标，并审慎使用"去说服"类指令。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly take part in extended conversations where they can both shape and be shaped by another party's views, yet most persuasion research measures only one side of that exchange. We introduce a controlled LLM-LLM debate framework in which two language-model roles repeatedly discuss a contested topic (climate-change attribution): one is given an assigned stance and, in some conditions, an explicit instruction to persuade, while the other receives no assigned position. Across four ordered model combinations involving GPT-6 Luna and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, we track both each role's explicitly reported position and a separate measure of its turn-by-turn behavior inside the conversation. Opinion dynamics differ substantially across model combinations: same-model interactions narrow the opinion gap on average, clearly for only one of the two models, while cross-model interactions widen it. Explicit reports and in-dialogue behavior capture distinct aspects of opinion dynamics, and a blanket instruction to persuade does not reliably increase influence. These findings suggest that conversational influence depends not only on assigned roles and initial positions but also on the participating models, with implications for how opinion change is measured in extended LLM interactions.

</details>


### [44] [Fig: Automated Multiagent Behavior Elicitor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28891)
*Anrui Gu,Zhichao Wang,Nicolai Ouporov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fig 是一个智能体系统，通过在游戏环境中实施有针对性的诱发干预，实现可编程、可控、可复现的多智能体行为评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统的社会性评估多观察环境里自发涌现的行为，缺乏对环境进行系统性干预以主动诱发特定行为的手段，导致评估的针对性与可控性不足。

Method: 提出 Fig（Finding Interactions in Games）：输入自然语言行为描述，将其分解为子行为，生成新的社会博弈游戏或选取已有游戏，并对游戏引擎、对手、指令与上下文执行单一或组合干预；评估上结合内置机械性检查与合成评分标准的判定，并通过人工审核验证干预、生成游戏与评估机制。

Result: 在六种测试行为（合作、利用、欺骗、隐蔽协调、背叛、破坏）上，模型在 Fig 生成的有效场景中平均 45.2% 的回合尝试目标行为；对存在匹配对照的"游戏—行为"组合，Fig 的干预使尝试率平均提升 10.9%。

Conclusion: 多智能体环境可作为可编程测试床，支持有意图、可控且可复现的行为评估。

Abstract: Many social evaluations for multiagent systems study behaviors as they emerge from environments instead of systematically intervening in the environment to elicit such behaviors. We introduce Fig (Finding Interactions in Games), an agentic system that performs targeted elicitation interventions on game environments. It takes a natural language behavior description, decomposes it into sub-behaviors, generates new social games or selects existing ones, and executes single or combined interventions to the games' engine, opponents, instructions, and context. To evaluate model behavior, it combines built-in mechanical checks and synthetic rubric judgments. We validate the interventions, generated games, and evaluation mechanisms via human review. Models attempt the target behavior in 45.2% of episodes on average for valid scenarios generated by Fig for six test behaviors -- cooperation, exploitation, deception, covert coordination, betrayal, and sabotage. For game-behavior pairs with available controls, Fig's interventions increase attempt rates by 10.9% on average compared to matched control games. Fig shows that multiagent environments can serve as programmable test beds for intentional, controllable, and reproducible behavior evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/fleet-ai/fig.

</details>


### [45] [Hidden Messages Between Diffusion Language Model Agents: Causal Evidence for Latent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28905)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Gavrilov,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"隐藏消息"（Hidden Messages）方法，让扩散语言模型智能体在连续去噪状态层面进行隐式通信，并通过消融与替换实验提供因果证据。类


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统通常在解码为文本后才通信，丢弃了扩散模型可修正的连续中间状态，浪费信息且引入离散化损失；作者想验证一条独立的扩散轨迹能否将紧凑隐消息传给另一条轨迹，且接收方是否真正利用了消息的语义内容。类

Method: 发送方将去噪状态压缩为4个带符号的稠密向量，接收方在保留自身上下文、画布与轨迹的前提下消费这些向量。设置两类因果对照：移除消息（derangement）检验通道是否起作用；替换为他例消息检验内容是否起作用。类

Result: 移除消息使 Tiny-A2D 上 +63.6 分、Dream-7B 上 +100.0 分的优势消失，定向替换可把接收方引向替换后的事实；QASC 双事实分工任务中匹配通信达 80.8%，错误示例消息降至 46.8%（内容效应 +34.0 分）；仅一次晚期传输达 87.8%，比四次循环交换高 +7.0 分。类

Conclusion: 实验结果构成扩散语言模型智能体间连续隐通信的因果证据，并表明信息传输的时机可能比交换次数更重要。类

Abstract: Diffusion language models form answers through a sequence of revisable continuous states, but multi-agent systems usually discard those states and make agents communicate only after decoding text. We ask whether one independent diffusion trajectory can pass a compact hidden message into another and, crucially, whether the receiver uses what that message means. In Hidden Messages, a sender compresses its denoising state into four signed dense vectors; the receiver consumes them while retaining its own context, canvas, and trajectory. Removing a message tests whether the channel matters; replacing it with a message from another example tests whether its content matters. Across controlled tasks, derangement removes a +63.6-point advantage on Tiny-A2D and a +100.0-point advantage on Dream-7B, while targeted substitutions steer the receiver toward the substituted fact. On QASC with the two supporting facts split across agents, matched communication reaches 80.8%, but a wrong-example message lowers accuracy to 46.8%, a content effect of +34.0 points. The surprising result is temporal: one late transfer reaches 87.8%, exceeding four recurrent exchanges by +7.0 points. These experiments provide causal evidence for continuous hidden communication between diffusion-LM agents and show that when information is transferred can matter more than how many times agents exchange it.

</details>


### [46] [DiffResearch: Accelerating Deep Research with Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28909)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Gavrilov,Mikhail Mozikov,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出开源多智能体深度研究框架DiffResearch，将扩散语言模型（dLLM）置于规划、检索、综合、修订各阶段核心，在保持其他条件不变下与AR模型对比，端到端约快6倍，并在RACE评分上取得最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究系统需迭代规划、取证、综合与修订，端到端延迟成为交互使用的关键瓶颈；现有生产系统几乎全基于自回归（AR）模型，继承从左到右解码，限制规划与长文写作效率。

Method: 构建开源多智能体框架DiffResearch，各阶段均使用dLLM；固定智能体脚手架、检索代码、提示与评测，仅替换骨干模型（开放扩散模型 vs 可比AR模型）；在100个Deep Research Bench任务上评测；并引入dFast分解模式，读取中间去噪状态、在生成完成前提取子查询。

Result: 扩散骨干端到端约快6倍，主要来自规划与写作的數量级提速；dFast使分解环节单独提速1.99倍（中位数）且报告质量无损；在RACE评分上dFast在三配置中总分最佳；公开系统级排行榜快照中，基于更强扩散模型的实例排名高于若干商业系统。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型可作为深度研究系统的有效骨干，显著降低延迟并保持甚至提升报告质量；利用中间去噪状态可进一步加速子查询分解；框架以Apache-2.0开源发布。

Abstract: Deep research systems iteratively plan, gather evidence, synthesize, and revise, making end-to-end latency a key bottleneck for interactive use. Almost all production systems are built on autoregressive (AR) language models and inherit left-to-right decoding in planning and long-form writing. We present DiffResearch, an open multi-agent deep research framework that places a diffusion language model (dLLM) at the core of every stage, and measure how a diffusion backbone changes latency and report quality. Holding the agentic scaffold, retrieval code, prompts, and evaluation fixed, we swap the backbone between an open diffusion model and a comparable AR model. Across 100 Deep Research Bench tasks, the diffusion backbone is roughly 6 times faster end-to-end, driven by order-of-magnitude reductions in planning and writing. We further introduce dFast, a decomposition mode that reads intermediate denoising states and extracts subqueries before generation finishes, providing a 1.99 times median speedup for decomposition in isolation with no loss of report quality. On the benchmark's RACE rubric, dFast obtains the best overall score among the three configurations. Finally, in a public system-level leaderboard snapshot, an instance built on a stronger diffusion model ranks above several commercial systems. We release DiffResearch under an Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [47] [Simply Logical -- The First Three Decades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28912)
*Peter Flach,Kacper Sokol,Jan Wielemaker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文追溯Prolog教材《Simply Logical》从1994年附软盘的可运行印刷版，历经多个在线版本，到如今全交互式在线版本的演化历程，从作者与读者双重视角探讨技术对教育材料的潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察技术（尤其是Web技术）对教育材料的潜在影响，并总结交互式Prolog教学材料在未来的发展机会；同时为Prolog教师及关注最新教育技术者提供参考。

Method: 采用三位作者互补视角的案例式回顾：Peter阐述原书理念及据此授课的经验；Jan展示当代Web编程如何实现该书理念的完全交互化；Kacper说明如何用多种技术构建支撑交互式在线版的多功能写作工具链（authoring toolkit）。

Result: 汇集三种不同但连贯的视角，形成了提供沉浸式学习体验的交互式在线教材，并据此形成对未来交互式Prolog教学材料发展的反思。

Conclusion: 该书从印刷+软盘到全交互在线版的演化表明Web技术的持续发展可为教育材料带来实质性变革；所获洞见对Prolog教学者及教育技术研究者具有参考价值。

Abstract: This paper charts the evolution of the Prolog textbook Simply Logical -- Intelligent Reasoning by Example from print with runnable programmes on a 3.5-inch diskette published in 1994, via various intermediate online versions, to the fully interactive online edition available today. Our aim is to investigate -- from both the writer's and the reader's perspectives -- the potential impact of technology on educational materials. The three authors of this paper present three distinct and complementary points of view, which come together to shape an interactive online book that offers an immersive learning experience. Peter describes the philosophy behind the original book and experiences teaching from it. Jan demonstrates how contemporary web programming has enabled a fully interactive realisation of the book's philosophy. Kacper reports how different technologies can be adopted to build a versatile authoring toolkit that underpins the interactive online edition of the book. Collating these distinct yet coherent perspectives allows us to reflect on future opportunities for interactive Prolog teaching materials arising from the continuous development of web technologies. The insights we offer should be of interest to teachers of Prolog as well as those wanting to find out about the latest educational technologies.

</details>


### [48] [D-Skill: Distilling Teacher Knowledge through Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28914)
*Shuo Yuan,Dacheng Li,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D-Skill 将教师模型视为黑盒，通过对比配对轨迹提取可解释的自然语言"技能文档"，在小模型推理时注入，无需权重更新与标注，即可弥合 75%-95% 的师生差距并显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型智能体在复杂任务上表现优异，但推理成本高昂，因此需要把能力迁移到小模型。传统知识蒸馏依赖参数更新，需要访问学生权重、额外训练算力，且知识以隐式方式编码在参数中，缺乏可解释性且迁移门槛高。

Method: 提出 D-Skill 框架：教师作为黑盒，收集成对的师生 rollout 轨迹，分析二者行为的一致性与分歧点以识别教师的行为优势；将得到的经验离线整合为技能文档（可接入任意兼容的技能更新后端）；推理时把该文档作为上下文提供给学生。整个技能构建完全离线、基于固定轨迹完成，不需要标签、额外环境交互，也不需要 rollout 之后的权重更新。

Result: 在 SpreadsheetBench-Verified、ALFWorld 与国际象棋残局三类任务、多组师生配对上的实验显示：不改动任何模型权重即可弥合 75%-95% 的师生差距；单次离线遍历配对轨迹即优于 one-epoch SFT 基线，并与多轮 SFT 具有竞争力；同模型家族下学生每个任务的推理成本比教师低 5.7x-8.3x。

Conclusion: 自然语言技能蒸馏可作为基于权重的能力迁移的高效替代方案，兼具性能、可解释性与成本优势。

Abstract: Frontier language-model agents excel at complex tasks, but high inference costs make transferring their capabilities to smaller models essential. Traditional knowledge distillation achieves this through parameter updates, requiring access to the student's weights and additional training compute while encoding the transferred knowledge implicitly in the parameters. We introduce D-Skill, a framework that treats the teacher as a black box and distills its behavioral advantage into an interpretable natural-language skill by analyzing paired rollouts and identifying behavioral divergences and agreements. The resulting lessons are consolidated offline, with any compatible skill-update backend, into a skill document that is provided to the student at inference time. D-Skill constructs the skill entirely offline from fixed trajectories, without labels, further environment interaction, or weight updates after rollout collection. Across SpreadsheetBench-Verified, ALFWorld, and chess puzzles spanning multiple teacher-student pairs, D-Skill closes 75%-95% of the teacher-student gap without touching model weights. After a single offline pass over the paired traces, D-Skill outperforms one-epoch SFT baselines and remains competitive with multi-epoch SFT. Under the same model family, the resulting student also incurs 5.7x-8.3x lower inference cost per task than the teacher. Our results demonstrate that natural-language skill distillation offers a performant, interpretable, and efficient alternative to weight-based capability transfer.

</details>


### [49] [Back Up Before You Regret: Measuring Backup Awareness and Automating Backup in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28934)
*Xu Li,Simon Yu,Minzhou Pan,Yiyou Sun,Zhaorun Chen,Bo Li,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RegretBench基准评估编码智能体的"备份意识"，发现六个前沿模型任务完成率86.1%但仅在15.2%的关键时刻提前备份；为此提出BackUpper，在每次工具调用前自动保存受影响状态，规则+子代理结合，恢复相似度92.2%，成本仅$0.014/任务，token比自备份基线少7倍，用户偏好对齐F1达84.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可自主执行任务并修改真实状态（产物、软件环境、配置），但缺乏"备份意识"——即预判某操作会改动未来可能需要恢复的状态并事先保存。已在X.com、GitHub、Stack Overflow上大量出现用户后悔未备份的真实案例。由于难以预测哪些改动会引发后悔，需要在机制层面保证动作可逆。

Method: 1) 构建RegretBench：205个可执行任务，来源于真实"后悔时刻"报告；2) 提出BackUpper，作为harness插件的备份层，拦截每次工具调用并在修改前保存受影响状态；3) 规则策略覆盖常见操作，轻量子代理处理复杂情形，规则表可随子代理决策持续扩展；4) 支持用户指定备份范围以收窄作用域。

Result: 六个前沿模型平均任务完成率86.1%，但关键时刻备份率仅15.2%，说明完成能力不等于备份意识。BackUpper覆盖全部后悔时刻动作，状态恢复与原状态平均相似度92.2%，每任务成本$0.014，token消耗比主代理自备份基线少7倍；在用户偏好约束下，判断"动作是否值得保存"与人工标注的F1为84.5%。

Conclusion: 将备份从"智能体的认知能力"转为"系统的结构保障"是可行且廉价的：智能体可在低成本下保持动作可逆，同时尊重用户偏好。基准与即插即用备份层为可逆性研究提供了评测与实现范式。

Abstract: Coding agents now complete tasks autonomously, modifying real-world state such as artifacts, software environments, and configurations along the way. Yet they show limited backup awareness: recognizing that an action will modify state that may later be needed, and preserving it beforehand. To evaluate backup awareness, we introduce RegretBench, a benchmark of 205 executable tasks grounded in regret moments reported on platforms such as X.com, GitHub, and Stack Overflow, where users explicitly regretted not preserving state they later needed. Across six frontier models, agents complete 86.1% of tasks on average yet back up before these moments in only 15.2%, showing that task completion ability does not imply backup awareness. Because it is hard to predict which changes will be regretted, we propose BackUpper, a harness plug-in backup layer that checks every tool call and preserves affected state before modification. It combines rule-based strategies for common operations with a lightweight subagent for complex cases, and its rule table can expand with subagent decisions over time. On RegretBench, BackUpper covers every regret moment action and restores states with 92.2% average similarity to the original. Achieving this costs only $0.014 per task, with 7x fewer tokens than the main-agent self-backup baseline. When users have preferences on what to back up, BackUpper can also narrow its scope accordingly, matching human annotations on whether an action is worth preserving with an 84.5% F1 score. These results suggest that agents can keep actions reversible at low cost while respecting users' preferences.

</details>


### [50] [Self-Improving Agents with World Model Predictive Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28945)
*Haori Lu,Tianyu Gao,Yaoyao Liu,Yingying Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种受MPPI控制的测试时框架，将多分支预测分析与基于经验的自适应结合，用于LLM智能体的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需在部署中与环境交互并改进决策，但"预判未来结果"与"利用累积交互经验"如何协同支撑决策仍研究不足。

Method: 测试时框架（类比MPPI控制）：每步提出多个候选动作，用世界模型"想象"其结果以在不实际执行的情况下比较；因语言环境无显式动力学与成本，预测结果按任务进展与风险评估，并以MPPI式权重对成本加权指导最终决策；双分支经验记忆实现自改进——规划分支优化候选生成与动作选择，世界模型分支改进下一观测预测。

Result: 在ALFWorld与ScienceWorld上，基于三种骨干模型均表现强劲整体性能，并验证经验引导预测控制的有效性。

Conclusion: 多分支预测分析与经验驱动自适应可有效提升LLM智能体测试时决策；代码将公开。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to interact with environments and improve their decisions during deployment. However, how anticipating future outcomes and drawing on accumulated interaction experience can jointly support decision-making remains underexplored. We propose a test-time framework, inspired by Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control, that combines multi-branch predictive analysis with experience-based adaptation. At each step, the agent proposes multiple candidate actions and imagines their outcomes with a world model, so that alternatives can be compared without being executed in the environment. Since language environments provide neither explicit dynamics nor costs, the predicted outcomes are evaluated for task progress and risk, and MPPI-style weights over the resulting costs guide the final decision. A dual-branch experience memory enables self-improvement: the planner branch refines candidate generation and action selection, while the world model branch improves next-observation prediction. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld across three backbones demonstrate the strong overall performance of our method and the effectiveness of experience-guided predictive control. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [51] [Behavior Beyond Text: Evaluating Refusal Transfer Failure in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28982)
*Ekagra Gupta,Raj Saha,Aniekan-Abasi Inyang,Adam Al Gharib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了跨四领域、2,304条提示的匹配三元组基准（纯文本/标准工具/对抗性工具框架），发现文本中被拒绝的有害请求在工具调用场景下大量转化为不安全工具调用（10-52%），并从残差流拒绝方向给出机制性解释，指出仅靠激活干预无法有效修复，强调必须直接评估智能体的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益作为工具调用智能体部署，但安全评估仍主要停留在"文本层面拒绝有害请求"的能力上。作者试图回答一个关键问题：文本中的拒绝行为是否能迁移到工具使用场景，即"说不会做"是否等于"不会做"。

Method: 1）构建包含2,304条提示、覆盖四个领域的基准，采用匹配三元组设计：同一有害/良性请求分别以纯文本、标准工具启用、对抗性工具框架三种形式呈现；2）以20个预定义禁止动作作为工具调用安全性的判定标准；3）测试五个不同规模与家族模型；4）通过残差流探针识别各模型的"拒绝相关方向"，考察工具情境下该方向激活的变化及其对不安全调用的预测能力（AUC）；5）使用激活修补（activation patching）将信号恢复至纯文本水平，并进行方向引导（steering）实验。

Result: 文本中被拒绝的请求频繁导致不安全工具调用：标准工具提示下，此类分歧出现在10-52%的有害请求上。工具情境下，五个模型中有四个沿拒绝方向的激活下降；激活值越低越能预测不安全工具调用（AUC 0.76-0.86）。将信号修补回纯文本水平仅能阻止部分不安全调用，且主要通过抑制工具使用实现，而非生成更安全的调用；沿拒绝方向引导只有在强度高到同时抑制良性工具使用或损害输出质量时，才几乎消除不安全调用。

Conclusion: 所识别出的拒绝方向部分参与"模型是否行动"的决策，但只能部分解释拒绝行为的分歧。因此，评估工具调用智能体的安全性必须同时直接检验其动作与文本响应，不能以文本拒绝率替代行为安全评估。

Abstract: While large language models are increasingly deployed as tool-calling agents, their safety evaluations still focus primarily on their ability to refuse harmful requests in text. We investigate whether the refusal behavior in text transfers to tool use. We construct a benchmark of 2,304 prompts across four domains, consisting of matched triplets that present the same harmful or benign request in text-only, standard tool-enabled, and adversarially framed tool-enabled settings. We then assess tool-call safety using 20 predefined forbidden actions. Testing five models of varying sizes and families, we find that requests refused in text frequently result in unsafe tool calls. Under standard tool-enabled prompting, this divergence occurs for 10-52 percent of harmful requests refused in chat. To mechanistically investigate this gap, we identify a refusal-associated direction in the residual stream of each of the five models. We find that in tool-enabled contexts, the activations along this direction decrease in four of five models. Furthermore, lower activation predicts unsafe tool calls across all five models (AUC 0.76-0.86). However, restoring the signal to the respective text-only value through activation patching only prevents a subset of unsafe calls, primarily by suppressing tool use rather than by producing safer calls. Steering along the refusal direction nearly eliminates unsafe calls only at strengths that also suppress benign tool use or degrade the output quality. Our findings indicate that the identified refusal direction contributes to whether a model acts, but only partially explains the refusal divergence. Therefore, evaluating the safety of tool-calling agents requires directly examining their actions along with their textual responses.

</details>


### [52] [PerMIT: Securing Persistent Memory for LLM Agents via Machine-Checked Integrity Theorems and Scalable Community Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28987)
*Tanqiu Jiang,Min Bai,Sadat Shahriar,Wanyu Du,Sandesh Swamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PerMIT 是一种带可证明完整性保证的持久记忆架构，通过有限记忆接口中介不可信环境值的释放与使用，使所有攻击归约为门控处的单次可审计确认；实验显示零攻击成功率，且经确认的知识可在用户间累积共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆是注入与投毒几乎不设防的攻击面，同一风险也限制了智能体之间的记忆共享。现有工作缺乏在显式信任假设下的可证明完整性保证。

Method: 提出 PerMIT 架构，采用显式信任假设（仅信任用户键入输入与门控确认值）；通过有限记忆接口中介不可信环境派生值的释放与使用，同时保留智能体驱动的多轮规划与工具选择。在该有限接口上给出两个完整性定理并用 Lean 4 机器验证：环境派生记忆仅在门控确认后才能到达敏感动作，且仅通过门控展示内容影响行为。确认即对确切内容的日志化裁决，可跨用户累积为社区确认。

Result: 在 Trojan Hippo 上，自验证与轻量验证器均观察到零攻击成功率；在显式假设下证明并经仿真验证"共同扩展"性质：社区扩大时投毒被推广与良性知识被误逐的风险均下降，而单用户验证开销不上升。在 τ²-bench 上，最多 90 个自主智能体组成的社区使用 PerMIT 构建共享知识库，无直接记忆注入被推广，所得知识显著提升新智能体在留出任务上的表现。

Conclusion: PerMIT 将记忆投毒风险归约为可审计的门控决策，实现可证明的完整性与可扩展的安全共享，暗示了一条智能体在测试时安全自我改进的路径。

Abstract: Persistent memory is a largely undefended surface for injection and poisoning, and the same risk limits memory sharing among agents. We present PerMIT, a memory architecture with provable integrity guarantees under an explicit trust assumption: only user-typed inputs and values confirmed at a gate are trusted. PerMIT mediates the release and use of untrusted environment-derived values through a finite memory interface, while preserving agent-driven multi-turn planning and tool choice. Over this finite interface, we prove two integrity theorems that are also machine-checked in Lean 4, which together ensure that environment-derived memory reaches a sensitive action only if confirmed at the gate and influences the agent's behavior only through what the gate displays. Every attack in our threat model thereby reduces to a single auditable decision, which a human, an LLM verifier, or both can make in practice. On Trojan Hippo, PerMIT records zero observed attack success with self-verification and lightweight verifiers. Because each confirmation is a logged verdict on exact content, endorsements accumulate across users, and sufficiently endorsed items become community-confirmed with no further verification required. Under explicit assumptions, we prove and corroborate in simulation a co-scaling property: as the community grows, the risks of promoting poison and of wrongly evicting benign knowledge fall, while per-user verification effort does not rise. On $τ^2$-bench, communities of up to 90 autonomous agents use PerMIT to build shared knowledge bases with no direct memory injection promoted, and the resulting knowledge significantly improves new agents on held-out tasks, suggesting a path toward agents that safely self-improve at test time.

</details>


### [53] [Real PMIDs, Wrong Papers: Harness Design for Biomedical Claim-Verification Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29812)
*Mohammed Saqlain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在SciFact生物医学论断验证任务上以PubMed为唯一工具进行受控消融，发现检索而非推理是主要瓶颈：Agent自写查询使金标准召回率提升三倍并取得最佳判定准确率，但需金摘要才能显著提升到79-86%；闭源模型引用的PMID几乎都存在却几乎都不相关，存在性检查不足以防伪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献检索型语言Agent已成为生命科学科研助手的标准组件，但其框架（harness）中哪些部分真正起作用缺乏受控证据。

Method: 固定骨干模型与输出格式，在SciFact上用实时PubMed API作为唯一工具，比较四种harness：闭卷作答、以论断为查询的单次检索、ReAct式自写PubMed查询的Agent、以及给定金摘要的oracle；每种配两个高效能前沿骨干。并进行引用审计，用跨家族LLM评审判断引用相关性。

Result: 朴素单次检索仅6%论断找到金标准论文，且只把过度自信的错误转为弃答而无准确率提升；自写查询使金召回率三倍化，判定最佳（62% vs 其他现实harness的49-59%，但样本量下不显著），且Agent仅用不到一半检索预算；给定金摘要时准确率达79-86%，显著高出17-24个点。引用审计：闭卷模型引用PMID存在率327/335，但跨家族评审认为仅3/327相关，而搜索Agent引用相关率为67%。

Conclusion: 检索是主要瓶颈而非推理；查询构造能力是harness关键杠杆；仅做引用存在性检查不足以保障科学Agent的可靠性，需进一步核查相关性。代码、提示词与全部缓存工具调用已开源。

Abstract: Literature-searching language agents are becoming a standard component of scientific assistants for the life sciences, yet there is little controlled evidence on which parts of their harness matter. We run a controlled ablation on biomedical claim verification (SciFact) with the live PubMed API as the only tool. Holding the backbone and output format fixed, we compare four harnesses: closed-book answering, single-shot retrieval using the claim as the query, a ReAct-style agent that writes its own PubMed queries, and an oracle given the gold abstracts, each with two efficient frontier backbones. Naive single-shot retrieval finds the gold paper for only 6% of claims and turns overconfident errors into abstentions without improving accuracy. Letting the model formulate its own queries triples gold recall and yields the best verdicts (62% accuracy vs. 49-59% for the other realistic harnesses), although these differences are not significant at our sample size and agents use fewer than half of their search budget. With gold abstracts, the same models reach 79-86% accuracy, a significant 17-24 point gap showing that retrieval, not reasoning, is the main bottleneck. Finally, a citation audit shows that closed-book models cite PubMed IDs that almost always exist (327/335) but are almost never relevant: a cross-family LLM judge rates only 3 of 327 existing citations as relevant to the claim, versus 67% for citations produced by the search agent. Existence checks are therefore insufficient safeguards for scientific agents. We release code, prompts, and all cached tool calls at https://github.com/saqlain2204/real-pmids-wrong-papers.

</details>


### [54] [AgentPolicyForge: From Executable Environments to Policy Benchmarks and Adaptive Guards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28989)
*Chao Shang,Wanyu Du,Yi Zhang,Sandesh Swamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AgentPolicyForge 框架，利用可执行环境构建策略基准并迭代训练策略守卫（policy guard），据此构建 PolicyGym 数据集（10 类策略、698 个训练场景、254 个独立留出测试场景），相比仅靠提示词自我约束，守卫方案将攻击成功率相对降低 40-44% 且良性拒绝更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定制化 agent 部署需对工具、资源和执行条件施加策略约束，但将策略写入任务 agent 提示词无法可靠阻止违规；训练守卫又缺乏标注好的合规/违规工具调用样本。

Method: AgentPolicyForge：以可执行环境构建策略基准并迭代训练守卫。构建 PolicyGym 数据集（10 个策略族，698 个训练场景 + 254 个独立留出场景，测试集经人工审核）。工具调用被实际执行并记录结果以判定合规性；每个违规样本均使用被允许的工具，需检查参数、资源与上下文而非仅凭工具身份。训练流程中攻击者基于检索到的策略生成请求，守卫在平衡的、经环境验证的样本上微调，并支持策略档案（archive）的迭代更新。

Result: 即使档案固定，训练即可提升策略执行效果；更新档案相比固定档案进一步降低域内攻击成功率。在 PolicyGym 上，最终基于守卫的配置相比仅策略提示实现攻击成功率 40-44% 的相对下降，且良性请求误拒更少。

Conclusion: 基于可执行环境验证样本训练的独立策略守卫，优于依赖任务 agent 提示词自我调节；档案迭代更新与平衡数据微调是提升策略合规性的关键，并在降低攻击成功率与减少误拒间取得更好权衡。

Abstract: Customized agent deployments define policies over tools, resources, and execution conditions. Providing these policies in the task agent's prompt does not reliably prevent violations. Training a guard requires examples of tool calls that comply with or violate those policies. We introduce AgentPolicyForge, a framework that uses executable environments to construct policy benchmarks and iteratively train policy guards. With this framework, we construct PolicyGym, a dataset across ten policy families using 698 scenarios for training data generation and 254 separate held-out scenarios for human-reviewed test sets. Tool calls are executed and their outcomes recorded to help determine whether they comply with the policy. Every violating case uses a permitted tool, requiring checks of arguments, resources, and context rather than tool identity alone. The case construction process supports iterative guard training: an attacker generates requests using retrieved strategies, and the guard is fine-tuned on balanced, environment-verified examples. Training improves policy enforcement even with a fixed archive. Updating the archive further reduces in-domain attack success compared with keeping it fixed. We compare policy-aware task agent self-regulation with a trained policy guard. On PolicyGym, the final guard-based configuration achieves a 40-44% relative reduction in attack success rate and fewer benign rejections than policy prompting alone.

</details>


### [55] [An Evidence-Driven Multi-Agent Compound AI System for Head and Neck Proton Radiotherapy Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29010)
*Qingqing Wang,Liqiang Xiao,Chang Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个证据驱动的多智能体复合AI系统，将基于DRL（PPO）的质子PBS自动计划与基于LLM的对话式计划改进相结合，实现个性化放疗计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗计划需在满足临床标准的同时平衡相互竞争的目标并契合医师偏好，单一自动规划难以应对需进一步调整的复杂病例。

Method: PPO策略网络无人干预生成每位患者多个候选计划，选一为目标、其余作参考；复杂病例由LLM多智能体系统通过自然语言改进：规划智能体（Qwen3-14B，经SFT+GRPO后训练，依据历史计划证据给出推荐）、聊天机器人（统一对话接口）、评估智能体（检索临床指标、识别约束违背）。21例头颈癌患者，每人5个PPO计划，其中50个计划用于指令引导改进。

Result: 相比基座LLM，后训练Qwen3-14B在指令遵循、信息检索与领域推理上提升；改进后的调整更有效优化用户优先目标、限制竞争目标恶化，形成更符合临床的患者特异权衡。

Conclusion: 该复合AI系统提供用户友好的计划平台，为个性化质子PBS放疗计划提供有前景的方案。

Abstract: Radiation treatment planning requires balancing competing objectives to satisfy clinical criteria while accommodating physicians' preferences. This study aims to develop an evidence-driven, multi-agent compound AI system that integrates DRL-based automated treatment planning with LLM-based conversational plan refinement for proton PBS therapy. A policy network trained using PPO first generates multiple plans without human intervention, from which one is selected as the target plan and the others serve as references for subsequent refinements. For challenging cases requiring further adjustments, an LLM-based multi-agent system enables plan refinement through natural language interactions. The system comprises a planning agent that recommends evidence-supported adjustments, a chatbot providing a unified conversational interface, and an evaluation agent that retrieves clinical metrics and identifies constraint violations. To enhance domain-specific reasoning, the planning agent based on Qwen3-14B is designed to justify its recommendations using evidence from previously generated plans and post-trained on task-specific data using SFT and GRPO. The framework was trained and evaluated using 21 patients with H&N cancers, with 5 PPO-generated plans per patient. Of these, 50 plans were selected for instruction-guided refinement. Compared with the base LLM, the post-trained Qwen3-14B demonstrated improved instruction-following, information retrieval and domain-specific reasoning capabilities. The resulting adjustments more effectively improved user-prioritized objectives while limiting deterioration in competing objectives, yielding more clinically appropriate trade-offs in patient-specific plan refinement. The proposed compound AI system provides a user-friendly planning platform, offering a promising approach to personalized proton PBS treatment planning.

</details>


### [56] [GenXR-Bench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Interactive XR Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29014)
*William Huang,David Li,Xiang 'Anthony' Chen,Xun Qian,Nels Numan,Zhongyi Zhou,Hyunsung Cho,Salman Muin Kayser Chishti,Ding Xia,Xincheng Huang,Jiahao Ren,Faraz Faruqi,Tianyu Xu,Yang Zhang,David Kim,Ruofei Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个用于端到端评估编程智能体开发XR应用的基准GenXR-Bench，含112个专家任务（24个功能实现、21个缺陷修复、67个应用生成），测试套件与 blinded 专家判断一致率96.5%；10个模型中最优配置总体Pass@1仅50.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程智能体已从单文件生成迈向长程仓库级工程，但其编写交互式扩展现实（XR）应用的能力尚缺乏评估手段。XR应用运行于动态三维物理环境，运行时鲁棒性同时依赖连续用户运动、空间输入与环境感知，传统软件评测范式无法覆盖。

Method: 构建GenXR-Bench：112个专家撰写任务，覆盖24项功能实现、21项缺陷修复、67项应用生成；保留测试结合脚本化与自适应交互，并采用程序化断言与视觉断言双重校验核心功能与运行时行为；测试套件在消费级XR硬件上执行，与124个候选应用的盲审专家判断对比验证；对10个模型进行评测并做失败分析。

Result: 测试套件与盲审专家判断的一致率达96.5%；10个模型中最强配置总体Pass@1仅50.9%；失败分析揭示三类反复出现的错误：异步传感器生命周期、物理接地（physical grounding）、具身交互语义。

Conclusion: GenXR-Bench为空间计算中交互性、物理具身条件下的编程智能体评估提供了可复现测试平台，表明现有模型在XR软件开发上仍存在显著能力缺口。

Abstract: Coding agents are rapidly advancing from single-file generation to long-horizon repository-scale engineering, yet the evaluation of their capabilities to author interactive extended reality (XR) applications remains an open challenge. Unlike traditional software, XR applications operate within dynamic 3D physical environments where runtime robustness depends jointly on continuous user motion, spatial input, and environmental sensing. We introduce GenXR-Bench, the first benchmark for the end-to-end evaluation of coding agents in XR software development. GenXR-Bench contains 112 expert-authored tasks spanning 24 feature implementations, 21 bug repairs, and 67 application generation tasks. Held-out tests combine scripted and adaptive interactions with programmatic and visual assertions to evaluate core functionality and runtime behavior. GenXR-Bench's testing suite achieves 96.5% accuracy against blinded expert judgments of 124 candidate applications executed on consumer XR hardware. Evaluating 10 models on GenXR-Bench, we find that the strongest configuration achieves only 50.9% overall Pass@1. Failure analysis identifies recurring misunderstandings in asynchronous sensor lifecycles, physical grounding, and embodied interaction semantics. GenXR-Bench establishes a reproducible testbed for evaluating coding agents in the interactive and physically situated conditions of spatial computing.

</details>


### [57] [Inheritance as an Admission Test for LLM Prompt Merge Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29023)
*Zan-Kai Chong,Hiroyuki Ohsaki,Bryan Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以"合并准入检验"替代饱和固定验证器的LLM智能体迭代改进框架，用种群相对评分与剂量-响应检验判定算子是否真正有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过迭代直到固定验证器饱和来提升，一旦验证器饱和，循环便无提升空间；且种群均值可能仅因淘汰效应而在固定种子内上升，导致"虚假训练"。

Method: 以当前种群为评分基准，冻结面板记录是否有人提升；仅当最终均值超过零代最优成员才计为训练。合并准入检验要求：子代朝供体方向移动、超过零剂量下同算子产生的子代、且该移动的下置信界达到请求剂量的一半；可检测的不足则拒绝算子，否则判为未定。以实测适应度φ为选择器，参考性能θ为未使用标尺；全部测量使用gemini-3.1-flash-lite、单一谈判任务，未设固定oracle对照组。比较tactic append、free rewrite、template append、constrained rewrite四种算子，并进行剂量响应与留出重探测。

Result: 开放循环中：tactic append被准入；free rewrite仅移动约三分之一请求剂量被拒绝；template append首次探测通过界但在重探测未通过，判为未定；constrained rewrite被准入但未在闭循环运行。对合并未见过的五个情境进行留出重探测，确认两种append有效移动，未能挽救free rewrite；二者均不随剂量上升。无任何free-rewrite运行超过零代最优成员，而所有tactic-append与template-append运行均超过，采样循环子代保持该分化；free rewrite也未进一步朝供体移动，两种append仍通过界但幅度小于开放循环。

Conclusion: 基于种群的相对评分与剂量约束的合并准入检验能有效区分真实改进与淘汰假象，稳定识别append类算子有效、free rewrite无效；但算子效果不呈剂量响应，且开放循环结论需在闭循环中进一步验证。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are often improved by iterating until a fixed verifier is done. Once that verifier saturates, the loop has nothing left to climb. We instead score agents against the current population, and a frozen panel records whether anyone got better. The panel mean can rise inside the seeds by elimination alone. We therefore count a run as training only when its final mean beats the best generation-zero member. A merge is admitted only when the child moves toward the donor, past the child the same operator produces at zero dose, and the lower confidence bound on that movement reaches half the requested dose. That is the admission test. A detectable shortfall rejects the operator, and the test is otherwise undetermined. We keep realised fitness φas the selector and reference performance θas the unused ruler. Every measurement uses gemini-3.1-flash-lite on one negotiation task, and no fixed-oracle arm was run. In the open loop, tactic append grafts short donor rules and is admitted. Free rewrite moves only a third of the requested dose and is rejected. Template append pastes raw donor replies into the child. Its movement clears the bound on the first probe but not on the re-probe, and it is left undetermined. A held-out re-probe on the five situations the merge never saw confirms that both appends move, and it does not rescue free rewrite. Neither append rises with the dose. Constrained rewrite is admitted in the open loop and was not run in the closed loop, which changes only the operator. The population, the scores and the task stay fixed. No free-rewrite run beats the best generation-zero member. Every tactic-append run and every template-append run does. Sampled loop children keep that split. Free rewrite moves no further toward the donor, and both appends still clear the bound, by less than in the open loop.

</details>


### [58] [GRAIL: From Saliency to Semantics in Time-Series Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29036)
*Enver Menadjiev,Yiqiu Shen,Jaesik Choi,Julia Stoyanovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GRAIL：以领域区间（如 ECG 的 P 波、QRS 波群）为博弈参与者、带可读特征与流形上反事实基线、并用 Owen 值（含公共随机数方差缩减）归因的区域级时间序列解释框架，可同时纳入静态协变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列模型解释需既定位证据又提取语义知识，但单个时间步并非语义单元：有意义的模式跨多个样本存在。逐时间步显著性因而噪声大、维度高且不稳定。已有方法用固定窗口作为独立参与者，并用固定反事实（平直线、噪声或谱替代）定义“基线”区域，这一选择常主导解释结果，掩盖模型真实决策；同时难以把静态协变量与信号证据放在同一尺度比较。

Method: 1）以领域特定区间（如 ECG 心搏的 P 波、QRS 波群）为博弈参与者；2）每个区域用人类可读特征描述；3）用流形上的条件生成器产生反事实基线，替代固定反事实；4）静态协变量作为单元素参与者，与时间区域在同一尺度上归因（如年龄、性别等表格证据直接与信号证据相比较）；5）通过 Owen 值进行特征归因，并采用公共随机数做方差缩减。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据上实验表明，该框架产生忠实且稳健的解释；评估在匹配的流形上/流形外算子下进行，并能为领域专家提供有意义的知识。

Conclusion: 以语义化领域区间为参与者、配合流形上条件基线生成，能缓解逐时间步显著性的噪声与不稳定，以及固定反事实对解释的主导性；Owen 值归因使时间区域与静态协变量可比，整体提升了时间序列解释的忠实性、稳健性与对领域专家的可解释价值。

Abstract: Explaining a time-series model requires both locating the evidence and extracting meaningful knowledge from it. Yet a single timestep is not a semantic unit: the meaningful patterns live across many samples, not in any one of them. Per-timestep saliency is therefore noisy, high-dimensional, and unstable. Existing methods address this by introducing fixed-size windows and treating them as independent players in a cooperative game. Evaluating a coalition of such players requires defining a ``baseline'' region, which prior work tackles with fixed counterfactuals (e.g., a flat line, noise, or a spectral surrogate). This choice frequently dominates the resulting explanation, obscuring the model's true decisions.
  To address these issues, we propose GRAIL, a region-based time-series explanation framework that uses domain-specific intervals, such as the P-wave and the QRS complex of an electrocardiogram (ECG) beat, as players. We describe each region with human-readable features and generate counterfactual baselines with an on-manifold conditional generator. Such a choice of players also admits non-temporal features: static covariates enter as singleton players attributed with the temporal regions on one scale, so a patient's tabular evidence (e.g., age, sex) is weighed directly against the signal. We attribute the predictions to the features via Owen values with common-random-number variance reduction. Experimental results on both synthetic and real data show that the proposed framework produces faithful and robust explanations, evaluated under matched on- and off-manifold operators, offering meaningful knowledge to domain experts.

</details>


### [59] [AutoFraming: Synthesizing Dynamic Frames of C Code with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29046)
*Han Zhou,Yu Luo,Dianxiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的方法，利用代码属性图（CPG）提取frame相关信息，从C代码同时合成ACSL frame规范与功能契约，显著提升frame条件生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM合成形式化规范的方法主要关注功能契约与循环不变式，忽视了frame条件（描述执行期间可/不可修改的内存位置），而frame条件是形式化规范的重要组成部分。

Method: 分析代码属性图（CPG）以从输入代码中提取与frame相关的信息，并用该信息引导LLM生成准确的frame条件，所得规范同时捕获区域分离约束与支配变量修改的数据依赖。

Result: 在含84个ACSL兼容函数的真实系统基准上，92.9%的函数成功合成无错frame规范，相较SOTA相对提升179%；在51个函数的第二基准上成功率由80.4%提升至96.1%；作为辅助组件集成到现有LLM-based ACSL合成框架中，frame规范生成性能最高提升61.9%。

Conclusion: 通过CPG引导LLM可有效弥补frame条件合成的空白，显著优于现有技术，且可作为插件增强已有ACSL合成框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown considerable promise in synthesizing formal specifications from source code. However, existing approaches primarily focus on functional contracts and loop invariants, while largely neglecting frame conditions, which specify the memory locations that may or may not be modified during execution and constitute an essential part of formal specifications. To address this limitation, we propose an LLM-based approach for synthesizing ACSL frame specifications alongside functional contracts from C code. Our approach analyzes code property graphs (CPGs) to extract frame-relevant information from the input code and leverages this information to guide the LLM in generating accurate frame conditions. The resulting specifications capture both region-separation constraints and data dependencies that govern variable modifications. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. On a benchmark of 84 ACSL-compliant functions from non-trivial real-world systems, our method successfully synthesizes error-free frame specifications for 92.9% of the functions, representing a 179% relative improvement over state-of-the-art techniques. On a second benchmark consisting of 51 functions, the success rate increases from 80.4% to 96.1%. Furthermore, when integrated as an auxiliary component into existing LLM-based ACSL-synthesis frameworks, our approach improves frame-specification generation performance by up to 61.9%.

</details>


### [60] [Does Every Instance Count Equally? Order Effects in LLM Multi-Instance Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29057)
*Jingxuan Chen,Mohammad Taher Pilehvar,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM在多实例处理（MIP）任务中的实例顺序敏感性：在需聚合多个独立实例信息并输出单一预测的设定下，模型答案本应对顺序不变，但实证发现顺序会带来约90个百分点的准确率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越多地让LLM分析一组独立输入（如商品评论、社交媒体帖子）并给出单一聚合答案，这属于多实例处理（MIP）。这类任务的正确答案理论上应与实例顺序无关，但模型是否真的顺序不变尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 设计任务：为每个实例推断二值标签，并识别整个实例集中出现最频繁的标签。在15个LLM、5个数据集、7种实例顺序模式上评估，且同一实例集在各顺序模式间保持固定以控制内容差异；辅以推理轨迹分析和基于梯度的归因分析。

Result: 存在显著的顺序敏感性：最佳与最差顺序模式间准确率可相差约90个百分点；多数标签实例出现在开头时模型更准确，出现在中部或末尾时更差。推理轨迹过度提及靠前实例；梯度归因分数在开头和末尾高于中部，显示模型对不同位置强调不均。

Conclusion: 实例顺序会系统性偏置聚合预测，因此MIP评估必须在多种输入排列下进行，而不能仅在单一顺序下报告性能。

Abstract: Users increasingly rely on large language models (LLMs) to analyse collections of independent inputs, such as product reviews or social media posts, and return a single aggregated answer. This setting requires multi-instance processing (MIP), where a model combines information from multiple instances into one prediction. We study a task in which the model must infer a binary label for each instance, and identify the most frequent label among the full instance set. The correct answer should be invariant to instance order. By evaluating fifteen LLMs across five datasets and seven instance order patterns, with each instance set held fixed across patterns, we find substantial order sensitivity. Accuracy can differ by about 90 percentage points between the best and worst order patterns, with models generally more accurate when majority-label instances appear at the start than when they appear in the middle or at the end. For subsets of models, available reasoning traces and gradient-based attribution reveal uneven emphasis across positions. Traces disproportionately mention early instances, while attribution scores are generally higher near the beginning and end than in the middle. These results show that instance order can systematically bias aggregated predictions, highlighting the need to evaluate MIP across different input arrangements.

</details>


### [61] [Context-Augmented, Session-Calibrated Blood Pressure Tracking from Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29091)
*Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi,Timothy Oladunni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出带显式对照的校准评估框架，区分采集情境与PPG对会话校准血压追踪的贡献，并保留阴性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清可穿戴血压追踪中采集情境（context）与PPG信号各自的真实贡献，避免因重复使用数据集而高估额外信号或复杂特征的增益，同时保留可复现的对照。

Method: 在AuroraBP 653名受试者上做三次重复的五折受试者级交叉验证（排除校准标签预测目标）；用配对的因子分析分离情境与PPG并剔除时间项；探索性随访保留PPG、加速度信号与三次初始袖带读数；另设脉搏率融合研究与术中VitalDB模型。

Result: 原始可穿戴状态模型SBP/DBP MAE为7.697/6.139 mmHg；加入情境使MAE降低0.436/0.126 mmHg。无外部PPG先验的配对因子中，PPG相对校准、时间及姿态/活动改善0.214/0.135 mmHg，但去时间后DBP区间含零。传感器历史集成7.299/5.445 vs 参考7.363/5.470，增益仅0.064/0.025 mmHg。质量加权融合使脉搏率MAE由3.985降至3.690 bpm。VitalDB MAE仍为15.375/8.833 mmHg。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个可复现、含显式对照与阴性发现的校准评估框架；传感器历史增益属数据集重复使用后的探索性结果；独立估计的可穿戴状态、所提供富特征的原始信号完全复现、外部迁移与临床充分性仍待确立。

Abstract: Objective.To distinguish the contributions of acquisition context and photoplethysmography (PPG) to session-calibrated blood pressure (BP) tracking.Approach. The original comparison used three repeats of five-fold participant-level cross-validation in 653 AuroraBP participants, excluding calibration-labelled prediction targets. Matched analyses separated context and PPG and removed elapsed-time terms. Later exploratory follow-ups retained PPG, accelerometer signals and three initial cuff readings. Separate studies evaluated pulse-rate fusion and an intraoperative VitalDB model. Main results.The original wearable-state model achieved participant-level mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 7.697/6.139 mmHg for systolic/diastolic BP (SBP/DBP). Added context reduced MAE by 0.436/0.126 mmHg (97.5% intervals 0.305-0.573/0.053-0.200). In the matched factorial without the external PPG prior, PPG improved on calibration, time and protocol-provided posture/activity by 0.214/0.135 mmHg; removing time left the DBP interval including zero. Standard PPG descriptors contributed, but additional nonlinear/attractor benefit was not established. In a separate follow-up series, a sensor-history ensemble achieved 7.299/5.445 mmHg versus its static-rich reference's 7.363/5.470, small gains of 0.064/0.025 mmHg. Larger DBP errors did not improve uniformly. Quality-weighted fusion reduced pulse-rate MAE from 3.985 to 3.690 beats per minute. VitalDB MAEs remained 15.375/8.833 mmHg despite small PPG gains. Significance. The contribution is a calibrated evaluation framework with explicit controls, reproducible feature-cache-to-results analyses and retained negative findings. The sensor-history gain is exploratory after repeated dataset reuse. Independently estimated wearable states, complete raw-signal reproduction of the supplied richer features, external transfer and clinical adequacy remain unestablished.

</details>


### [62] [Same Answer, Different Reasons: Trace-Level Divergence in Multilingual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29092)
*Rui Sun,Peizhao Mei,Xiewei Cheng,Kaizhuo Chen,Taoran Li,Zhiyuan Yu,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"迹级（trace-level）分歧"框架，将多语言大模型可见推理迹视为结构化序列比较，分解为结构相似性、顺序一致性与整体相似性，并做配对校准。实验表明常规整体嵌入余弦相似度会系统性高估跨语言一致性，人类判断更贴近结构相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型的可见推理迹被用于验证、教育与安全审计，但标准评测只测最终答案一致性或整体响应相似度，只能说明"结果一致"，无法判断不同语言用户是否获得可比的支撑证据。因此需要迹级评测来揭示解释性内容的一致性。

Method: 构建迹级分歧框架：把可见推理迹作为结构化序列比较，将一致性分解为结构相似性、顺序一致性和整体相似性，并采用配对专属（pair-specific）校准以支持跨语言比较。实验覆盖3个推理基准、7种语言、6个前沿LLM；以"整段响应嵌入+余弦相似度"为自然基线；辅以多种句子编码器与步骤切分方式的稳健性检验，以及两组以英语为锚的盲标注双语人工研究，并设置同语言对照。

Result: 整体相似度top四分位样本中，有52.1%–67.3%并不在结构相似性top四分位内，说明整体嵌入相似度系统性高估一致性；该解离在句子编码器与步骤切分变化下稳定；盲标注双语人工研究显示人类判断与结构相似性一致而非整体基线。同语言对照表明分歧程度具有语言特异性：同一问题两个英语改写迹之间的吻合度高于英语迹与其他语言对应迹。

Conclusion: 答案级与整体式评测会显著高估多语言解释性产物的一致性，需采用迹级评测以保障多语言部署的可靠性。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where visible reasoning traces support verification, education, and safety auditing, yet standard evaluations typically measure only final-answer agreement or holistic response similarity. As a result, they establish outcome consistency without determining whether users across languages receive comparable supporting evidence. We introduce a trace-level divergence framework that compares visible reasoning traces as structured sequences, decomposing agreement into structural similarity, order consistency, and holistic similarity, with pair-specific calibration to enable comparison across languages. Across three reasoning benchmarks, seven languages, and six frontier LLMs, the natural baseline---embedding each whole response and comparing the two by cosine---systematically overstates agreement: $52.1\%$--$67.3\%$ of examples in the top quartile of holistic similarity are not in the top quartile of structural similarity. This dissociation is stable across sentence encoders and step segmentations, and blind bilingual annotation studies in two English-anchored language pairs show that human judgments align with structural similarity rather than with the holistic baseline. A same-language control shows that the level of divergence is language-specific: two English traces for paraphrases of the same question agree more closely than an English trace and its counterparts in other languages. Our results show that answer-level and holistic evaluations can substantially overestimate the consistency of multilingual explanatory artifacts, motivating trace-level evaluation for reliable multilingual deployment.

</details>


### [63] [GRAM: Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging via Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29110)
*Di Wu,Shuaidong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GRAM：利用任务向量间的**主角（principal angle）几何**进行分组，再在组内做标准 SVD 合并，仅需微调权重即可缓解"子空间干扰"，在 K=2 时即显著优于现有合并方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务部署需将多个（尤其在医疗等受监管领域）本地微调的模型合并为单一模型，且受隐私约束只能基于微调权重进行聚合。现有方法对所有任务施加**统一合并**，忽略各任务学习子空间的几何关系；当任务更新落在失配子空间时，将其投影到共享低秩基会丢弃任务相关方向，即"子空间干扰"（subspace interference）。

Method: 1）理论分析：证明合并模型的**逐任务投影误差**由各任务与共享子空间之间的**主角对齐度**决定，因而将几何兼容的任务分组可证明地降低组内误差；2）据此提出 GRAM（Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging）：以任务向量（task vectors）的主角几何作为分组依据决定哪些任务共同合并，组内采用标准 SVD 低秩合并；3）流程在合并阶段仅需微调权重，无需训练数据。

Result: 在仅引入一个额外模型（K=2）的设置下，GRAM 显著缓解子空间干扰，并在语言理解（GLUE + Flan-T5）、8 任务 CLIP 视觉、以及医疗指令微调（Qwen2-7B-Instruct + 四个中英文医疗 LoRA）上一致优于已有合并方法。

Conclusion: 以主角几何为分组准则的分组式、秩感知合并（GRAM）为隐私受限场景下的多任务模型合并提供了理论与实证支持的有效方案，且开销小（仅需一个额外模型），具备跨语言、跨模态与医疗等受监管领域的实用性。

Abstract: Merging different models into a single model is highly desirable for multi-task deployment, particularly in regulated domains like healthcare. In such settings, models are often locally fine-tuned due to strict data privacy policies, necessitating that aggregation operates exclusively on the fine-tuned weights. Existing methods apply a uniform merge across all tasks, ignoring the geometric relationships among their learned subspaces. When task updates occupy misaligned subspaces, projecting them onto a shared low-rank basis discards task-relevant directions, a failure mode named \emph{subspace interference}. Through this study, we show that the per-task projection error of a merged model is governed by the principal-angle alignment between each task and the shared subspace, so that grouping geometrically compatible tasks provably reduces this error within each group. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging (GRAM)}, which uses the principal-angle geometry of task vectors to decide which tasks should be merged together and then performs a standard SVD-based merge within each coalition; the pipeline requires only the fine-tuned weights at merge time. With only one additional model ($\nK{=}2$), GRAM significantly mitigates subspace interference and consistently outperforms prior merging methods across language understanding (GLUE with Flan-T5), 8-task CLIP vision, and medical instruction tuning (Qwen2-7B-Instruct with four English/Chinese medical LoRAs).

</details>


### [64] [Recursive Self-Improvement on a Budget: Scoring Code Edits Before They Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29121)
*Erfan Darzi,Sree Bhargavi Balija,Sheng He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究自改写脚手架（self-rewriting scaffold）编码代理中"先选编辑、后评估"的选择难题：在固定预算下用线性读出对父节点solve率与diff坐标做打分并冻结系数，于HumanEval+/Polyglot的steering划分上做选择，最终返回held-out上表现最佳的节点；GPT-4o下达87.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理可重写自身脚手架，但必须在编辑被评估之前决定展开哪个提议编辑，且搜索最终只能返回一个代理。需在有限评估预算（96次脚手架编辑、每次4次16实例fit划分评估）下设计一种在未见数据上稳健的选择策略，并与父节点solve率基线、校准最优编辑等对照。

Method: 每次运行在父节点steering划分solve率与提议diff的8个坐标上拟合一个线性读出，使用96个脚手架编辑、由4次16实例fit划分评估打标签，然后冻结系数。HumanEval+保留48实例为steering划分，Polyglot保留64。该划分对父节点打分并维护四节点档案。Huxley-Gödel Machine选择通过对已评估节点的steering计数做Thompson采样抽取父节点，再展开其提议的子节点。各方法最终返回steering-split solve计数最大的最早被评估节点。

Result: 在hold-out上，GPT-4o的12次运行返回节点在HumanEval+剩余100实例上达87.0%，高于父节点solve率消融的84.2%；同划分下Huxley-Gödel Machine选择为85.1%，根脚手架81.2%，校准最优编辑82.8%。Claude Sonnet 4.5上对应为86.6%，HGM选择86.5%，父节点消融85.3%，校准最优编辑85.1%。

Conclusion: 基于冻结线性读出对父节点接口进行选择，在两种模型与两套基准上均优于根脚手架、校准最优编辑和父节点solve率消融，表明该方法能有效在评估前筛选提议编辑并返回更优代理；但HGM选择在部分设置下略低，说明选择策略尚有改进空间。

Abstract: A coding agent that rewrites its own scaffold has to choose which proposed edit to expand before that edit has been evaluated, and the search then has to return one agent. Each run fits one linear readout on the parent's steering-split solve rate and eight coordinates of the proposed diff, from 96 scaffold edits labeled by four evaluations of a 16-instance fit split, and then freezes the coefficients. HumanEval+ reserves 48 instances as the steering split and Polyglot reserves 64. That split scores parents and keeps an archive of four nodes. Huxley-Gödel Machine selection draws a parent by Thompson sampling on the steering counts of nodes that have already been evaluated, and it expands the child that parent proposes. Every method then returns the earliest evaluated node whose steering-split solve count is maximal. The tables report the held-out score of that node on the other 100 HumanEval+ instances and the other 145 Polyglot instances, next to the root scaffold and the calibration edit with the highest fit-split label. Twelve runs of GPT-4o return a node at 87.0% on held-out HumanEval+, against 84.2% for the parent-rate ablation. On that split Huxley-Gödel Machine selection returns 85.1%, the root is at 81.2%, and the calibration-best edit is at 82.8%. On the same split Claude Sonnet 4.5 returns 86.6%, beside 86.5% for Huxley-Gödel Machine selection, 85.3% for the parent-rate ablation, and 85.1% for the calibration-best edit.

</details>


### [65] [StochIKM: Automated High-Fidelity Reconstruction of Individual Patient Data from Published Kaplan-Meier Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29122)
*Liangze Ke,Daniel Zelterman,Wei Wei,Guannan Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 StochIKM：用视觉语言模型数字化 Kaplan-Meier 图，并以显式"高保真"约束（精确复现风险表、贴合曲线至图像分辨率、符合中位数/区间/事件数）作为条件，随机生成可接受（admissible）的个体患者数据集集合，而非单一数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存证据二次分析的黄金标准是个体患者数据（IPD），但原始数据极少公开，从已发表 KM 图重建是可行替代。现有方法各自宣称高保真，却只用自己恰好达成的某几项一致性来评价，缺乏统一的保真度定义；且图到数据的映射非双射，单数据集输出掩盖了不确定性。

Method: 将保真度显式定义为约束：数据集"可接受"当且仅当精确复现风险表、数字化曲线误差在图像分辨率内、并满足印刷的中位数、区间与事件数。StochIKM 采用随机算法，结合视觉语言模型完成图形数字化，据此求解满足约束的个体患者数据集集合；只要图被正确读取，解的存在性有保证，失败即指向原图或数字化环节的问题。

Result: 在仿真实验及 79 个已发表肿瘤试验组中，StochIKM 复现已发表风险表与中位数的精度优于 LLM 改进版 IPDfromKM 这一高影响力替代方法。

Conclusion: 以显式可接受性约束取代模糊的"高保真"宣称，可提供可验证、可诊断的重建范式；多解集合形式诚实地反映非双射映射带来的不确定性。

Abstract: Individual patient data are the gold standard for secondary analysis of time-to-event evidence, but they are rarely released, and reconstruction from a published Kaplan-Meier figure could help. Existing methods all claim high fidelity, each judged by whichever agreements it happens to achieve, and each returns a single dataset although the map from figure to data is not bijective. We define fidelity explicitly: a dataset is admissible when it reproduces the risk table exactly, follows the digitized curve to within the image resolution, and respects the printed median, interval and event count. We propose StochIKM, a stochastic algorithm that digitizes the figure with a vision-capable language model and uses the high-fidelity definition as constraints, returning an admissible set of individual patient datasets. A solution is guaranteed to exist whenever the figure has been read correctly, so a failure indicts either the published figure or its digitization. In simulation and in 79 arms from published oncology trials, StochIKM reproduces the published risk tables and medians more closely than LLM-improved IPDfromKM, a high-impact alternative.

</details>


### [66] [NormTune: Norm-Guided Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Reasoning-Intensive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29134)
*Zhangming Li,Qinghao Hu,Weifan Guan,Yifan Zhang,Peisong Wang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现PEFT在推理密集任务上与全参数微调存在性能差距，该差距与"权重更新范数不足"（norm-related under-adaptation）密切相关，据此提出NormTune——按权重更新范数自适应增强更新不足的层，从而显著提升多种PEFT方法在推理基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调（full fine-tuning）在推理密集任务上效果好但GPU开销巨大；PEFT虽能降本，却在推理密集任务上存在明显性能差距。作者希望通过诊断该差距的成因，缩小PEFT与全参数微调的鸿沟。

Method: 通过三项关键观察指出PEFT的权重更新范数显著小于全参数微调，即norm-related under-adaptation是差距主因；据此提出NormTune：依据各层权重更新范数，自适应地对更新不足的层进行增强。该方法为通用策略，可即插即用地集成到LoRA、Bone、MoRA等多种PEFT方法中。

Result: 在S1K-1.1上训练：rank-8 LoRA、Bone、MoRA结合NormTune后在AIME 2024、MATH 500、GPQA、AIME 2025上平均准确率分别提升24.24%、11.33%、16.21%；rank-256下三者分别比S1全参数微调高2.17%、0.93%、3.00%，达到可比甚至更优性能。

Conclusion: PEFT在推理任务上的性能差距主要源于权重更新范数的欠适配；按范数引导的自适应增强（NormTune）是一种简单有效、方法无关的改进方案，可使PEFT在推理密集任务上逼近甚至超越全参数微调。

Abstract: Full fine-tuning has achieved remarkable success on reasoning-intensive tasks, but it often requires substantial GPU resource consumption. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) provides a promising alternative for reducing this cost, yet we observe a substantial performance gap on reasoning-intensive tasks.
