In this paper, we present three key observations showing that this gap is closely associated with norm-related under-adaptation, where PEFT produces markedly smaller weight-update norms than full fine-tuning. Based on these observations, we propose Norm-Guided Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (NormTune), a simple yet effective strategy that adaptively strengthens insufficiently updated layers according to their weight-update norms. Extensive experiments show that NormTune consistently enhances multiple PEFT methods across challenging reasoning benchmarks. Specifically, when trained on S1K-1.1, integrating NormTune into rank-8 LoRA, Bone, and MoRA yields substantial average accuracy improvements of 24.24\%, 11.33\%, and 16.21\% , respectively, across AIME 2024, MATH 500, GPQA, and AIME 2025. Moreover, NormTune-enhanced rank-256 LoRA, Bone, and MoRA achieve performance comparable to S1 full fine-tuning, with respective average gains of 2.17\%, 0.93\%, and 3.00\% over this baseline.

</details>


### [67] [LaVGuard: Selective Decoding for Contextual Value Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29135)
*Jiale Dai,Liuxian Ma,Hongcan Deng,Xiaoke Niu,Wenjing Zhang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Guojie Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LaVGuard，将"上下文价值漂移"建模为带显式变更允许量的任务相关解码问题：用十维授权价值画像与可见前缀价值估计的密度门控选择介入时机，并以签名进展驱动受位置变更与累积KL预算约束的logit倾斜，在可证局部改进与路径预算可行的同时提升边界TPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容安全的回复仍可能推动与任务相冲突的价值取向（如被要求维护自主性时反而要求服从），现有方法缺乏对"允许变更幅度"的显式刻画与可控介入。

Method: LaVGuard将授权十维价值画像与冻结的可见前缀价值估计分离；以画像相对密度门控决定何时评估候选续写；由候选续写的签名进展计算logit倾斜，并同时受变更位置预算与累积KL预算约束。

Result: 5% FPR下边界TPR由.58提升至.72；在相同0.12平均介入比例下，选择研究给出LaVGuard风险安全分.88，位置匹配编辑为.72；并提供局部代理改进、路径预算可行性与响应分布变化上界的理论保证。

Conclusion: 该接口使任务优先级可操作，同时使介入程度显式且有界，为上下文价值漂移提供可审计的解码级控制框架。

Abstract: A content-safe response can still promote priorities that conflict with its task: an assistant may demand compliance when asked to preserve autonomy. We study contextual value drift as a task-relative decoding problem with an explicit allowance for change. LaVGuard separates an authorized ten-dimensional profile from a frozen estimate of the values expressed in the visible prefix. A profile-relative density gate selects when to evaluate candidate continuations; their signed progress determines a logit tilt constrained by both changed-position and cumulative-KL budgets. We establish local surrogate improvement, pathwise budget feasibility, and a bound on response-distribution change. Profile conditioning raises boundary TPR at 5% FPR from .58 to .72. At the same .12 mean intervention ratio, the selection study reports risk-safety scores of .88 for LaVGuard and .72 for position-matched edits. The resulting interface makes task priorities actionable while keeping the extent of intervention explicit and bounded.

</details>


### [68] [Steering Black-Box Large Language Model Agents with Pitfall Anticipation World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29139)
*Jiongxiao Wang,Han Ding,Youzhi Luo,Yawei Wang,Yun Zhou,Linbo Liu,Panpan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Pitfall Anticipation World Model (PAWM)，一种可选择性介入的世界模型：仅在潜在陷阱出现时预测即将发生的失败并引导黑盒 LLM 智能体规避，通过 SFT + GRPO 两阶段训练，使 Claude Sonnet 4.5 在 ScienceWorld 提升 24.3%、WebArena-Lite 提升 7.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿黑盒 LLM 智能体擅长长程推理与精确动作生成，但缺乏对特定任务环境的内部化知识。世界模型可提供环境特定知识，然而现有面向 LLM 智能体的世界模型逐步预测下一观测，代价高且信息量有限：单步观测对任务指导有限，且并非每一步都需要前瞻。因此核心问题是：面向智能体任务，世界模型应预测什么、何时调用。作者的前期研究（pilot study）表明，当世界模型在有界多步未来内预见陷阱时，其引导最有效。

Method: PAWM 采用选择性介入机制：仅在潜在陷阱出现时介入，预测即将发生的失败以引导智能体避开。训练分两阶段：1) 在标注了智能体陷阱的合成数据上做监督微调（SFT）；2) 与黑盒 LLM 在目标环境中进行 GRPO 协同训练（co-training），进一步优化。

Result: 相比多种基线，PAWM 将 Claude Sonnet 4.5 的性能在 ScienceWorld 上提升 24.3%，在 WebArena-Lite 上提升 7.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明 PAWM 为黑盒 LLM 智能体提供了一种有效的世界模型引导形式，即"预测有界未来内的陷阱"而非逐步预测观测，是更具性价比与信息量的设计范式。

Abstract: Frontier black-box Large Language Model (LLM) agents are proficient at long-horizon reasoning and precise action generation. However, when it comes to a specific agentic task, they have no internalized knowledge of how that particular environment unfolds. A world model offers a natural remedy, providing the agent with environment specific knowledge by foreseeing how the environment will respond to the action it is about to take. Prior world models for LLM agents mainly predict next observations at each step, which is costly and often uninformative because individual observations provide limited task-relevant guidance and not every step requires call for foresight. This raises a key question: what a world model should predict and when should it be invoked for agentic tasks? Our pilot study suggests that world model guidance is most useful when it anticipates pitfalls within a bounded multi-step future. Motivated by this, we introduce Pitfall Anticipation World Model (PAWM), which selectively intervenes when a potential pitfall arises and predicts the impending failure to steer the agent away from it. PAWM is trained in two stages: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) on synthetic data labeled with agent pitfalls, followed by GRPO co-training with a black-box LLM to further optimize it in the target environment. PAWM improves Claude Sonnet 4.5 performance by 24.3% on ScienceWorld and 7.7% on WebArena-Lite, outperforming a range of baselines. These results demonstrate that PAWM provides an effective form of world model guidance for black-box LLM agents.

</details>


### [69] [TeachBack: Learning to Teach via Bidirectional Preference Optimization for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29167)
*Linfei Ji,Jiayun Li,Haozhao Wang,Siyu Jiang,Zehao Wang,Longtao Xiao,Yu-Jie Yuan,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TeachBack——一种用于on-policy自蒸馏的双向偏好学习框架，不仅学习教师产出的轨迹，还学习"教师应如何教学"，通过控制参考推理后缀长度与提示风格来塑造教师行为，从而提升学生模型在数学和逻辑推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD中，教师模型借助特权信息（如参考解答）可生成高准确率轨迹，但准确率不等于适合学生学习：直接接触完整解答会使教师产生"被参考条件化"的简短推导，省略了无特权信息时所需的探索、验证与自我纠错过程。

Method: 在每次rollout刷新时，共享策略同时生成带特权的教师轨迹与仅含问题的学生轨迹。采用成对偏好目标：用特权教师轨迹提升学生学习；再用反向偏好以学生行为来塑造未来的教师监督。同时通过控制注入提示的参考推理后缀长度与变化提示风格，在保持教师准确率相当的前提下促使教师探索并重新求解问题、降低对参考解答的依赖。

Result: 在数学与逻辑推理任务的实验中，TeachBack相对现有OPSD方法及相关基线持续提升学生性能。

Conclusion: 教师的高准确率并不保证其轨迹适合学生学习；通过双向偏好学习调节教师的"教学方式"、减少对参考解答的依赖，可稳定提升on-policy自蒸馏中学生模型的效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) enables a language model to learn from a Teacher instantiated from the same model but given access to privileged information, such as a reference solution. We find that access to sufficient privileged information allows the Teacher to generate highly accurate trajectories; however, accuracy alone does not guarantee that these trajectories are suitable for Student learning. Direct access to a complete solution may encourage shortened, reference-conditioned derivations that omit the exploration, verification, and self-correction required to solve problems without privileged information. We introduce TeachBack, a bidirectional preference-learning framework for on-policy self-distillation that learns not only from the Teacher but also how the Teacher should teach. TeachBack shapes the Teacher's generation behavior by controlling the length of the reference-reasoning suffix injected into the prompt and by varying the prompt style. While maintaining comparable Teacher accuracy, it encourages the Teacher to explore and re-solve problems, thereby reducing its reliance on the reference solution. At each rollout refresh, the shared policy generates both a privileged Teacher trajectory and a problem-only Student trajectory. A pairwise preference objective uses privileged Teacher trajectories to improve Student learning, while the reversed preference uses the Student's behavior to shape future Teacher supervision under the privileged condition. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning tasks show that TeachBack consistently improves Student performance over existing OPSD methods and related baselines.

</details>


### [70] [When Multiple Choices Are Correct: Asymmetric Probability Allocation in Jev and Other Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29217)
*Rushab Munot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估三种决策模型（Jev、Liquid d1、GPT-6 Luna Decisions）在三个数据集（MetaTool、HotpotQA、ECtHR）上的概率分配与阈值化行为，发现模型即便正确排序全部正解，仍在28%–50%的列表中给某些正解低于均匀概率1/n的概率，且对同一正解的五个相同副本给出17–79个百分点的概率落差，导致概率阈值无法识别正解集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型提供方强调输出概率的可靠性，但现有校准评估仅限于"单一正确选项"任务，无法保证在存在多个正确选项时概率仍然可靠，因此需要系统研究多正确选项情形下的概率分配与阈值化表现。

Method: 在MetaTool、HotpotQA、ECtHR三个数据集上评测Jev、Liquid d1、GPT-6 Luna Decisions三种决策模型；分析其概率分配与排序质量，统计正解概率低于1/n的比例；向模型提供同一正解的五个完全相同的副本以测量概率差异；并考察在上述分配差异下概率阈值化对多正确选项召回的影响。

Result: 即使模型将所有正解排在所有干扰项之上，仍有28%–50%的列表中至少一个正解的概率低于均匀概率1/n；对五个相同正确选项副本，模型给出的概率存在显著落差，平均差距达17–79个百分点；这些分配差异进一步损害了基于概率阈值恢复多个正确选项的效果。

Conclusion: 决策模型输出的概率可用于对正确选项进行排序，但单一概率阈值无法识别正确选项集合；提供方所宣称的概率可靠性在多正确选项场景下并不成立，下游应用不应直接以概率阈值作为选择依据。

Abstract: Decision models return normalized probability distributions over candidate choices, which downstream applications can use to select among choices. Although their providers emphasize the reliability of these probabilities, calibration on tasks with a single correct choice does not establish their reliability when multiple choices are correct. We evaluate three decision models (Jev, Liquid d1, and GPT-6 Luna Decisions) on three diverse datasets (MetaTool, HotpotQA, and ECtHR) to study probability allocation and thresholding. Even when models rank every correct choice above every distractor, in 28% to 50% of those lists, at least one correct choice receives a probability below the uniform probability of 1/n, where n is the number of choices. We also present each model with five identical copies of a correct choice and observe probability disparities, with mean gaps ranging from 17 to 79 percentage points. We further show how these allocation disparities affect the recovery of multiple correct choices under probability thresholding. Decision-model probabilities can rank correct choices, but a probability threshold does not identify the set of correct choices.

</details>


### [71] [From Natural Language Intent to Executable Control: Hierarchical Feedback-Guided LLM Planning for Multi-component Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22441)
*Jinglin Dai,Jiawan Wang,Ruiqi Hong,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层反馈框架，用不同层次反馈引导LLM为信息物理系统(CPS)生成可执行任务计划与可行控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可将自然语言指令转为CPS任务计划，但存在两大障碍：生成计划可能误解或遗漏用户意图；即使逻辑合理的计划在组件动力学、交互或安全约束下也可能不可行。既有LLM规划方法缺乏"失败原因"的解释性反馈，难以指导修正。

Method: 让LLM同时生成结构化任务计划与对应STL约束，设计可靠性(sound)算法识别二者间的确定矛盾并反馈以修正不一致；在执行层，当控制合成失败时提取多类诊断信息（违反的约束、冲突的组件分配等）回馈给LLM进行重规划。

Result: 在7个异构多组件CPS场景上，框架能生成可执行计划与可行控制执行，满足原始自然语言任务，GPT-5下成功率达87.9%。

Conclusion: 分层、多类型反馈可弥补LLM规划中意图对齐与可执行性验证的缺口，是实现CPS端到端可执行规划的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a convenient interface for translating natural language commands into task plans for cyber-physical systems (CPSs). However, two gaps hinder their use for real execution: generated plans may misinterpret or omit the user's intent, and even a logically plausible plan may be infeasible under component dynamics, interactions, or safety constraints. Existing LLM-based planning approaches often lack informative feedback on why a generated plan fails, providing little guidance for revising the plan accordingly. To address these challenges, we present a hierarchical feedback framework that uses different types of feedback information to guide LLMs in generating executable plans for CPSs. We prompt LLMs to generate both a structured task plan and corresponding signal temporal logic (STL) constraints, and design a sound algorithm that identifies definite contradictions between them and provides feedback for revising inconsistencies. At the execution level, when control synthesis fails, we extract different types of diagnostic information, such as violated constraints and conflicting component assignments, and feed them back to guide task replanning. Experiments on seven heterogeneous multi-component CPS scenarios have shown that our framework can generate executable plans and feasible control executions that satisfy the original natural language missions with a success rate of 87.9% with GPT-5.

</details>


### [72] [From Scores to Safe Sets: Exact Safety--Constrained Decoding for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29228)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Anushka Swarup,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定FLAME分类器分数、输出规模与DDI约束的前提下，比较局部修复与精确全局解码（MILP），发现全局解码在更紧的DDI约束下Jaccard更高，说明“如何把分数转化为受约束的药物集合”本身会显著影响预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式用药推荐最终输出药物集合，而DDI约束依赖于整体集合而非单个药物，常见的做法是先预测再对冲突药物对做局部修复（逐个移除）。作者质疑这一局部修复步骤本身是否引入了可避免的误差。

Method: 在MIMIC-III上使用FLAME冻结分类器给出的固定药物分数，保持分数、输出规模和DDI约束不变，对比逐级增强的局部修复策略（贪心修复、度感知修复、度感知+穷举1-swap局部搜索）与全局解码器；全局解码器用混合整数线性规划（MILP）联合选取最高分的可行药物集合。并在精度—DDI权衡扫描及三种分数表示下复验。

Result: 在验证集与测试集上，全局解码的Jaccard均稳定高于各局部基线；优势在宽松DDI约束下较小，随约束收紧而增大，测试集在严格DDI工作点比最强局部基线高+0.0064 Jaccard。结论在匹配实际DDI暴露的精细精度—DDI扫描与三种分数表示下均成立；严格设置下全局解码保留更多真阳性并去除更多假阳性。

Conclusion: 用药推荐不只是表示学习问题：即使临床模型冻结，其分数如何被转换为受约束的药物集合也会实质性影响最终预测，因此全局约束解码应作为独立且重要的设计环节。

Abstract: Recent generative medication recommendation systems ultimately produce sets of drugs, but drug--drug interaction (DDI) constraints depend on the set as a whole. A natural post-hoc approach is to first predict medications and then locally repair interacting pairs by removing one drug at a time. We ask whether this local repair step itself introduces avoidable error. Using fixed medication scores from FLAME's frozen classifier on MIMIC-III, we compare increasingly strong local repair strategies with an exact global decoder while holding the scores, output size, and DDI constraint fixed. The global decoder jointly selects the highest-scoring feasible medication set using mixed-integer linear programming. Across validation and test splits, global decoding consistently achieves higher Jaccard similarity than greedy repair, degree-aware repair, and degree-aware repair strengthened with exhaustive 1-swap local search. Its advantage is small under loose DDI constraints and becomes larger as the constraint tightens; on the test split it reaches +0.0064 Jaccard over the strongest local baseline at the strict DDI operating point. The finding also holds across a fine accuracy--DDI sweep at matched realized DDI exposure and under three score representations. At the strict setting, global decoding retains more true-positive medications while removing more false positives. These results show that medication recommendation is not only a representation-learning problem: even with a frozen clinical model, how its scores are converted into a constrained medication set can materially affect the final prediction.

</details>


### [73] [CrashGym: Measuring the Decision Value of Experimentation for LLM Agents in Counterfactual Market Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29245)
*Haocheng Lu,Haoxuan Cheng,Mingyang Chen,Minjun Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CrashGym——一个确定性、可重放的限价订单簿模拟器，用于衡量语言模型智能体通过"实验室实验"改善危机决策的价值，以相对最优干预的regret来评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体越来越多被要求做实验，但现有基准很少衡量这些实验是否真正改善了它们本应支持的决策。

Method: 构建CrashGym：确定性可重放的限价订单簿模拟器，智能体需在去杠杆干预与流动性后备之间选择。提交前可在实验室实现中查询干预实验，随后在独立种子的目标实现上按regret评分（对同一目标重复直接模拟所有可行干预，无需人工或模型评判）。显式实现四类放大机制（持久价格冲击、流动性撤出、保证金驱动清算、演化策略组合），可分阶段开启。在24个新采样世界中进行前瞻性预设研究。

Result: 实验室访问将唯一具完整记录的部署（Luna）的平均regret从8.878降至3.785，配对降低5.092效用单位（95%世界bootstrap区间1.028–9.507），为OBSERVE-ONLY均值的57.4%。另两个部署的前瞻比较不完整；在独立的探索性研究中，全部四个部署在实验室访问下regret均更低。策略组合内生演化的实验室相较固定组合未显示确立优势。

Conclusion: CrashGym将实验的价值度量为避免的决策损失，并使模拟器细节由其所支持的决策来评判。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to run experiments, yet benchmarks rarely measure whether those experiments improve the decisions they are meant to inform. We introduce CrashGym, a deterministic, replayable limit-order-book simulator in which an agent must choose between a deleveraging intervention and a liquidity backstop for a market about to suffer a shock-triggered crash. Before committing, the agent may query intervention experiments in a laboratory realization of the market. Its choice is then scored on an independently seeded target realization in which the simulator executes every feasible intervention, so regret relative to the best intervention is computed by directly simulating every alternative on the same target repeats, with no human or model-based judging. The simulator implements each amplification channel---persistent price impact, liquidity withdrawal, margin-driven liquidation, and an evolving strategy mix---as an explicit mechanism that can be switched on in stages. In a prospectively specified study on 24 newly sampled worlds, laboratory access reduces the mean regret of the one deployment with a complete record (Luna) from 8.878 to 3.785, a paired reduction of 5.092 utility units (nominal 95% world-bootstrap interval: 1.028--9.507), or 57.4% of the OBSERVE-ONLY mean. Prospective comparisons for two further deployments are incomplete; in a separate exploratory study, mean regret is lower with laboratory access for all four deployments evaluated. A laboratory whose strategy mix evolves endogenously yields no established advantage over one with a fixed mix. CrashGym thus measures the value of experimentation as avoided decision loss and lets simulator detail be judged by the decisions it supports.

</details>


### [74] [ARCANA: A Benchmark for Abstraction and Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29271)
*Ruqi Chen,Zilei Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出远类比推理基准 ARCANA（将真实世界物体/物理现象映射为抽象网格变换），人类可稳定求解而 LLM 存在显著差距；并提出 ConceptSIR 框架以缓解该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远类比推理（表层不相似但深层关系结构相似的跨域推理）是智能的核心成分，然而缺乏显式评测它的基准；已有 ARC-AGI 侧重先天先验而非跨域映射。

Method: 构建 ARCANA 基准：任务要求从真实世界物体或物理现象进行远类比映射到抽象网格变换；通过人类被试研究验证任务有效性；评测多类 LLM；提出 ConceptSIR 推理框架（概念滑移 concept slippage、内省 introspection、反思 reflection）。

Result: 人类可可靠求解 ARCANA；LLM 与人类间存在明显性能差距，并暴露系统性失败模式；ConceptSIR 使标准 LLM 解决了若干直接提示无法解决的困难任务。

Conclusion: 远类比推理对当代 LLM 仍是开放问题；基准、数据与代码公开，为该方向提供评测与改进基础。

Abstract: Analogical reasoning is widely considered a fundamental component of human intelligence. Few benchmarks are designed to explicitly test far analogical reasoning - reasoning between domains that are superficially dissimilar yet share deep relational or structural similarities. In this work, we introduce an analogical reasoning benchmark called Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus of ANAlogy (ARCANA). The tasks of ARCANA require performing far analogical mappings from real-world objects or physical phenomena to abstract grid-based transformations, different from the well-known ARC-AGI benchmark which primarily emphasized innate human priors. We validate ARCANA through a human study, demonstrating that humans solve these tasks reliably. In contrast, evaluations across a range of large language models show a distinct performance gap compared to humans, revealing systematic failure modes that remain challenging for current models. To address this gap, we propose ConceptSIR, a reasoning framework based on concept slippage, introspection, and reflection. Experiments demonstrate that ConceptSIR enables standard LLMs to solve several challenging ARCANA tasks that were unsolved by direct prompting in our evaluation. Overall, our results suggest that far analogical reasoning remains an open problem for contemporary LLMs. All the materials are available at sites.google.com/view/arcanacogtest. The complete codebase, including benchmark, and ConceptSIR, is publicly available at https://github.com/density-11/arcana-test. All ARCANA data and task instances can be accessed and explored at https://arcanacogtest.org/.

</details>


### [75] [Same Probabilities, Different Advice: Gain-Loss Framing Effects in High-Stakes LLM Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29294)
*Yuyao Zhang,Yakun Cui,Han Zhu,Jinsong Lu,Yike Guo,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FrameBench-HPS 基准，系统检验 LLM 在同等概率的不同损益表述下是否会给出不同高利害建议，发现框架效应显著存在且可通过概率规范化缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 日益用于支持高利害决策，但已有研究关注提示敏感性、认知偏差与置信度校准，尚未明确当同一概率以互补的收益/损失框架表达时，高风险建议是否会发生变化。

Method: 构建受控基准 FrameBench-HPS：由医疗、金融、教育/职业、法律/消费者权益四大领域的 240 个高利害情境生成 2,880 条提示，覆盖面互补的收益与损失表述；在 7 个 LLM 上比较推荐类别变化，结合人类评估检验感知风险与指令性变化，并开展对话式改写鲁棒性实验与概率规范化干预。

Result: 在完整提示组中，32.4% 的推荐类别因损益框架而改变，且多数变化涉及不确定性表达而非直接反转；人类评估证实感知风险与指令性随之偏移；框架效应在对话式改写后依然存在，并超出常规重复生成的随机波动；概率规范化显著降低框架敏感性。

Conclusion: 损益框架会实质性影响 LLM 的高利害决策建议，其影响稳定且非随机噪声；概率表示是可操作的一致性改进着力点，为高风险 LLM 决策支持系统的可靠性设计提供依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support consequential decisions, yet their reliability under equivalent representations of uncertainty remains unclear. Existing work has documented prompt sensitivity, cognitive biases, and confidence miscalibration, but it is still unknown whether high-stakes advice changes when the same probability is expressed through complementary gain and loss frames. We introduce FrameBench-HPS, a controlled benchmark of 2,880 prompts derived from 240 high-stakes scenarios across healthcare, finance, education/career, and legal/consumer-rights domains. Across seven LLMs, gain-loss framing changes the recommendation category in 32.4% of complete groups, with most changes involving uncertainty rather than direct reversals. Human evaluation confirms corresponding shifts in perceived risk and directiveness. Robustness experiments show that framing effects persist under conversational paraphrases and exceed ordinary repeated-generation variability. Probability canonicalization substantially reduces framing sensitivity, identifying probability representation as a practical target for improving consistency in high-stakes LLM decision support.

</details>


### [76] [LLM-Based Exogenous World Modeling for Dynamic Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29308)
*Ni Zhang,Haofeng Yuan,Jianan Zhou,Shiji Song,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将预训练大语言模型作为结构化世界模型的外生分量，负责预测不确定的未来作业到达，并据此构建Afterstate世界模型规划（AWMP），在动态作业车间与柔性作业车间基准上显著降低加权拖期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态调度需在作业在线到达时进行派工，同时预判未来作业，决策不当会在非线性拖期目标下累积下游代价；本文旨在解决未来作业到达不确定前提下的前瞻式派工问题。

Method: 1）采用afterstate分解，将已知的确定性车间状态转移与不确定的外生作业到达分离，使LLM仅需通过紧凑信念表示预测后者；2）提出AWMP，将演化得到的信念条件派工策略与短视角的情景式rollout相结合；3）设计惊讶触发的刷新机制，仅当新观测到达显著偏离先前预测时才查询LLM；4）引入在线残差自校准，使LLM以近期预测误差为条件以应对非平稳到达。

Result: 在多种到达分布下，AWMP相对八种经典优先派工规则中的最优者，在动态作业车间基准上降低加权拖期6–21%，在柔性作业车间基准上降低22–46%。

Conclusion: 预训练LLM可作为结构化世界模型的有益外生预测组件，配合afterstate分解、惊讶触发刷新与在线自校准，能有效实现前瞻式动态调度并在非线性拖期目标下取得显著改进。

Abstract: Dynamic scheduling requires dispatching online arrivals while anticipating future jobs, as poor decisions can compound downstream costs under nonlinear tardiness objectives. We show that a pretrained large language model (LLM) can serve as the exogenous component of a structured world model, forecasting uncertain future job arrivals. Specifically, we exploit an afterstate decomposition, which separates known deterministic shop transitions from uncertain exogenous job arrivals, enabling the LLM to forecast only the latter through a compact belief representation. Building on this, we develop Afterstate World-Model Planning (AWMP), which combines an evolved, belief-conditioned dispatching strategy with short scenario-based rollouts. A surprise-triggered refresh mechanism queries the LLM only when newly observed arrivals deviate sufficiently from previous forecast, and online residual self-calibration further conditions the LLM on its recent forecast errors to handle nonstationary arrivals. Across diverse arrival distributions, AWMP reduces weighted tardiness by 6-21% on dynamic job-shop and 22-46% on flexible job-shop benchmarks, relative to the best of eight classical priority-dispatching rules.

</details>


### [77] [SpatialBrain: Spatial Intelligence Growing from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29317)
*Yifan Zhou,Ziyang Gong,Xiaojun Ye,Yifei Liu,Donggang Li,Xiaolong Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialBrain 框架，在固定视觉语言骨干的前提下，将"场景证据"与"推理经验"分离，并通过 3DFusionMemory、Spatial Skill Library 与 Spatial Cognition 三个模块积累和复用决策经验，在五个空间推理基准中四个优于同骨干直接回答、一个持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不只是调用几何工具，推理者还需判断何时直接观察足够、何时需要重建、测量或变换参考系；作者希望在不微调模型的前提下保留关于这些决策的经验。

Method: 核心区分"关于场景的证据"与"关于如何使用证据的经验"。3DFusionMemory 将当前场景观测融合为可查询的空间关系；Spatial Skill Library 提供可复用的工具过程；Spatial Cognition 将任务条件与"何时、如何"使用记忆和技能的经验关联。完整轨迹被抽象为过程记录，并在任务块之间进行整合，使后续决策能借鉴先前执行，但不迁移场景坐标或参考答案。

Result: 在五个空间推理基准上，SpatialBrain 在四个基准上优于同一骨干的直接回答，在第五个上持平；并通过组件对比、经验覆盖曲线与资源决策追踪刻画了框架累积与使用这些资源的方式。

Conclusion: 在保持视觉语言骨干固定的条件下，显式分离场景证据与推理经验、并跨任务块抽象与整合过程记录，可有效提升空间推理表现，且该机制的资源累积与调用过程可被追踪与分析。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires more than access to geometric tools: a reasoner must decide when direct observation is sufficient and when to reconstruct, measure, or change reference frames. We present SpatialBrain, a framework that retains experience about these decisions while keeping its vision-language backbone fixed. The key distinction is between evidence about a scene and experience about how to reason with that evidence. 3DFusionMemory fuses current-scene observations into queryable spatial relations. A Spatial Skill Library provides reusable tool procedures, and Spatial Cognition associates task conditions with experience about when and how to use memory and skills. Completed trajectories are abstracted into process records and consolidated between task blocks, allowing later decisions to draw on earlier execution without transferring scene coordinates or reference answers. Across five spatial-reasoning benchmarks, SpatialBrain improves on direct answers from the same backbone on four and ties on the fifth. Component comparisons, experience-coverage curves, and traced resource decisions characterize how the framework accumulates and uses these resources.

</details>


### [78] [AgentMonopoly: Behavioral Patterns and Persona Control in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29330)
*Changshun Wu,Mengyu Li,Mathis Marsault,Xiaowei Huang,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AgentMonopoly 评测环境，对比四个模型家族、两个规模的大语言模型智能体在大富翁式博弈中的策略差异，并检验"人格指令"对行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏需要长程决策、多轮沟通与资源交易的环境来系统比较 LLM 智能体的行为策略；同时不清楚给智能体指定人格（persona）能在多大程度上改变其实际行为。

Method: 构建 AgentMonopoly 环境，智能体在其中沟通、买卖与交易地产、并做出影响后续回合的决策。设置三种对局组合：同一模型自对弈、同家族不同规模、不同家族；并为其中一个智能体注入人格描述（针对无该人格时观察到的倾向）。每个配置运行五个随机种子。

Result: 不同模型在交易频率、租金豁免、现金与地产管理上表现差异明显；更大的模型并不稳定赢得更多对局。人格指令会改变智能体的用词及部分行动，但改变在所有决策上并不一致符合所设定角色。

Conclusion: 人格遵循性应同时通过行为与结果、以及语言来评估而非仅凭话语；结论受限于所测 checkpoint、提示词与种子数，AgentMonopoly 的开源特性为后续扩展比较提供了实验基础设施。

Abstract: How do LLM agents' strategies differ, and how much can instructions to adopt a persona change their behavior over a game? We study these questions with AgentMonopoly, an environment where agents communicate, buy and trade properties, and make decisions that affect later turns. We compare models from four families, each at two sizes, in games with identical models, different sizes of the same family, or different families. We also assign one agent a persona, a description of how it should behave, intended to change a tendency observed in games without that persona. The tested models differ in how often they trade, waive rent, and manage cash and properties. Larger models do not consistently win more games. Persona instructions change the words agents use and some of their actions, but the changes do not always match the role across all decisions. These results show why persona following should be assessed through actions and outcomes as well as language. As our findings are conditional on the tested checkpoints, prompts, and five seeds per configuration, the open-source nature of AgentMonopoly provides the experimental infrastructure for further extending these comparisons. Our code is available at https://github.com/SuELS-Group/Agent_Monopoly.

</details>


### [79] [From Prompts to Posteriors: Can LLM Agents Perform Bayesian Inference in Context?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29331)
*Linus Filbry,Luckeciano C. Melo,Yarin Gal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估大语言模型在上下文中进行贝叶斯信念更新的能力，发现原生LLM普遍违背贝叶斯法则，但结合推理能力与代码执行后可大幅改善，甚至能近似解析不可解的后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于科学发现流程（文献综述、假设生成、实验设计），其共性需求是随证据累积持续更新信念，而贝叶斯推断是自然框架。既有工作普遍假设LLM无法在上下文中做有原则的贝叶斯更新，只能依赖简化与启发式；本文旨在质疑并检验这一假设。

Method: 构建多层评估框架：(1) 定性认识论案例，检验概率一致性与内部自洽性；(2) 贝叶斯推断能力的定量基准；(3) 外部给定后验的恢复能力；(4) 分析非贝叶斯更新对下游任务性能的影响；并对比现代推理能力与代码执行能力接入后的表现。

Result: 在多种场景下，LLM在上下文中更新信念时普遍违反贝叶斯法则；但在具备现代推理能力与代码执行能力时，性能显著提升，能够对解析上难以处理的后验分布给出接近的近似。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在配备合适工具的条件下已可用于科学发现中的复杂信念更新，有望快速提升AI驱动科学发现的效率与规模。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied across the scientific discovery pipeline, from synthesizing literature to generating hypotheses to informing experimental design. A unifying principle behind these tasks is the need to continuously update beliefs as evidence accumulates, for which Bayesian inference provides a natural framework. However, existing work assumes that LLMs cannot carry out principled Bayesian belief updating in context, instead relying on simplifications and heuristics. To challenge this assumption, we present a principled evaluation of Bayesian reasoning capabilities in LLMs, analyzing both the model's internal consistency as well as its ability to recover an externally determined posterior. Specifically, we build an evaluation framework spanning qualitative epistemological examples to probe probabilistic coherence, quantitative benchmarks of Bayesian inference capabilities, and analyses of the impact of non-Bayesian updates on downstream task performance. Across different scenarios, we find that LLMs generally violate Bayes' rule when updating their beliefs in context. However, when equipped with modern reasoning abilities and code execution capabilities, their performance drastically improves, enabling even the close approximation of analytically intractable posteriors. Our evaluation shows that current LLMs with access to appropriate tools could already be used to make complex belief updates in scientific discovery, enabling rapid acceleration of the efficiency and scale of AI-powered scientific discovery.

</details>


### [80] [Solving Go under Positional Superko: Sound Proof Transfer and New Board Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29414)
*Sami Kharma,Florian Schintke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并行深度优先证明数求解器Sanko，恢复位置超级劫（PSK）下可解的三大工具，首次证明5×5围棋并给出23个棋盘的PSK博弈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSK禁止重现任何历史全局局面，被写入中国规则与计算机围棋规则，但此前几乎所有的围棋求解结果都是在更简单的基本劫规则下计算的。PSK使合法性依赖整局历史，破坏了求解可处理性的三大工具：置换表（图-历史交互问题）、静态死活识别和安全着法剪枝，因此需要专门方法。

Method: Sanko为并行深度优先证明数搜索器，核心是一个廉价的证明迁移定理：已存储的证明在新路径上仍然有效，除非证明获胜方必须创造的某个局面已经出现过，从而可靠地恢复置换表、死活识别与剪枝三项技术。

Result: 六线程16分钟证明5×5围棋（此前最优需六进程5.25小时），单机192线程约1.5分钟完成；另确定23个棋盘的PSK值，其中11个在任何规则下均未被解出。PSK改变博弈值：4×7在基本劫下为全盘胜，在PSK下仅值12点，并定位了已发表最优着法序列的失败着手。

Conclusion: PSK下围棋求解可行且显著改变博弈论取值；作者公开求解器源码及每次运行的命令行与结果，使所有报告数值可复现。

Abstract: Positional superko (PSK) forbids recreating any earlier whole-board position. It is part of the Chinese rules as written and of the rules used in computer Go. Yet almost all solved Go boards were computed under a simpler basic-ko rule set. PSK makes solving hard: legality depends on the whole game history, and this breaks the three tools that make solving tractable, namely transposition tables (the graph-history interaction problem), static life-and-death recognition, and safe move pruning. We present Sanko, a parallel depth-first proof-number solver that restores all three soundly. Its core is a cheap proof-transfer theorem: a stored proof stays valid on a new path unless a board that the proof's winner must create has already occurred. Sanko proves 5x5 Go in 16 minutes with six threads, where the previous state of the art needed 5.25 hours with six processes, and in about a minute and a half on one 192-thread machine. It also establishes the first PSK values of twenty-three further boards, eleven of which were unsolved under any rules. Superko changes game-theoretic values: 4x7, a full-board win under basic ko, is worth only 12 points under PSK, and we pinpoint the move at which the previously published optimal line fails. Unlike earlier solutions of empty Go boards, we release the solver source together with the command line and outcome of every run behind our values and measurements, so that all reported values can be reproduced.

</details>


### [81] [MERP: A Latent-Bottleneck Framework for Robust Multi-Modal Multi-Label Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29430)
*Chuangxin Zhao,Meimei Zhang,Canran Xiao,Jiachen Luo,Jiajun He,Kaijie Chen,Huy Phan,Tomoki Toda,Biörn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多模态情感识别感知器（MERP），通过固定尺寸潜在表示、标签特定证据对齐与置信度感知融合，解决测试时模态缺失导致的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态多标签情感识别模型在训练时使用完整的文本、视觉、声学流，但部署时部分模态缺失会导致性能急剧下降，存在训练-测试鸿沟。

Method: MERP将联合序列映射为固定尺寸的潜在库（latent bank），使重复推理与原始输入长度无关；在潜在推理前后分别提取标签特定证据，并通过重建对齐两个层次；引入正负类原型、带频域扰动且带噪声门控的特征增强器，以及置信度感知的类级融合。

Result: 在20%测试时随机模态丢弃下，CMU-MOSEI上micro-F1为0.563、M3ED上为0.780，分别超过最强基线0.004和0.005；严重缺失的CMU-MOSEI纯音频设置下保持0.473 micro-F1，而LDDU仅为0.022。

Conclusion: 固定潜在推理为鲁棒多模态情感识别提供了实用且可验证的路径，且训练阶段无需缺失模态样本。

Abstract: Multimodal multi-label emotion recognition is important for affect-aware systems, but models trained with complete text, visual, and acoustic streams often degrade sharply when some streams are unavailable at deployment. We address this train-test gap by learning a compact representation that preserves emotion-specific evidence from any available subset of modalities. We propose the Multi-modal Emotion Recognition Perceiver (MERP), which maps the joint sequence into a fixed-size latent bank, thereby keeping repeated reasoning independent of raw input length; it extracts label-specific evidence before and after latent reasoning and aligns the two levels through reconstruction. MERP further uses positive and negative class prototypes, a noise-gated feature enhancer with frequency-shaped perturbations during training, and confidence-aware class-wise fusion to strengthen separability, reduce sensitivity to missing inputs, and combine complementary predictors. Under 20 percent test-time random modality drop, MERP achieves micro-F1 scores of 0.563 on CMU-MOSEI and 0.780 on M3ED, exceeding the strongest evaluated baselines by 0.004 and 0.005, respectively; in the severe CMU-MOSEI audio-only setting, it retains 0.473 micro-F1 versus 0.022 for LDDU. These results show that fixed latent reasoning provides a practical, testable route to robust multimodal emotion recognition without requiring missing-modality examples during training. Code is available at https://github.com/Creative-zcx/MERP.

</details>


### [82] [Events, Not Pixels: Where World Models Get the Return Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29435)
*Haoyue Liu,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出世界模型的"渲染质量"与"想象回报准确性"存在错位：引入转移级诊断发现，少数"事件转移"（获得奖励或episode结束的步骤）虽渲染质量并不差，却贡献了大部分回报误差，且该结构可用于指导修复与改进规划排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被用于规划时，规划器依赖模型想象的回报对候选动作排序，但现有评价主要看渲染帧的保真度。然而视觉上忠实的rollout仍可能给出错误回报，作者希望定位这种"渲染—回报"错位的来源。

Method: 提出转移级诊断：将模型想象的转移替换为真实转移，并以预算匹配的随机对照为基线，测量回报误差的下降量。据此比较不同选择策略（事件转移 vs. MSE/SSIM/LPIPS误差最大的转移）对误差的削减效果，并进一步分析剩余误差的结构（互补补丁区分真实事件与其他位置、八步时序容差）及对修复的预测能力。

Result: 发现1：事件转移渲染质量与其他步骤相当（像素误差比中位数0.85），但恢复它们可消除中位数70%的回报误差；而恢复MSE/SSIM/LPIPS误差最大的转移甚至不如随机选择。在通过可靠性检验的44个Atari-100k模型—数据集对上，39个事件补丁优于随机补丁（符号检验p=1.4×10⁻⁷），中位增益+44个百分点。发现2：剩余误差具有结构性，互补补丁可区分真实事件与其他位置误差，八步时序容差仅能找回少量被漏检事件；该结构还能预测扩散模型何时可修复这些失败。事件补丁可改善规划排序，且在稀有奖励事件的控制任务上同样出现集中现象。

Conclusion: 渲染保真度不能代表想象回报的可靠性；回报误差高度集中于事件转移，这一结构性认识既能诊断世界模型缺陷，也能指导修复策略并提升规划排序效果。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used for planning, where a planner ranks candidate actions by returns imagined by the model. However, these models are judged mainly by how well they render frames, even though a visually faithful rollout can still assign the wrong return. To identify where this mismatch arises, we introduce a transition-level diagnostic that replaces imagined transitions with real ones and measures the reduction in return error against a budget-matched random control. Our analysis yields two findings. (1) Event transitions, the few steps where a reward is earned or an episode ends, are rendered as well as other steps (median pixel-error ratio 0.85), yet restoring them removes a median of 70% of the return error. In contrast, restoring transitions with the largest MSE, SSIM or LPIPS error removes less than a random choice. Across the 44 Atari-100k model--collection pairs that pass our reliability checks, including three released families, event patches outperform random patches in 39 (sign test p=1.4 x 10^{-7}), with a median gain of +44 percentage points. (2) The remaining error is structured: a complementary patch separates errors at true events from those elsewhere, while an eight-step timing tolerance recovers few events missed by our deterministic models. This structure also guides repair: a rule recorded before four repair outcomes were known predicts when a diffusion model repairs these failures. Event patches improve plan ranking, and the same concentration appears on control tasks with rare reward events.

</details>


### [83] [PreCoMem: Predictive Cognitive Memory for Self-Evolving Long-Term Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29467)
*Jian Zhong,Zeyu Liu,Pingchuan Cao,Rongduo Han,Shunye Tang,Guohuan Xie,Mingyuan Qin,Ran Ji,Xiao Liang,Wei Wang,Haining Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PreCoMem是一个受预测编码启发的记忆巩固框架，将用户记忆表示为置信度加权的信念，并以Effective Surprisal为门控信号实现MAINTAIN/PROFILE/CORRECT三态更新，在多个长程对话基准上取得最优准确率并对非平稳信念漂移具备适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程对话智能体需要记忆既稳定以支持个性化，又能在用户真实改变时适应。现有记忆系统要么追加所有观测、要么用启发式规则覆写旧条目，导致瞬时噪声与真实信念漂移难以区分。

Method: 提出PreCoMem记忆巩固框架，借鉴预测编码，将记忆建模为对用户状态的置信度加权信念。核心信号Effective Surprisal融合语义偏离与矛盾证据，并在检索弥散、不可靠时对其进行降权。基于该信号的双阈值门控将每轮对话映射为三种确定性更新：MAINTAIN（强化被支持的信念）、PROFILE（将模糊线索存为低置信假设）、CORRECT（对可靠被否证的事实做软衰减）。PROFILE充当显式"候诊室"，避免对噪声证据过早承诺。

Result: 在LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S与PersonaMem-v2上取得一致的SOTA准确率；新构建的DiMoBench验证PreCoMem能适应非平稳信念漂移且不过度反应噪声。

Conclusion: 以置信度化信念表示加Effective Surprisal双阈值门控，可有效统一记忆的稳定性与可塑性，为长程对话个性化记忆提供可解释、确定性的巩固机制。

Abstract: Long-horizon dialogue agents need to keep user memories stable enough for personalization while adapting when the user genuinely changes. Existing memory systems often append every observation or overwrite old entries by heuristic rules, which makes transient noise hard to distinguish from real belief drift. We introduce PreCoMem, a memory consolidation framework inspired by predictive coding that represents memory as confidence-weighted beliefs about the user's state. Its core signal, Effective Surprisal, combines semantic deviation and contradiction evidence, then downweights this signal when retrieval is diffuse and unreliable. According to effective surprisal, a two-threshold gate maps each turn to one of three deterministic updates: MAINTAIN reinforces supported beliefs, PROFILE stores ambiguous cues as low-confidence hypotheses, and CORRECT softly decays reliably contradicted facts. The PROFILE stage acts as an explicit waiting room, preventing premature commitment to noisy evidence. Experiments on LoCoMo, LongMemEval-S, and PersonaMem-v2 show consistent state-of-the-art accuracy; our new DiMoBench confirms that PreCoMem adapts to non-stationary belief drift without over-reacting to noise. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhongWishing/PreCoMem.

</details>


### [84] [RubricOpt: Self-Revision of Semantic Rubrics for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29491)
*Wei Chen,Yubing Wu,Shigui Li,Delu Zeng,Jingfeng Liu,Junmei Yang,Zhiheng Zhou,Bo Li,Min Chen,Zhou Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RubricOpt框架，让策略在偏好优化中自我修订行为准则（rubric），以弥合训练标准与模型失败之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型的失败可揭示其训练所依据的行为标准存在缺口，但标准本身通常固定不变，未进入策略的反馈回路。

Method: 策略诊断自身"无rubric"输出，保留已被覆盖的要求，改写未能解决反复失败条款；用共享语义表示连接任务需求、偏好数据对比与rubric条款，并以确定性门控校验证据引用和固定语义锚点；rubric仅在训练期间引导DPO，偏好对、标签与目标保持不变，评估时移除。

Result: 理论与分析将rubric对无rubric成功率的局部影响关联到语义对齐及未测因素残差；能力与开放式生成基准上，rubric条件优于普通DPO，但增益/退化随模型与数据集而异；匹配续训实验隔离修订贡献，显示该编辑带来混合的增量效果，且其反馈与评估集有重叠。

Conclusion: RubricOpt将行为规范本身纳入策略反馈回路，但修订的独立收益有限且受反馈泄漏影响，需谨慎评估。

Abstract: A model's failures can reveal gaps in the behavioral criteria guiding its training. We introduce RubricOpt, a framework that lets a policy revise these criteria within preference optimization. The policy diagnoses its own rubric-free outputs, retains requirements already covered by the rubric, and edits clauses that leave recurring failures unaddressed. A shared semantic representation connects task demands, preference-data contrasts, and rubric clauses, while a deterministic gate checks evidence references and fixed semantic anchors. The rubric then guides Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) during training, with preference pairs, labels, and the objective held fixed, and is removed at evaluation. Our analysis relates the local effect on rubric-free success to semantic alignment and a residual beyond the measured factors. Experiments on capability and open-ended generation benchmarks show that rubric conditioning can improve outcomes over ordinary DPO, with gains and regressions varying across models and datasets. A matched continuation isolates the contribution of revision from additional training and finds mixed incremental effects for the tested edit, whose feedback overlaps the evaluation set. RubricOpt thus brings the behavioral specification itself into the policy's feedback loop.

</details>


### [85] [An Exact 93-Move Solution to a Nonlocal Crystal-Exchange Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29493)
*Zilai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以精确代价受限的广度优先搜索，证明了"沉星教团"某一关卡（11×11 水晶交换谜题）的最优方向指令数为 93，给出显式解并排除所有更短解，形成针对该实例的计算机辅助证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是确定该关卡所需的最少方向指令数，即对该具体谜题实例给出最优性证明，而非仅给出一个可行解；同时明确实现的可信边界（trust boundary）。

Method: 将问题建模为水晶交换谜题：指令使玩家与所选行/列中最近的晶体交换（无视距离），仅存在空射线时才允许单位步行；仅当 16 个晶体全部位于目标格时双门开放。用显式 93 步解配合精确、代价受限的 BFS 排除更短解。状态表示为 17 个被占格加 1 个活动格；采用可采纳启发式，将指令代价分解为压缩核心抽象与残余移动两部分，并用必要的"激活绕行"条件和"角点访问"条件加强剪枝。文中证明约简与剪枝条件的正确性，描述精确存储方案。

Result: 给出显式 93 指令解；搜索过程中保留 605,349,215 个占位集合、1,863,925,136 个状态，穷尽并排除了所有更短解，从而证明 93 为最优。

Conclusion: 该实例的最优指令数为 93，结论为计算机辅助证明，并附带源代码、执行日志与复现驱动，同时明确标注实现的可信边界。

Abstract: We determine the minimum number of directional commands needed to solve alevel of Order of the Sinking Star, modeled as a crystal-exchangepuzzle on an 11*11 board. A command swapsthe player with the nearest crystal in the selected row or column, regardlessof distance; only an empty ray permits an ordinary unit walk. Two gates admitthe player to the exit exactly while all sixteen crystals occupy their targetcells. We give an explicit 93-command solution and exclude every shortersolution by an exact, cost-bounded breadth-first search. The search representsa position by its seventeen occupied cells and one active cell. Its admissibleheuristic partitions command costs between a compressed core abstraction andresidual motion, strengthened by a necessary activation detour and a cornervisit condition. The completed computation retained 605,349,215 occupied setsand 1,863,925,136 states. We prove the reductions and pruning conditions,describe the exact storage scheme, and provide source code, execution logs,and a reproduction driver. The result is a computer-assisted proof for thespecified instance, with an explicit implementation trust boundary.

</details>


### [86] [Parallel Thinking for Large Language Model with Path Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29501)
*Jiaru Qian,Yihong Dong,Xue Jiang,Chun-Wah Hsu,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有基于强化学习的并行思维存在"推理路径模式坍缩"问题，提出DPT方法，通过惩罚并行路径前缀相似度来奖励多样性，从而提升推理性能并达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行思维通过同时探索多条推理路径来增强LLM推理能力，但基于RL的并行思维常出现推理路径模式坍缩，多条轨迹退化为相似路径，违背了并行思维"同时保持多种假设以避免过早陷入次优解"的核心动机。因此需要显式鼓励路径多样性。

Method: 提出Diverse Parallel Thinking（DPT），一种简单有效的强化学习并行思维方法。它对并行思维路径之间的前缀相似度施加惩罚，以此奖励模型发展出彼此不同的推理策略，从而更全面地探索推理空间。

Result: 在多个推理基准上进行实验与分析，DPT优于先前的并行思维方法，在基线上取得state-of-the-art性能，并验证了路径多样性是提升并行思维的关键要素。

Conclusion: 路径多样性是并行思维有效性的核心，DPT通过惩罚前缀相似度缓解模式坍缩，为并行思维RL训练提供了简洁有效的改进方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Parallel thinking has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) by exploring multiple reasoning paths simultaneously. However, we identify that parallel thinking via Reinforcement Learning (RL) typically suffers from the mode collapse of reasoning paths, where parallel thinking trajectories degenerate into similar ones. This homogeneity undermines the fundamental motivation of parallel thinking, that is, maintaining multiple hypotheses concurrently to prevent premature loss into a suboptimal solution. In this paper, we propose Diverse Parallel Thinking (DPT), a simple yet effective RL approach for parallel thinking designed to encourage path diversity. DPT rewards the model to develop distinct reasoning strategies by penalizing the prefix similarity of parallel thinking paths, thereby enhancing a more comprehensive exploration of the reasoning space. We conduct various experiments and analyses across multiple reasoning benchmarks to demonstrate the effectiveness of DPT. Results show that DPT successfully outperforms previous parallel thinking methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance over baselines. We validate path diversity as a key element to improve parallel thinking. Our code is available at https://github.com/JiaruQian/DPT.

</details>


### [87] [From Association to Decision Value: Testing Paired Instruction Dependence for VLA Corpus Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29646)
*Nurmukhambet Serikbayev,Abdul Basit,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文把"机器人示范语料筛选"重新定义为排序问题，提出"配对指令依赖性"这一反事实响应指标，并在136个独立种子四足导航语料上做前瞻性验证：该指标可靠且与下游配对正确性相关，但在既定预测器设定下压缩为无符号统计量后，增量决策价值有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上游诊断（如数据集质量指标）应保留"哪些候选能产生更好下游策略"的排序信息，而非仅与平均性能相关。作者希望检验一类反事实响应结构指标能否在语料排序选择中提供增量价值，并揭示"可预测"与"可用于决策"之间的落差。

Method: 定义"配对指令依赖率"：仅改变目标时示范者首次决策发生变化的比率。在136个独立随机种子的四足导航语料上，用56个语料开发预测模型，在80个留出语料上做前瞻评估；与"正确性感知"基线比较，采用proper log score增量、平均绝对预测误差及预设的top-20%选择对比；另做探索性响应分解（区分both-correct与both-wrong响应）。

Result: 该审计指标可靠，且与下游配对正确性相关；加入基线后降低平均绝对预测误差。但主要proper-log-score增量仅+0.059 nats（95%语料自举CI [-0.027, 0.133]），预设top-20%比较未确立增量选择增益。探索性分解显示，分离both-correct与both-wrong响应带来的留出增益大于其无符号切换统计量；种子间一致性表明留出端点仍保留可恢复的排序信号。

Conclusion: 反事实响应结构可以是可靠且具预测性的，但当被压缩为无符号统计量时，在此设定下增量决策价值有限。因此诊断指标应保留与结果相关的结构，并直接针对其意图支持的具体决策进行验证。

Abstract: Choosing among robot demonstration corpora is a ranking problem: an upstream diagnostic must preserve which candidates produce better downstream policies, not merely correlate with average performance. We study paired instruction dependence, the rate at which a demonstrator changes its first decision when only the goal changes. Across 136 independently seeded quadruped-navigation corpora, predictions developed on 56 corpora are evaluated prospectively on 80 held-out corpora. The audit is reliable and associated with downstream paired correctness, and lowers mean absolute prediction error when added to a correctness-aware baseline, but the primary proper-log-score increment is +0.059 nats (95% corpus-bootstrap CI [-0.027, 0.133]) and the prespecified top-20% comparison does not establish an incremental selection gain. An exploratory response decomposition suggests a structural source of this predictive-ranking mismatch: under the tested predictor specification, separating both-correct and both-wrong responses yields larger held-out gains than their unsigned switch statistic. Seed-to-seed agreement indicates that the held-out endpoint retains recoverable rank signal. These results show that counterfactual response structure can be reliable and predictive while providing limited incremental decision value in this setting when compressed into an unsigned statistic, motivating diagnostics that preserve outcome-relevant structure and are validated against the decisions they are intended to support.

</details>


### [88] [TraceForge: Distilling with Your Own Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29680)
*Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种合规的替代方案：以教师模型的答案与学生模型家族风格自行生成推理过程，通过"监督+对抗"两阶段训练生成器与判别器，生成轨迹的真实性评分接近真实轨迹，并在代码任务上显著超越教师自带思维内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿团队出于商业/合规考虑不再返回详细推理链，仅给出答案或简短摘要，使基于思维链蒸馏的训练数据获取受阻；而复现加密推理块的做法违反服务条款，无法作为长期产品基础。因此需要一种不依赖教师隐藏推理、且合规可部署的推理数据生成方式。

Method: 两阶段训练：先监督微调，再进行对抗训练。生成器以"上下文+教师答案"为输入，学习生成学生模型自身家族风格的推理轨迹；判别器（critic）学习判断推理轨迹的真实性。推理时由生成器为教师答案撰写家族风格推理，并可用判别器在多个候选中择优。评估采用独立裁判给出0–1真实性打分，并在五个基准、相同5000题与相同教师答案的条件下对比不同训练语料。

Result: 真实性评分（非正确率）：生成轨迹均值0.68，真实轨迹0.66，经判别器四选一筛选后达0.82。五个基准上通用任务中五种训练语料效果相当；代码任务上生成推理优于教师Fable 5自带的思维内容：HumanEval+提升3.7分、MBPP+提升7.1分（最终检查点），且MBPP+优势在所有检查点均保持。

Conclusion: 在不获取教师隐藏推理的前提下，可按学生家族风格合成推理轨迹，既能规避条款风险，又可超过教师原生思维数据的蒸馏效果；推测原因是"学生家族风格的推理更易被其自身学习"。局限性在于真实性评分不等于正确性，且结论主要来自单一教师与学生组合及五个基准。

Abstract: Using an LLM to generate training data for another is among the most general ways to train one, and it remains common practice. Some frontier teams have closed that route for their own models: what comes back is an answer, or a short summary, without the detailed reasoning trace behind it. That is a little ironic: they were themselves built on distilling human knowledge, and distilling an open-source model is still entirely routine; the restriction applies to their own outputs alone. Recent work recovers the hidden reasoning by replaying encrypted blocks across models. It somehow works, but it breaks the providers' terms of service, so it is not something a product can be built on long-term.
  We built the alternative. We take the context and the teacher's answer as input, and generate the reasoning trace ourselves, in the style of the student's own model family. Training is two-stage, supervised and then adversarial: a generator learns to regenerate the family's own reasoning traces from their context and answer, and a critic learns how real a reasoning trace looks. Applied to a teacher's answer, the generator writes one in the family's style. An independent judge rates how likely traces are to be genuine on a scale from 0 to 1: ours average 0.68, real traces 0.66, and traces selected by the critic from four candidates 0.82. These are authenticity scores, not correctness rates.
  We test the idea on five benchmarks with everything else held fixed. On general tasks the five training corpora are about the same; on coding, the generated reasoning beats Fable 5's own thinking content. With the same 5,000 questions and the same teacher answers, HumanEval+ improves by 3.7 points and MBPP+ by 7.1 at the final checkpoint, and the MBPP+ lead holds at every checkpoint. We suspect that reasoning written in the student's family style is easier for it to learn from.

</details>


### [89] [COMET: A coordinated multi-layer runtime monitoring framework for human-supervised AI-enabled safety systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29816)
*Niclas Flehmig,Mary Ann Lundteigen,Shen Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向人监督AI安全系统的协调式多层运行时监控框架COMET，将多层监控输出融合并映射为交通灯式显示，在压缩空气泄漏数据集上显著降低可信错误答案率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AI系统嵌入安全系统需监控以避免静默故障，但单一监控器难以为作为自适应控制器的人类监督者提供可操作信息；因此需要多监控协调与面向人的反馈。

Method: COMET由三层监控构成：信号完整性、物理一致性与AI能力包络；各监控器锚定不同故障模式，输出经协调层融合、由通信层映射为交通灯显示。在开放压缩空气泄漏数据集上评估（AI基于声发射检测泄漏），设置留出条件模拟协变量偏移、语义偏移与传感器故障；监控器开发后冻结并零样本测试，与无监控及单监控方案对比。

Result: 裸AI系统在三类偏移下均静默且自信地失效；COMET在协变量偏移的无泄漏数据及四类传感器故障中的三类上，将可信错误答案率降至零，标称误报率约1%。单监控器检测率接近，但无法归因故障模式；COMET将其匿名检测标志转化为诊断结论。

Conclusion: 语义偏移下残余的静默漏检界定了被动监控的能力边界；AI安全系统的运行时监控框架须将定量故障检测与隔离同定性的、面向人的反馈相结合。

Abstract: Incorporating AI systems into safety systems requires monitoring to avoid silent faults and ensure safe and reliable operation. Single monitors often do not provide actionable information for human supervisors, who act as the system's adaptive controller. We present COMET, a coordinated multi-layer runtime monitoring framework for human-supervised AI-enabled safety systems in which each monitor is anchored in a different fault mode. Monitor outputs are combined in a coordination layer and mapped by a communication layer to a traffic-light display. In this work, COMET consists of three monitoring layers: signal integrity, physics consistency, and the AI system's competence envelope. The framework is evaluated on an open dataset of compressed-air leaks, where an AI system detects leaks from acoustic emissions, with held-out conditions simulating covariate shift, semantic shift, and sensor faults, events expected during runtime but unknown in their exact appearance. All monitors are frozen after development and tested zero-shot, and the framework is compared against no-monitoring and single-monitor setups. The bare AI system fails silently and confidently under all three shift types, whereas COMET reduces the trusted-wrong-answer rate to zero on covariate-shifted no-leak data and on three of four sensor faults, at a nominal false-alarm rate of $\approx 1\%$. Single monitors come close in detection but cannot attribute the fault mode. COMET converts their anonymous detection flags into diagnoses. Residual silent misses under semantic shift define the boundary of passive monitoring. Runtime monitoring frameworks for AI-enabled safety systems must therefore combine quantitative fault detection and isolation with qualitative, human-oriented feedback.

</details>


### [90] [UserSWE: Evaluating Coding Agents in Multi-Turn Conversations with Dynamic Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29822)
*Zhan-Yu Wu,Ji Luo,Alyssa Wu,Junjielong Xu,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu,Chongming Gao,Xinting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 UserSWE，一个面向多轮交互式仓库级缺陷修复的编程智能体评测基准，并配套动态用户模拟与每轮执行验证的判定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多在单轮内给出完整 issue，缺少交互场景，导致评测出现假阳性（接受错误补丁）与假阴性（拒绝正确补丁），无法刻画智能体的真实交互式修复能力。

Method: 构建流水线将仓库历史与现有单轮基准转为多轮任务；每轮用信息池约束模拟用户可披露的信息，并以在 20,859 条开发会话上校准的转移模型采样人格链控制表达方式；每轮后执行测试；由来自不同模型家族的两个智能体分别撰写与把关判定，测试结果为主要证据，模型判断仅用于测试无法覆盖的方面。

Result: 在 42 个仓库的 72 个任务上评测 11 个智能体，发现排名随交互预算变化：Claude Fable 5.1 单轮解决最多，Claude Opus 5 在多数中间预算下领先，GPT-6-Astra 在第 8 轮解决最多。

Conclusion: 单轮性能不足以刻画交互式编程能力，交互预算应作为评测编程智能体的关键维度。

Abstract: As coding agents become more capable, developers increasingly use them to fix bugs through multi-turn interactions. Existing benchmarks do not adequately cover this scenario. Most present a complete issue report in a single turn without interaction. Their evaluation can produce both false positives and false negatives, accepting incorrect patches or rejecting correct ones. We introduce UserSWE for evaluating coding agents' ability to fix repository-level bugs through multi-turn interactions with dynamic users and execution-grounded verdicts at every turn. Its construction pipeline turns repository history and existing single-turn benchmarks into multi-turn tasks. At each turn, information pools constrain what the simulated user can reveal, while a persona chain sampled from a transition model calibrated on 20,859 development sessions governs how it speaks. Tests run after every turn, and a pair of agents from different model families writes and gates each verdict, using test results as primary evidence and model judgment only for aspects the tests cannot resolve. We evaluate eleven agents on 72 tasks from 42 repositories and find that agent rankings change with the interaction budget. Claude Fable 5.1 resolves the most tasks in one turn, GPT-6-Astra resolves the most by the eighth turn, and Claude Opus 5 leads at most intermediate budgets. These results indicate that single-turn performance is insufficient to characterize interactive coding ability.

</details>


### [91] [ToM2Vec: Continuous Theory-of-Mind Tokens for Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29934)
*Nithin Sivakumaran,Ali Payani,Hyunji Lee,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ToM2Vec：用固定预算的连续隐空间推理 token 来学习抽象心智理论（ToM），token 以伙伴真实想法为监督，被训练为抽象并对齐伙伴状态；测试时生成的 ToM token 作为自身思考与行动的调节条件，在多种协作/竞争/混合动机环境中一致优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作需要准确的心智理论，并须在不确定性下推理伙伴的多种可能状态。而 LLM 智能体原生的文本表示空间更易描述单一伙伴状态，难以承载多状态不确定性推理，因此需要更丰富的隐空间表示。

Method: ToM2Vec 在丰富隐空间中通过固定预算的连续推理 token 形成抽象 ToM。训练时以伙伴的真实想法作为这些 token 的监督信号，使 ToM token 学会抽象并匹配伙伴状态；测试时各智能体生成 ToM token，作为其思考与行动的 conditioning。评估覆盖 Overcooked-AI、多方谈判、Decrypto、Hanabi、Collab Escape/Capture 等协作、竞争与混合动机环境，并与基础模型及多种训练型、提示型基线比较。

Result: 在各环境中对基础模型和多种竞争性基线（训练与提示方法）均取得一致提升；将推理改为显式文本表达时性能下降，说明隐式推理是关键；训练可泛化到训练时未见过的伙伴模型族，包括专有模型 GPT-5.4；分析表明隐 token 编码的是 ToM 而非任务特定信息——可直接提升智能体回答伙伴信念、欲望与下一步行动问题的能力。

Conclusion: 隐空间连续推理 token 是构建可泛化心智理论的有效途径：以伙伴真实想法为监督的 ToM token 可作为行动条件器，跨环境、跨伙伴模型族稳定提升多智能体协作表现，且具备可解释的 ToM 语义。

Abstract: Forming an accurate theory-of-mind (ToM) is key to successful multi-agent collaboration and requires reasoning under uncertainty to capture multiple possible partner states. This is hard to do in the text representation space native to large language model (LLM) agents, which more easily describe a single partner state. To address this challenge, we introduce ToM2Vec, which learns to form abstract theories-of-mind in a rich latent space via a fixed budget of continuous reasoning tokens. During training, supervision for these tokens comes from the partner's actual thoughts, which the ToM tokens are trained to abstract and match. At test-time, ToM tokens are generated by each agent and act as conditioners for that agent's thoughts and actions. Across several environments covering collaborative, competitive, and mixed-motive settings (Overcooked-AI, multiparty negotiation, Decrypto, Hanabi, and Collab Escape/Capture), we evaluate ToM2Vec's ability to support multi-agent interactions and collaboration, finding consistent improvements over the base model and a variety of competitive trained and prompted baselines. We show that latent reasoning is critical, with performance dropping when reasoning is expressed in text. Moreover, we show that ToM2Vec training generalizes to partner model families not seen at train-time, including a proprietary model (GPT-5.4). In our analysis, we provide evidence that the latent tokens encode a ToM, rather than task-specific information: ToM2Vec's tokens improve the agent's ability to directly answer questions about partner beliefs, desires, and next actions.

</details>


### [92] [Commit or Abstain? System One Decision Models, LLMs, and Missing Legal Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29961)
*M. Mikail Demir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: System One决策模型用LLM输出校准概率而非文本，在CaseHOLD扩展的弃权条件下错误选择率最低（30%），标准任务上与最佳生成式LLM持平（88.1% vs 88.2%）而成本仅1/55；与决策模型一致性过滤可将错误引用减半，并可通过无标签蒸馏得到校准良好的本地小模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律AI需核验的断言数量巨大，要求可扩展、低成本的模型满足法律实务需求；法律权威核验属于封闭式问题（给定判例要旨或不选），需检验模型在正确权威缺失时能否弃权，并主张以错误引用而非仅准确率评价法律AI。

Method: 基于LLM构建System One决策模型，不做文本生成而是为每个选项输出校准概率；将CaseHOLD扩展出弃权条件，在5,314项中的1,670项替换掉正确判例要旨；对比4个决策模型与6个生成式LLM；并测试决策模型与LLM一致性过滤，以及将决策模型概率无标签蒸馏进Qwen3-8B。

Result: 无正确选项时生成式LLM仍有38%–86%的题目强行选择，最谨慎的决策模型为30%；标准CaseHOLD上决策模型与最佳生成式LLM相当（88.1% vs 88.2%），成本为其1/55；仅在决策模型同意时采纳LLM答案可将错误引用减半；无标签蒸馏得到的Qwen3-8B本地模型校准良好。

Conclusion: 决策模型在封闭式法律权威核验中兼具高准确率、低成本和更优的弃权行为，可作为LLM的验证与守门机制；法律AI应以错误引用率而非单一准确率作为评价标准。

Abstract: Given the enormous number of claims to be checked, verifiable legal AI requires scalable, cost-effective models that answer the practical needs of law. System One decision models are built on large language models but, instead of generating text, return a calibrated probability for each option. This suits the verification of legal authority, where the question is closed: a given holding, or none of them. We test whether such models abstain when the correct authority is missing. We extend CaseHOLD with an abstention condition in which the correct holding is replaced in 1,670 of 5,314 items, and compare four decision models with six generative LLMs. When no option is correct, generative LLMs still pick one in 38-86% of items; the most cautious decision model does so in 30%. On standard CaseHOLD, a decision model matches the best generative LLM (88.1% vs. 88.2%) at 1/55 of the cost. Accepting that LLM's answer only when a decision model agrees halves its wrong citations, and distilling a decision model's probabilities into Qwen3-8B, without labels, yields a well-calibrated local model. Legal AI should be judged by its wrong citations, not by accuracy alone.

</details>


### [93] [TIDE: Tree-Induced Fine-Grained Diagnostic Evaluation from Model Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29985)
*Jiayi Chen,Hui Li,Yujiong Shen,Qiyuan Peng,Yanxin Li,Chenhao Huang,Shaofan Liu,Guoqiang Zhang,Junjie Ye,Shihan Dou,Ming Zhang,Maxm Pan,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TIDE方法，将诊断性评估重构为行为驱动的决策树归纳，从基准样本与模型分数递归构建分层能力树，实现细粒度能力诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准评估通常以单一聚合分数（准确率、通过率、评分表得分）概括，信息量有限，无法揭示模型底层能力结构，而这对模型研发与评估至关重要。

Method: TIDE递归构建分层能力树：每个节点由LLM评判器提出候选划分并执行样本到组的分类，再以基于行为的信息增益在候选划分中选择。

Result: 在4个基准、5个模型上验证有效，发现聚合层面不可见的细粒度能力对比：Claude Opus 4.6在微观经济市场推理上达100%（GPT-5.4为83.3%），但在合同法推理上以77.8%落后于GPT-5.4的88.9%。

Conclusion: TIDE可作为细粒度能力诊断工具，作者期望将其扩展到过去与未来的基准，以充分利用评估结果中的丰富行为信号来理解模型能力。

Abstract: Benchmark performance is the dominant standard for comparing large language models (LLMs), typically summarized by a single aggregate score, such as accuracy, pass rate, or rubric score. This aggregate score provides limited information about a model's underlying capabilities, yet understanding these capabilities is crucial for model development and evaluation. To this end, we propose Tree-Induced Diagnostic Evaluation (TIDE), which recasts diagnostic evaluation as behavior-driven decision-tree induction to perform fine-grained capability diagnosis. TIDE recursively builds a hierarchical capability tree from benchmark samples and model scores: at each node, an LLM judge proposes candidate splits and performs sample-to-group classification, and behavior-based information gain selects among them. We apply TIDE to five models across four benchmarks to demonstrate its effectiveness, and find fine-grained capability contrasts invisible at the aggregate level: Claude Opus 4.6 achieves 100% on microeconomic market reasoning where GPT-5.4 scores 83.3%, but trails GPT-5.4 on contract law reasoning with 77.8% versus 88.9%. We hope TIDE can be extended to past and future benchmarks so that the rich behavioral signal in evaluation results is fully leveraged for understanding model capabilities.

</details>


### [94] [GuideEvolve: Self-Evolving Agent Framework with Expert Guidance and 3Ts Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30017)
*Meihong Jia,Yasemin Timar,Jonathan Taws*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自进化智能体框架 GuideEvolve，融合专家评估驱动的进化流程与"3Ts"自主调控机制（快慢思考、回退、自主终止），在多基准上以更低方差、更高进化成功率和更少迭代次数达到与 SOTA 相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体范式存在关键局限（如优化效率低、稳定性与鲁棒性不足、缺乏有效终止与纠错机制），亟需引入专家引导与认知调控手段以提升进化质量与部署可靠性。

Method: 双创新：(1) 任务特定的专家评估驱动的进化流水线；(2) 集成 3Ts 机制——Think Fast and Slow（快慢双系统思考，用于认知优化）、Throwback（时间回滚以恢复性能）、Termination by choice（自适应终止协议）。在 MGSM 与 MMLU 基准上进行实证验证。

Result: 性能与 SOTA 相当：MGSM 89.8% vs Gödel Agent 90.6%，MMLU 89.1% vs 87.9%；方差降低 25–59%，鲁棒性提升；进化成功率 100%（基线 82%）；优化迭代次数减少约 50%（由基线 30 次降至 15.1 与 13.4 次）。

Conclusion: 通过专家引导与 3Ts 调控机制，GuideEvolve 在保持与 SOTA 相当精度的同时显著降低方差、提升进化成功率与收敛效率，为自进化智能体的生产级稳定部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This study introduces GuideEvolve, a self-evolving agent framework incorporating expert guidance and three autonomous regulatory mechanisms, collectively termed the 3Ts: Think Fast and Slow, Throwback, and Termination by choice. The framework addresses critical limitations in extant self-evolving agent paradigms through dual innovations: (1) a task-specific expert-evaluation-driven evolutionary pipeline, and (2) the integration of 3Ts mechanisms to facilitate cognitive optimization, performance recovery via temporal rollback, and adaptive termination protocols. Empirical validation across the MGSM and MMLU benchmarks demonstrates that GuideEvolve attains performance metrics comparable to current state-of-the-art models (89.8% accuracy versus Gödel Agent's 90.6% on MGSM; 89.1% versus 87.9% on MMLU), while exhibiting notably reduced variance (25-59%) and enhanced robustness. Furthermore, the framework achieves a 100% evolution success rate, superior to the baseline's 82%, diminishes required optimization iterations by approximately 50% (from 30 iterations in baseline to 15.1 and 13.4 across tasks), and provides improved stability for production deployment.

</details>


### [95] [Robust Soft Skill Adaptation via Behavioral Localization in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30041)
*Zijian Jiang,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Behavioral Localization：通过对比有无文本技能的冻结智能体，定位技能收益集中的轨迹位置，并仅用这些位置监督短软提示；在 SpreadsheetBench 上以 10% 位置获得 96.12% 的技能增益，任务特定设置下成功率达 57.38%，优于完整文本技能与 OPCD 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本技能可增强工具使用智能体的能力，但长技能描述带来较高计算开销；软提示是紧凑替代方案，然而从整条成功轨迹学习会引入不必要的行为改变。作者观察到文本技能的有效作用高度集中于少数轨迹位置，因而希望只在该局部范围提取技能。

Method: Behavioral Localization：以冻结智能体为对象，对比有/无文本技能两种条件下的表现，筛选同时满足"提高成功 token 概率"与"显著改变下一 token 分布"的位置；再用这些局部化位置监督一个短软提示。在 SpreadsheetBench 上以 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 冻结智能体评测，涵盖共享提示与任务特定两种设置。

Result: 前 10% 轨迹位置覆盖 96.12% 的总正向技能增益；共享提示设置下，选择性监督将成功率从全轨迹的 30.36% 提升至 36.79%；任务特定设置下，使用 top 10% 局部化位置达 57.38%，超过完整文本技能与 on-policy OPCD 基线。

Conclusion: 少量行为相关的轨迹位置即可为软技能提供有效监督，说明技能信息在轨迹中稀疏分布，精准定位优于全轨迹蒸馏。

Abstract: Text skills can extend the capabilities of tool-using agents, but long skill descriptions increase computational costs. Soft prompts provide a compact alternative, yet learning from entire successful trajectories may also introduce unnecessary behavioral changes. We find that the useful effect of a text skill is highly concentrated in a small subset of trajectory positions. Based on this observation, we propose Behavioral Localization, which compares the frozen agent with and without the text skill to identify positions where the skill both increases the likelihood of successful tokens and meaningfully changes the next token distribution. We use these localized positions to supervise a short soft prompt. Experiments on SpreadsheetBench with a frozen Qwen3.6-35B-A3B agent show that the top 10\% of trajectory positions capture 96.12\% of the total positive skill gain. In the shared prompt setting, selective supervision improves success from 30.36\% with full trajectory to 36.79\%. Under task-specific conditions, using the top 10\% localized positions achieves 57.38\%, outperforming both the full text skill and the on-policy OPCD baseline. These results show that a small set of behaviorally relevant trajectory positions can provide effective supervision of soft skills.

</details>


### [96] [HGM-Dual: Lineage-Aware Guided Agent Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30042)
*Sudipta Paul,Pengrui Quan,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Vivek Kulkarni,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HGM-Dual 是一个面向规划智能体的诊断引导式 harness 进化框架，通过谱系感知上下文与错误条件下的双路径子代生成，在案例准确率上最高比最强基线相对提升 14.45%，并以 2–4 倍更少的评估预算达到 HGM 变体的最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harness 优化（改进智能体的提示、工具与工作流）是增强基于冻结或闭源 LLM 的智能体的有效手段。主流方法如 Darwin–Gödel Machine 与 Huxley–Gödel Machine（HGM）依赖递归自我改进，且主要针对编码任务，在编码任务中"改进智能体所需的能力"与"被改进的能力"恰好一致。而规划类任务要求智能体生成一组联合满足交互约束的工具调用序列，打破了这种一致性，导致开放式自我修改反复探索无效改动、消耗大量评估预算。

Method: 提出 HGM-Dual 诊断引导框架：(1) 谱系感知上下文（lineage-aware context），记录每个祖先改了什么、哪些行为提升或退化、哪些修改无效，为编辑器提供显式的"编辑—结果"归因，而非无结构的历史智能体档案；(2) 错误条件下的双路径子代生成（error-conditioned dual-path child generation），对每个被选父代生成一个保留开放式探索的通用子代，以及若干针对最可能导致父代最高排序约束违反的 harness 组件的专用子代。

Result: 案例准确率最高比最强基线相对提升 14.45%；以 2–4 倍更少的评估预算达到 HGM 变体的最终性能。

Conclusion: 在规划任务中，将进化过程结构化为谱系感知的编辑—结果归因，并结合面向约束违反的双路径子代生成，可显著提升 harness 进化的预算效率，弥补递归自我改进在规划场景下的能力错位问题。

Abstract: Harness optimization, which improves an agent's prompts, tools, and workflows, is an increasingly effective way to enhance agents built on frozen or closed-source LLMs. Leading approaches such as the Darwin--Godel Machine and the Huxley--Godel Machine (HGM) rely on recursive self-improvement and were developed mainly for coding, where the competence needed to improve the agent coincides with the competence being improved. Planning tasks, in which an agent must produce a sequence of tool calls that jointly satisfies interacting constraints, break this alignment. There, open-ended self-modification repeatedly explores ineffective changes and consumes substantial evaluation budgets. We introduce HGM-Dual, a diagnostic-guided framework for budget-efficient harness evolution for planning agents. First, HGM-Dual represents agent evolution as lineage-aware context. This context records what each ancestor changed, which behaviors improved or regressed, and which modifications were ineffective, giving the editor explicit edit-outcome attribution instead of an unstructured archive of past agents. Second, HGM-Dual introduces error-conditioned dual-path child generation. For each selected parent, it generates a general child that preserves open-ended exploration and specialized children that target the harness components most likely responsible for the parent's highest-ranked constraint violations. HGM-Dual outperforms the strongest baseline by up to 14.45% relative improvement in case accuracy while matching HGM variants' final performance with 2x-4x fewer evaluation budget.

</details>


### [97] [Which Differences Matter? Controlled Tests of Rule Transfer, Joint Reliability, and Information Use in Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30045)
*Tianyou Lai,Wentao Yue,Zhiwei Zuo,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过可执行算术规则，将受控输入变化与迁移、联合可靠性及有向信息使用联系起来，从而深入分析决策模型的规则利用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合准确率无法揭示决策模型究竟依据哪条规则、何种信息变化来给出离散答案，因此需要更细粒度的可测量框架。

Method: 采用可执行算术规则，通过语义规则分离及规则×状态交叉测试分别度量迁移与联合成功；比较等价措辞、用边缘保持的重关联校准联合正确性，并通过修改表格定义替代答案目标。

Result: 适应增益随与最近源答案的一致性提高；完全冲突的四元组下合并增益小且不同源划分增益符号相反；等价改写产生相反的任务级效应；联合分数偏离取决于参考保留的规则-状态关联；决策服务在等价措辞下的答案变化大于同文重复，并通常跟随修改表格值或映射所隐含的答案目标。

Conclusion: 决策模型的答案对规则呈现方式敏感，可用可控输入变化揭示其规则与信息使用方式，聚合准确率不足以刻画其可靠性。

Abstract: Decision models map stated rules and observed states to discrete answers. Aggregate accuracy leaves unresolved which rule and information changes guide those answers. We connect controlled input changes to transfer, joint reliability, and directed information use through executable arithmetic rules. Semantic rule separation and crossed rule and state tests make transfer and joint success separately measurable. We compare equivalent wordings, calibrate joint correctness through marginal-preserving reassociation, and alter tables to define alternative answer targets. Adaptation gains increase with nearest-source answer agreement; fully conflicting quartets yield small pooled gains and different gain signs across source splits. Equivalent rewrites have opposing task-level effects, linking transfer gains to rule presentation. Joint-score departures depend on which rule and state associations the reference preserves. A decision service changes answers more across equivalent wordings than across identical-text repeats and usually follows the answer targets implied by altered table values or mappings.

</details>


### [98] [Charts as Epistemic Actions for Experience-Driven Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30072)
*Xinzhe Wang,Xudong Weng,Zang Li,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将图表作为数据智能体推理循环中的内部认知动作，构建Probe诊断基准（200个任务、四种匹配界面条件），揭示"可读≠会用"的持久差距，并提出通过条件化轨迹蒸馏两类经验的方法，使智能体学会何时调用文本、图表或两者，在不改动模型参数的前提下提升分析准确率与视觉证据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ReAct循环的数据智能体已能执行复杂结构化数据分析工作流，但其中间推理几乎完全依赖文本；人类分析师则在数字、表格与图表之间切换，利用视觉证据揭示文本摘要遗漏的趋势与异常。作者希望探究图表能否作为数据智能体推理循环中的内部认知动作（epistemic action），以及如何教会智能体使用图表。

Method: 1）构建Probe基准：200个受控任务，覆盖四种匹配的界面条件，用于诊断智能体的图表使用行为；2）提出Method：从条件化轨迹rollout中蒸馏两类经验——一类记录如何选择与验证分析操作，另一类记录何时调用文本、图表或二者结合；3）由轨迹评判器（trajectory judge）对结果打分并标注中间步骤，并通过验证门（validation gate）只接纳能提升留出集表现的经验。

Result: Probe实验发现：智能体从自生成图表中获得的分数与从数值输出中获得的一样好，但仅有绘图权限并不能转化为一致的使用行为，存在"可读"与"会用"之间的持久差距；任务切片显示图表在时间、模式与异常推理上占优，文本在精确读数与条件验证上占优。Method在三个留出基准、四个LLM骨干上，相比匹配基线提升了分析准确率与视觉证据质量，且从未画过图的骨干模型开始使用图表，全程无需改动模型参数。

Conclusion: 图表可以作为数据智能体推理中的有效内部认知动作，但智能体难以自发且一致地使用，需要通过经验蒸馏显式教授"操作选择与验证"及"模态调用时机"两类知识；该方法以无参数更新的方式实现了跨骨干、跨基准的稳健增益。

Abstract: Data agents built on ReAct-style loops now execute complex analytical workflows over structured data, yet their intermediate reasoning still runs almost entirely through text. Human analysts instead move between numbers, tables, and charts, reading visual evidence to expose trends and anomalies that text summaries drop. We ask whether charts can serve as internal epistemic actions inside a data agent's reasoning loop, and how to teach an agent to use them. We first build \probe{}, a suite of 200 controlled tasks under four matched interface conditions, and find a persistent gap between what agents can read and what they choose to use. Agents score as well from self-generated charts as from numeric output, yet plotting access alone does not translate into consistent use. Task slices show that charts lead on temporal, pattern, and anomaly reasoning, while text leads on precise reading and conditional verification. We then propose \method{}, which distils two families of experience from conditioned trajectory rollouts. One family records how to select and verify analytical operations, and the other records when to invoke text, charts, or both. A trajectory judge scores outcomes and labels intermediate steps, and a validation gate admits only experience that improves held-out performance. On three held-out benchmarks with four LLM backbones, \method{} raises analytical accuracy and visual evidence quality over matched baselines, and backbones that never drew charts begin using them, all without touching model parameters.

</details>


### [99] [S^3martCirc: Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00755)
*Wendy Zheng,Yinhan He,Liang Wu,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出S^3martCirc统一框架，通过将节点行为抽象为两种可泛化的计算角色并定义定量指标，同时完成电路发现与功能解释，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机制可解释性方法采用"先识别重要组件（电路发现）、再解释其功能"的两阶段范式，忽略了组件重要性与功能角色本质上的相互依赖性；同时统一两阶段面临两大挑战：功能角色常与特定节点绑定而难以泛化，且其识别依赖主观解释而非可量化指标。

Method: 提出S^3martCirc（Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery）统一框架：将节点行为抽象为两种通用的计算角色（可跨任务泛化），并定义定量指标为节点分配角色，使重要性与功能角色被联合而非顺序地发现。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在电路发现任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将重要性与功能角色联合建模，并以可泛化的计算角色和定量指标替代主观解释，S^3martCirc为LLM机制可解释性提供了更有效且可量化的统一范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse tasks, from text summarization to question answering. Despite these capabilities, their black-box nature obscures internal decision-making processes. Mechanistic interpretability (MI) aims to address this by reverse-engineering neural networks into human-understandable algorithms. Current MI approaches for LLMs typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first identifying important components (circuit discovery), where components are typically individual nodes such as an attention head or feedforward neuron, and second determining the role they play in a certain task (functional interpretation). However, this sequential approach overlooks a fundamental insight: a component's importance and its functional role are inherently codependent. Unifying these stages presents two key challenges: (1) functional roles are often tied to specific nodes or components, limiting generalization, and (2) their identification relies on subjective interpretation rather than quantifiable metrics. To address these challenges, we propose S^3martCirc (Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery), a unified framework that simultaneously discovers circuits and interprets functionality. S^3martCirc abstracts node behavior into two general computational roles that generalize across tasks and defines a quantitative metric for assigning them, enabling importance and functional role to be discovered jointly rather than in sequence. Extensive experiments show that our framework outperforms existing methods in circuit discovery.

</details>


### [100] [Breaking Diffusion: Parallel Reasoning with Draft Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26542)
*Jaesang Yu,Seoyeon Byun,Yun Wang,Yan Lu,Juho Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Draft Refinement Language Models (DRLM)：用单个 denoiser 并行提出整稿修改，并通过基于已知污染过程校正“自 token 证据”得到的 cavity energy 在一次前向中评估候选草稿，整稿并行精炼优于自回归与离散扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草稿式作答让推理模型能在其他决策的语境下重新审视每个决策，但重复的扩散去噪并不能保证“各自看似合理的 token 更新”组合起来构成更好的答案。作者希望实现完整的“整稿并行精炼”，替代扩散逆向过程。

Method: 1) 单一 denoiser 从均匀随机污染的草稿中重建每个 token，从而同时学习保留(preservation)与修复(repair)；2) 其预测直接给出修改建议，不依赖扩散逆向转移；3) 评估时利用已知污染过程，校正“观察到该 token 自身”带来的自 token 证据，估计每个 token 受 prompt 与其余草稿支持的程度；4) 聚合为 cavity energy，单次前向即可计算，无需额外评分模型；5) 每轮提出修改、在修改后的上下文中读取完整候选，仅当能量更低时保留。

Result: 在 Countdown、Sudoku-Extreme、Maze-Hard 上优于自回归与离散扩散基线；仅 6M 参数即在 Countdown-5 达 44.4%、Sudoku-Extreme 达 93.9%。

Conclusion: 完整草稿的并行精炼是复杂推理任务中替代逆向扩散的有效方案。

Abstract: Drafting an answer allows a reasoning model to reconsider each decision in the context of the others. Yet repeated diffusion denoising does not ensure that individually plausible token updates form a better answer together. We introduce Draft Refinement Language Models (DRLMs), which use a single denoiser to propose parallel revisions and evaluate a candidate draft. We train the denoiser to reconstruct every token from uniformly corrupted drafts, teaching both preservation and repair. Its predictions specify revisions directly, without a diffusion reverse transition. Evaluation requires a different use of these predictions: confidence in an existing token reflects both contextual support and evidence from observing the token itself. Using the known corruption process, we correct for this self-token evidence to estimate how well each token is supported by the prompt and the remaining draft. Aggregating these estimates yields a cavity energy computed in one forward pass, without an additional scoring model. Each round proposes a revision, reads the completed candidate in its revised context, and retains it only if its energy is lower. Across Countdown, Sudoku-Extreme, and Maze-Hard, our method outperforms autoregressive and discrete diffusion baselines. With 6M parameters, DRLM reaches 44.4% on Countdown-5 and 93.9% on Sudoku-Extreme. The results establish complete-draft parallel refinement as an effective alternative to reverse diffusion for complex reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Density-Level Backward Error for Low-NFE Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29475)
*Ewsey Obzherin,Valentin Khrulkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出密度级后向误差框架，识别求解器修正方程缺陷中真正改变演化边缘分布的成分，据此给出求解器阶数感知的计算分配规则与速度代表选取目标，在低NFE下改进Euler采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低NFE生成式ODE采样受求解器阶数与固定网络评估预算沿流分配方式双重限制，现有基于轨迹/端点代理的调度搜索与高阶求解器设计未直接刻画对边缘密度的影响。

Method: 构建密度级后向误差框架，提取修改方程缺陷中改变演化边缘的部分，剔除在Wasserstein切几何中不可见的螺线管（无散）轨迹畸变；由残差导出阶数感知的算力分配规则，并借规范自由度构造目标以选取同一密度路径下数值上更优的速度代表。以精确高斯与混合模型作机制验证。

Result: 精确高斯与混合模型揭示了机制，包括曲率驱动分配失败的案例，以及连续最优传输并非离散最优；在预训练CIFAR-10 EDM概率流ODE上，密度感知分配在低NFE区间稳定优于固定步Euler采样；同种子收敛到稠密Euler参考，证明其加速逼近同一高NFE流而非改变渐近采样器。

Conclusion: 采样误差应以密度层面而非轨迹或端点代理衡量；预算分配与速度代表选择可在不改变渐近采样器的前提下显著提升低NFE采样效率。

Abstract: Low-NFE generative ODE sampling is constrained not only by solver order, but also by how a fixed budget of network evaluations is distributed along the flow.
