We expose both of these to message-passing graph neural networks, through a positional encoding built from return probabilities of directed place-transition walks (PNRWPE), and through same-type messages gated by diffusion kernels of the incidence matrix (DA-PNGNN). Our constructions raise macro balanced accuracy in predicting the generation factors of the 96 Process Discovery Contest 2023 models from 0.751 to 0.796 with PNRWPE, which nearly doubles accuracy on process loops, and to 0.830 with DA-PNGNN, which, without retuning, also improves net-to-tree retrieval top-1 accuracy on the self-supervised ProcRosetta benchmark by 4.1 to 5.9 points.

</details>


### [204] [Private Online Prediction from Experts with Small Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29575)
*Bo Li,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次给出在差分隐私下针对专家在线预测问题、遗憾为 Õ(√L⋆) 的算法，在低损失（L⋆≪T）情形显著优于既有 Õ(√T) 界，并在常数隐私参数下与非隐私下界仅差多对数因子；同时证明对自适应输入，即使 L⋆=0 也无法突破 Õ(√T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私专家在线预测算法的最优遗憾为 Õ(√T)，但当最优专家累积损失 L⋆ 远小于 T 时该界变得次优，低损失区间的隐私学习代价尚不清楚。

Method: 在差分隐私约束下设计专家在线预测算法（假设输入序列为 oblivious，即事先固定且与算法输出无关），使遗憾依赖 L⋆ 而非 T。

Result: 得到首个差分隐私遗憾界 Õ(√L⋆)；常数隐私参数下与非隐私下界匹配至多对数因子；并证明自适应输入下 Õ(√T) 遗憾不可避免，即使 L⋆=0。

Conclusion: 在 oblivious 输入假设下，隐私在低损失区间的代价很小，可实现近最优的 Õ(√L⋆) 遗憾；但该改进依赖 oblivious 假设，自适应情形下隐私本质地限制可达遗憾为 Õ(√T)。

Abstract: We consider online prediction from experts, a fundamental problem in machine learning, under differential privacy constraints. Existing private algorithms achieve near-optimal regret $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$, where $T$ is the time horizon. However, this bound becomes suboptimal when $L^\star$, the cumulative loss of the best expert, is significantly less than $T$. In this work, we present the first differentially private algorithm with regret $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{L^\star})$, offering a substantial improvement in the low-loss regime. Our regret bound matches the non-private lower bound up to poly-logarithmic factors for constant privacy parameters, demonstrating that privacy incurs only a small cost in this setting. Our result assumes that the input sequence is oblivious, i.e., it is fixed in advance and independent of the algorithm's outputs. We also prove that an $\tildeΩ(\sqrt{T})$ regret is inevitable for adaptively generated inputs, even when $L^\star = 0$.

</details>


### [205] [Fast Bayesian Optimization Through Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29580)
*Colin Doumont,Henry Moss,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一组基于线性结构加速的快速贝叶斯优化方法，在连续与组合输入（约10至6000+维）上达到与最先进BO相当的性能，同时快100至1000倍以上，填补了速度-性能帕累托前沿上的空白区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为BO仅用于"昂贵"函数（时间或金钱成本高），因而BO算法自身速度不重要；但实际问题成本从廉价到昂贵连续分布，现有方法在速度-性能帕累托前沿上存在空白区域（与CMA-ES等快速算法相比）。

Method: 以速度-性能帕累托前沿为视角，将BO与CMA-ES等更快算法进行对比分析；并利用现有BO算法中的线性结构，针对连续输入与组合输入（约10至6000+维）设计新的快速BO方法。

Result: 所提方法性能与最先进BO相近，速度快100至1000倍以上（10²×至10³×）。

Conclusion: 快速BO方法使贝叶斯优化可应用于此前因计算成本过高而无法涉足的全新问题类别，拓展了BO的适用范围。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is typically used only for problems where the underlying function is deemed "expensive" (e.g. in time or money), with the speed of the BO algorithm being of little importance. In the real world, however, problems span a continuous range of values, going from cheap to expensive. In this paper, we present BO algorithms through the lens of a speed-performance Pareto frontier: we compare BO against faster algorithms like CMA-ES, and highlight how certain regions of the Pareto frontier are currently empty. As a solution, we propose a new set of fast BO methods that exploit the linear structure in existing BO algorithms, for both continuous and combinatorial inputs (from ~10 to 6000+ dimensions). Our methods achieve performance similar to state-of-the-art BO, while being more than $10^2\times$ to $10^3\times$ faster, opening the field of BO to an entirely new class of problems.

</details>


### [206] [Fragment-Level Graph Encoding for Interpretable Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29589)
*Rafael Lopes Almeida,Ina Pöhner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将分子从原子级图改为BRICS片段级图输入，使任意GNN与任意事后归因方法都能直接产出官能团分辨率的解释，且预测精度不降、图规模约缩小五倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN是分子性质预测的标准工具，但其原子级解释对药物化学家难以直接指导实践（原子级归因不具合成/化学可操作性）。作者希望在不更换模型的前提下提升解释的可用性。

Method: 仅改变输入表示：用BRICS分解把分子编码为片段级图，节点为具合成意义的片段，节点特征为其组成原子特征的逐元素求和。因此任意消息传递GNN与任意事后归因方法都能在官能团粒度输出解释。实验覆盖4种架构、4个预测任务、2种解释器，采用scaffold划分，并与聚合式原子级基线做同条件对比。

Result: 片段级编码在约小五倍的图上达到与原子级相当的测试精度；Integrated Gradients归因在两个酶靶点上超随机水平恢复已知药效团（BACE约为随机率的1.5倍，PTR1为3.2倍）。同条件对比显示，原子级事后聚合不稳定：其BACE命中率区间随两个任意聚合选择而上下波动，既可能高于也可能低于片段模型；而片段级训练给出单一、无约定依赖的归因且无预测性能代价。

Conclusion: 以片段级图替换原子级图是一种"零模型改动"的解释性增强方案：在不牺牲精度的前提下获得稳定、化学可操作的官能团级归因，优于事后聚合原子归因。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are among the standard tools for molecular property prediction, but the atom-level explanations of their predictions are hard for medicinal chemists to act on. We propose a change of input representation rather than a new model: molecules are encoded as fragment-level graphs from BRICS decomposition, where each node is a synthetically meaningful fragment whose features are the element-wise sum of its atoms' features. Because only the representation changes, any message-passing GNN and any post-hoc attribution method produces explanations at the resolution of functional groups instead of single atoms. Across four architectures, four prediction tasks, and two explainers under scaffold splits, fragment-level encoding matches atom-level test accuracy on roughly five-fold smaller graphs. Integrated-Gradients attributions recover known pharmacophores above chance on two enzyme targets, namely BACE at 1.5 times the chance rate, and PTR1 at 3.2 times. A like-for-like comparison against an aggregated atom-level baseline shows that post-hoc aggregation is not a stable substitute: its BACE hit rate range both exceeds and falls below the fragment model's hit rate depending on two arbitrary aggregation choices, whereas fragment-level training yields a single convention-free attribution at no predictive cost. The code is available at: https://github.com/4D-Lab/frame

</details>


### [207] [Privacy in Context Is Being Overlooked in the Age of General-Purpose LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29925)
*Prakhar Ganesh,Yash More,Marco Romanelli,Ferdinando Fioretto,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场性论文，反对为提示（prompt）隐私设计"一刀切"统一框架，主张隐私本质上是情境化的，并提出应用特定部署、情境特定评估与提升隐私素养三条前进路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力与适用范围扩张，用户在同一通用系统中跨多种情境交互，学界与业界倾向于追求统一的隐私解决方案。但作者认为这忽视了隐私研究数十年的核心洞见——隐私是情境依赖的，统一框架会产生虚假的安全感，并掩盖内在张力。

Method: 采用论证性/立场性（position paper）分析：梳理现有提示隐私工作在"统一框架"方向上的漂移趋势，并剖析多种理想隐私属性之间存在的根本性张力，从而质疑统一框架路线的可行性。

Result: 指出统一框架不仅具有误导性，还可能使用户与开发者产生虚假安全感；即使未来尝试统一框架，也必须面对隐私属性间无法同时满足的根本张力，因此该路线需被重新审视。

Conclusion: 建议转向三条路径：面向具体应用部署LLM、对隐私框架进行情境特定评估、以及提升用户与开发者的隐私素养。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) continue to grow in scope and capability, privacy concerns are on the rise. Since users increasingly interact with a single general-purpose system across many distinct contexts, there is a natural tendency to pursue a one-size-fits-all solution to privacy. Unfortunately, this overlooks a crucial insight from decades of privacy research: privacy is inherently contextual. In this paper, we argue that existing work in prompt privacy is drifting towards attempts to design a unified framework, which is highly misleading and can foster a false sense of security. Furthermore, we argue that even future work in this direction needs to wrestle with fundamental tensions across various desirable properties of prompt privacy. Thus, such continued attempts towards a unified framework need to be reconsidered. We recommend three critical paths forward: application-specific deployment of LLMs, context-specific evaluation of privacy frameworks, and better privacy literacy.

</details>


### [208] [A Transfer Learning-Driven Methodology for Efficient and Sustainable Indoor Localization Using VLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29594)
*Masood Jan,Wafa Njima,Xun Zhang,Alexander Artemenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于迁移学习的可见光通信室内定位方法，并提出模型效率（ME）指标综合评估精度、能效与数据效率，在BOSCH工厂实测数据上精度提升47%，高噪声下ME达67.5%（传统模型仅10.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备间协作使室内定位成为工业自动化关键环节，但传统定位方法依赖大量高频数据采集与环境特定标定，可扩展性、互操作性差，且能耗代价高，难以适应光照波动与障碍物等动态环境变化，也难以复用已有研究。

Method: 面向可见光通信（VLC）室内定位，采用迁移学习（TL）应对环境变异（光照波动、物理遮挡），降低计算开销与能耗；同时提出模型效率（ME）指标，将定位精度、能效、数据效率与迁移增益统一为单一评价量以支持综合优化。

Result: 在BOSCH工厂采集的真实数据集上评估：相比未使用TL的传统模型，定位精度提升47%；高噪声条件下TL模型ME最高达67.5%，而传统模型仅10.4%。

Conclusion: 该方法可提供高效、可扩展的室内定位系统，适用于能量受限的工业场景，并表明迁移学习在环境鲁棒性与资源效率上的双重优势。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things enables seamless collaboration among connected devices, making indoor localization a critical component of industrial automation. However, conventional localization techniques struggle in dynamic environments. These methods depend on extensive, frequent data collection and environment-specific calibration, limiting scalability, interoperability, and effective use of prior research while imposing significant energy penalties that hinder practical deployment. To address these challenges, we propose an energy-efficient transfer learning (TL) based approach for visible light communication based indoor localization. Our method leverages TL to tackle environmental variability, including lighting fluctuations and physical obstacles that typically degrade localization performance, while simultaneously reducing computational overhead and energy consumption compared to conventional approaches. We also introduce a novel model efficiency (ME) metric, designed to integrate localization accuracy, energy efficiency, data efficiency, and transfer gain into a single evaluative measure for comprehensive optimization. Evaluations on a real-world dataset collected from a BOSCH factory demonstrate that our model achieves a 47% improvement in localization accuracy compared to a conventional model that does not utilize TL, and achieves up to 67.5% ME with TL models, compared to only 10.4% in conventional models under high-noise conditions. These results underscore the potential of our approach to deliver a highly efficient and scalable indoor localization system suitable for energy-constrained industrial applications.

</details>


### [209] [Your Benchmark Is an Empirical Measure Over Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29927)
*Yifan Sun,Naicheng Yu,Jiawen Gong,Jingyan Shen,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将基准测试视为难度轴上的经验测度，提出"测度失配"与"覆盖缺口"两种失效模式及诊断指标，并在25个基准上验证，最后给出BenchFill难度定向改写算法以缓解覆盖缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评测多被视为题目集合，忽视其隐含的难度分布；难度分布与预期不符或难度区间未被覆盖，会导致排行榜结论失真、无法监测模型局部训练进展。

Method: 建立"基于测度"的基准评测视角，形式化定义measure mismatch与coverage gaps两类失效模式，提出两个诊断指标并给出严格理论保证；在7个领域25个基准上以仔细估计的题目难度进行实证分析；提出BenchFill——基于难度目标的题目改写算法。

Result: 多数基准隐式地对某些难度区间过度加权，排行榜结论需谨慎解读；部分基准在难度轴上存在大量未覆盖区间，限制其监测模型改进过程的能力；BenchFill能有效减少局部覆盖缺口。

Conclusion: 基准评测应纳入难度测度视角；所提诊断指标可量化测度失配与覆盖缺口，BenchFill进一步将诊断转化为可操作的基准改进手段。

Abstract: A benchmark is not merely a collection of test questions; it naturally induces an empirical measure over difficulty. In this work, we develop this measure-based view of benchmark evaluation. This perspective allows us to identify and formalize two central failure modes: measure mismatch, where the benchmark difficulty distribution differs from the intended difficulty distribution, and coverage gaps, where uncovered regions along the difficulty axis prevent benchmarks from detecting meaningful localized training progress. We propose two diagnostic metrics that capture these two failure modes and provide rigorous theoretical guarantees. Empirically, we analyze 25 popular benchmarks spanning 7 domains using carefully estimated item difficulties. Our results show that many benchmarks implicitly overweight certain difficulty regions, so their leaderboard conclusions should be interpreted with caution; others leave substantial portions of the difficulty axis uncovered, limiting their ability to monitor the model improvement process. Finally, we turn diagnosis into action by introducing BenchFill, a simple difficulty-targeted item rewriting algorithm that effectively reduces local coverage gaps.

</details>


### [210] [Local and Global Recursive Ridge Regression for Online Residual Correction of Frozen Multivariate Forecasters under Label Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29604)
*Takumi Fujimoto,Hiroaki Nishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DRiC（DCT岭校正器）及其局部/全局变体，在标签延迟下用极小状态在线校正冻结的多步预测器，MSE平均降低14.55%且状态比ELF少15-17倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的多步预测器可利用自身残差在线校正，但标签延迟使目标块需等整个预测视界结束才到达，且校正器保留状态随通道数线性增长。目标是在小状态与大幅误差下降之间取得平衡。

Method: 将下一残差块与冻结预测块、最新输入块的前若干个DCT系数做岭回归，充分统计量递归更新。DRiC-L为每通道一个回归；DRiC-G将所有通道汇总为单一回归，统计量与通道数无关。另设守卫机制：按通道跟踪冻结预测残差的运行尺度，跳过远离该尺度的块更新并裁剪输出校正。

Result: 在7个数据集（4个ETT、Weather、Electricity、Traffic）与DLinear/PatchTST/iTransformer骨干上，对比10种方法。DRiC-L8在126种条件下平均降低MSE 14.55%，优于最优对比方法ELF的12.98%，状态少15-17倍，但Traffic上ELF更优。DRiC-G在321通道Electricity上仅需14.6 kB、862通道Traffic上18.9 kB，是其状态水平下最准确的方法，但仍有5-7种状态至少10倍于它的对比方法更准确。未填充缺失值的Weather上，无守卫的DRiC-L8 MSE为冻结预测的8.0倍，有守卫则降低7.94%。

Conclusion: DRiC以极小的递归充分统计量实现显著误差下降；全局变体状态与通道数解耦但在大通道数据上精度受限；守卫机制对缺失值/异常块至关重要，否则校正可能严重恶化。

Abstract: A frozen multi-horizon forecaster can be corrected online from its own residuals. However, under label delay the target of a block arrives only after the whole horizon has passed, and the corrector's retained state grows with the number of channels. Aiming to combine a small retained state with a large error reduction, we propose DRiC (DCT Ridge Corrector), which fits a ridge regression of the leading discrete cosine transform (DCT) coefficients of the next residual block on those of the frozen forecast and of the latest input block, with sufficient statistics updated recursively. Its local variant, DRiC-L, keeps one such regression per channel; its global variant, DRiC-G, pools all channels into one regression, whose statistics do not depend on the channel count. A guard tracks a running scale of the frozen forecast's residual for each channel; it skips updates on blocks far outside that scale and clips the issued correction. We evaluate on seven datasets (four ETT series, Weather with its missing-value codes filled, Electricity, and Traffic) with DLinear, PatchTST, and iTransformer backbones, against 10 naive, gradient-trained, and closed-form methods. DRiC-L with eight DCT coefficients (DRiC-L8) reduces the mean squared error (MSE) by 14.55% on average over 126 conditions, against 12.98% for ELF, the most accurate comparator, while retaining 15-17 times less state, although ELF is more accurate on Traffic. DRiC-G retains 14.6 kB on the 321-channel Electricity data and 18.9 kB on the 862-channel Traffic data and is the most accurate method at or below its state on both. However, 5 to 7 of the 10 comparators, all with at least 10 times its state, are more accurate there. On the unfilled Weather file, DRiC-L8 without the guard has on average 8.0 times the frozen forecast's MSE, whereas with the guard it reduces the MSE by 7.94%. Code: https://github.com/keiotakmin/dric.

</details>


### [211] [Reliability-Aware Probabilistic Fusion for Multimodal Learning with Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29621)
*Patitapaban Palo,Abhisek Tiwari,Mattia Carletti,Anshul Thakur,Xiao Gu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知的高斯专家乘积（PoE）框架，用输入依赖的可靠性权重调节各模态潜变量的精度，缺失模态直接从PoE中剔除；在两项临床多模态任务上验证其在缺失情形下的优势，并指出可靠质量信号的学习仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态输入可能缺失或退化，由此引出两个不同问题：证据是否可得（availability）与证据是否可信（trust）。现有融合方法往往将二者混同，缺少对可靠性如何影响后验信息量与各专家影响力的明确刻画。

Method: 构建可靠性感知的高斯专家乘积模型：每个模态作为一个高斯专家，其潜变量精度由输入依赖的可靠性权重进行缩放，缺失专家在乘积中被省略；从理论上刻画可靠性与可得性对后验信息量和专家影响力的作用。在两项临床多模态任务上评估，并以缺失感知训练、匹配PoE、一致性训练等消融进行对比；另用未见过的测试时退化作为诊断手段。

Result: 完整输入下框架与基线保持竞争力；在模态缺失条件下取得最明显的平均AUROC优势，尤其在HAIM-MIMIC-MM上。消融显示缺失感知训练带来显著增益，在匹配PoE、缺失感知与一致性训练的条件下，可靠性加权进一步带来HAIM上的额外提升。测试时退化诊断显示模态相关的可靠性权重与潜不确定性响应，但在严重退化下出现失效。

Conclusion: 可靠性加权为多模态融合提供了显式的证据控制机制，可区分"缺失"与"不可信"；然而模型尚不能可靠地学习质量信号，如何获得可依赖的可靠性估计仍是待解决的开放挑战。

Abstract: Multimodal inputs may be missing or degraded, raising distinct questions of evidence availability and trust. We introduce a reliability-aware Gaussian Product-of-Experts (PoE) in which an input-dependent reliability weight tempers each modality's latent precision, while missing experts are omitted. We characterize how reliability and availability affect posterior information and expert influence. Across two clinical multimodal tasks, the full framework remains competitive with complete inputs and achieves its clearest mean-AUROC advantage under modality absence, particularly on HAIM-MIMIC-MM. Ablations show a substantial gain from missing-aware training and an additional HAIM improvement from reliability weighting under matched PoE, missing-aware, and consistency training. Unseen test-time corruptions are used diagnostically and reveal modality-dependent reliability-weight and latent-uncertainty responses, including failure under severe degradation. Thus, reliability weighting provides an explicit evidence-control mechanism, while learning dependable quality signals remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [212] [Agent Behavior Maps: Locating Agents by What They Would Do, Without Running Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30014)
*Shota Fujikawa,Takuya Tamura,Kosuke Akimoto,Daichi Haraguchi,Masafumi Oyamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Agent Behavior Map：用共享参考轨迹上的中心化动作对数似然刻画 LLM-harness 组合，无需目标智能体重新 rollout 即可定位其行为坐标，并验证该坐标与轨迹 KL、性能及行为相似性的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功率相近的 LLM 智能体可能执行截然不同的动作序列，仅靠成功率无法区分行为差异；而已有行为刻画方法通常需要对目标智能体进行大量环境 rollout，代价高。

Method: 在共享参考轨迹上计算中心化动作对数似然，将每个 LLM-harness 组合映射为坐标点构成 Agent Behavior Map；在显式假设下推导坐标平方距离与轨迹 KL 之间的误差界；通过八个模型的交叉打分检验距离与直接测得轨迹 KL 的秩一致性；在 100 个 LLM、四个环境下评估坐标对性能预测与行为相似性的刻画能力，并在控制成功率与模型规模后检验相似性是否依然存在；进一步考察 harness 组件位移的近似可加性。

Result: 八个交叉打分模型中，地图距离与直接测得轨迹 KL 在秩上一致；ALFWorld 坐标可支持性能预测并识别行为相似性；该相似性在控制成功率与模型规模后依然稳健，且跨模型家族成立；harness 组件位移近似可组合，但精度随组件与环境而异。

Conclusion: Agent Behavior Map 提供了一种无需目标智能体 rollout、低成本且可解释的行为刻画工具，能超越成功率揭示行为结构与性能关联；组件位移的近似可加性为 harness 分析提供初步支撑，但其精度依赖组件与环境。

Abstract: LLM agents with similar success rates can follow different action sequences. An Agent Behavior Map represents LLM-harness combinations using centered action log-likelihoods on shared reference trajectories, without requiring new environment rollouts from the target agent. We derive error bounds relating squared coordinate distances to trajectory KL under explicit assumptions, and across eight cross-scored models the distances track the directly measured trajectory KL in rank order. Across 100 LLMs and four environments, ALFWorld-based coordinates support performance prediction and identify behavioral similarities. In ALFWorld, these similarities persist after controlling for success rates and model size, including across model families. As an extension, harness-component displacements approximately compose, with accuracy varying across components and environments.

</details>


### [213] [Cross-Area Transfer Learning for VLC-based Indoor Localization with a Transfer Efficiency Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29638)
*Masood Jan,Wafa Njima,Xun Zhang,Alexander Artemenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向VLC室内定位的迁移学习框架，用源区域预训练DNN+仅40%目标区域数据微调即可恢复97%全数据性能，并引入传输效率分数(TES)筛选最优源模型，最佳定位误差低至40.9 cm、成功率>86%，未见区域平均误差3.28 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLC室内定位虽具抗电磁干扰、高精度、节能等优势，但跨部署区域的环境差异导致性能下降，且目标区域数据采集成本高，需要提升跨区域鲁棒性并降低数据需求。

Method: 基于博世制造工厂四个真实区域的实测数据，构建迁移学习框架：在源区域数据上预训练DNN，再用少量目标区域测量微调；并提出复合指标TES（Transfer Efficiency Score），无需穷举评估即可识别最有效的源模型。

Result: 使用仅40%目标区域数据微调可恢复97%的全数据性能；经TES选出的源模型定位误差低至40.9 cm，成功率达86%以上；在未见区域验证泛化能力，平均误差3.28 cm。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业VLC定位提供了可扩展、数据高效的路径，在保持多样动态环境下高精度的同时显著降低部署成本与计算负担。

Abstract: Indoor localization systems are critical for Industry-4.0 applications. Visible Light Communication (VLC)-based systems offer advantages such as immunity to electromagnetic interference, high accuracy, and energy efficiency, but their performance is hindered by environmental variability across deployment areas. This paper presents a transfer learning (TL) framework for VLC-based indoor localization that enhances cross-area robustness while reducing data requirements. Using real-world measurements from four distinct areas of a Bosch manufacturing facility, we show that fine-tuning a deep neural network (DNN) pre-trained on source area data with only 40% of target-area measurements recovers 97% of full-data performance, thereby reducing the amount of target data needed. We further introduce the Transfer Efficiency Score (TES), a composite metric that identifies the most effective source models for transfer without exhaustive evaluation. Guided by TES, the framework selects the best source model, yielding localization errors as low as 40.9 cm with success rates above 86%. Validation on an unseen area confirms its generalization capability, with a 3.28 cm mean error achieved using the TES-selected source model. Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable and data-efficient pathway for industrial VLC localization, substantially reducing deployment costs and computational burden while maintaining high accuracy in diverse and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [214] [Probabilistic Machine Learning for Short-Term Atmospheric Seeing Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29654)
*Mary-Joe Medlej,Rahul Srinivasan,Simon Prunet,Aziz Ziad,Christophe Giordano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对莫纳克亚山顶的大气视宁度开展最多提前两小时的短期预测基准研究，重点比较概率预测与不确定性量化方法：以确定性RNN、LSTM为基线，对比高斯过程（GPs）与基于归一化流的FloTS，并同时用确定性指标（RMSE、Pearson相关系数）与概率指标（预测不确定性的质量与校准）评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气视宁度的准确短期预测对天文观测与自由空间光通信系统具有重要意义，因为光学湍流的快速变化会影响系统性能与运行规划，而现有工作对概率预测与不确定性量化的系统性评估不足。

Method: 在莫纳克亚山顶的视宁度实测数据上训练与评估多类模型：概率方法为高斯过程（GPs）与归一化流模型FloTS，确定性基线为RNN与LSTM；评价采用确定性指标（RMSE、Pearson相关系数）以及衡量预测不确定性质量与校准的概率指标。

Result: 深度学习方法在短期预测上优于标准循环网络，LSTM与FloTS取得最佳整体预测精度；高斯过程提供了有用的概率基线，但其预测分布受高斯假设限制；FloTS在保持有竞争力预测精度的同时给出灵活的不确定性量化，产生信息量最丰富的概率预测。

Conclusion: LSTM适合追求点预测精度，而在需要可靠不确定性刻画的应用中，FloTS是更优选择；高斯过程的概率表达受高斯假设制约。

Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of atmospheric seeing is of significant interest for astronomical observations and free-space optical communication systems, where rapid variations in optical turbulence can affect system performance and operational planning. In this work, we benchmark machine learning models for predicting atmospheric seeing up to two hours in advance, with a particular focus on probabilistic forecasting and uncertainty quantification. We compare two probabilistic approaches, Gaussian Processes (GPs) and a normalizing-flow- based model (FloTS), against deterministic recurrent neural network (RNN) and long short-term memory (LSTM) baselines. All models are trained and evaluated on seeing measurements from the Maunakea summit. We assess the models using both deterministic metrics, including the root mean square error and the Pearson correlation coefficient, and probabilistic metrics that evaluate the quality and calibration of predictive uncertainty estimates. The results show that deep learning approaches outperform standard recurrent networks for short-term forecasting, with LSTM and FloTS achieving the best overall predictive accuracy. While Gaussian Processes provide a useful probabilistic baseline, their predictive distributions are limited by the Gaussian assumption. FloTS combines competitive forecasting accuracy with flexible uncertainty quantification and produces the most informative probabilistic forecasts.

</details>


### [215] [SPARE: Sparsity Allocation via Routing Entropy for Efficient MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29662)
*Yifan Yang,Hanling Yi,Dongquan Yang,Feng Lin,Rong Xiao,Aijun Li,Xiaotian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPARE：基于路由熵的训练无关、选择器无关专家剪枝分配方法，按层分配剪枝数量而非均匀剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构可高效扩展模型容量，但内存占用大阻碍部署；专家剪枝可降低内存，但现有方法只关注"剪哪些专家"，并对每层均匀剪枝，忽略了层间路由分布差异。

Method: 以校准数据上token归一化Top-K门控权重的熵（路由熵）为指标，路由越弥散（熵越高）的层剪枝越激进；给出随机剪枝下的扰动上界以形式化该直觉，并通过固定选择器的受控干预验证路由集中度与输出变化的关系。

Result: 在相同选择器与预算下，SPARE在Mixtral、Qwen3、DeepSeek上平均精度优于均匀分配，高剪枝率尤为明显：50%剪枝下Qwen3-30B-A3B八任务均值57.2%→63.8%，DeepSeek-R1数理代码科学均值80.1→82.8。

Conclusion: 剪枝预算的层间分配与专家选择同等重要，路由熵可作为有效的免训练分配准则，与任意选择器兼容。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale model capacity efficiently, yet their substantial memory footprint poses a major barrier to deployment. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, but most methods focus on which experts to remove and prune every layer equally. We propose SPARE (Sparsity Allocation via Routing Entropy), a training-free, selector-agnostic method that decides how many experts to remove from each layer using routing entropy, the entropy of tokens' normalized Top-K gate weights on calibration data. When a token's gate weight is spread evenly, removing one active expert discards little of it, so SPARE prunes layers with more diffuse routing more aggressively. A perturbation bound under random pruning formalizes this intuition, and controlled interventions with a fixed selector link concentrated routing to larger output changes. With the same selector and budget, SPARE improves average accuracy over uniform allocation on Mixtral, Qwen3, and DeepSeek models, especially at high pruning ratios. At 50% expert pruning, it raises the eight-task average of Qwen3-30B-A3B from 57.2% to 63.8%, and the math, code, and science average of DeepSeek-R1 from 80.1 to 82.8.

</details>


### [216] [Differentially Private Sparse Reward Estimation with Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30099)
*Meng Ding,Mingxi Lei,Jie Zhang,Jinyan Liu,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"私有稀疏RLHF"问题，在Bradley-Terry-Luce偏好模型下给出差分隐私稀疏奖励估计的首个完整理论框架，并提出样本级DP与标签级DP的高效算法，其误差界仅依赖稀疏度s*而非模型维度d，并给出近最优下界及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF依赖敏感偏好数据，需差分隐私保护以防用户信息泄露；但现有私有对齐方法假设稠密奖励参数化，估计误差随环境模型维度d多项式增长，在过参数化大模型中不可接受。

Method: 假设人类偏好（奖励模型）仅由s*≪d个相关特征决定，在BTL模型下构建差分隐私稀疏奖励估计框架，分别针对Sample-DP（样本级DP）与Label-DP（基于随机响应的标签级DP）设计高效算法，并进行平方ℓ2参数估计误差的上界与下界理论分析。

Result: 在平方ℓ2误差下证明：Sample-DP上界为Õ(s*/n + s*²/(n²ε²))，Label-DP为Õ(s*/n + s*/(nε²))，n为训练数据量；下界表明Sample-DP速率近最优，随机响应框架下Label-DP保证不可进一步改进，并给出Label-DP的一般性下界。实验中在情感生成与安全对齐基准上优于稠密基线，隐私-效用权衡更佳。

Conclusion: 通过引入稀疏性假设，私有RLHF的隐私-效用权衡可摆脱对模型维度d的多项式依赖，仅与稀疏度s*相关，理论最优性与实验效果共同验证了稀疏私有对齐框架的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a central paradigm for aligning Large Language Models with human values. However, the reliance on sensitive preference data necessitates rigorous differential privacy (DP) guarantees to prevent user information leakage. Existing private alignment methods typically assume dense reward parameterizations, resulting in estimation error bounds that scale polynomially with the ambient {model size $d$}. Such dependence is prohibitive in modern {overparameterized models} where $d$ is vast. In this work, we study \emph{Private Sparse RLHF} problem to address this limitation, assuming that human preferences (reward model) are governed by a subset of $s^* \ll d$ relevant features. We provide the first comprehensive theoretical framework for differentially private sparse reward estimation from pairwise preference feedback under the Bradley-Terry-Luce model, and introduce efficient algorithms for both Sample-DP and Label-DP settings. Theoretically, under the squared $\ell_2$ parameter estimation error, we establish upper bounds of $\widetilde{O}(s^*/n + {s^*}^2/n^2\varepsilon^2)$ for $(\varepsilon,δ)$-Sample-DP and $\widetilde{O}(s^*/n + s^*/n\varepsilon^2)$ for $\varepsilon$-Label-DP based on randomized response, where $n$ is the training data size. For the lower bounds, we show that the Sample-DP rate is nearly optimal, and that the guarantee for our Label-DP method cannot be further improved within the randomized-response framework. We also provide a general lower bound for the problem with Label-DP. Empirically, our framework demonstrates superior efficacy over dense baselines across sentiment generation and safety alignment benchmarks, offering a significantly more favorable privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [217] [Leveraging Time-Series Foundation Models for Financial Crime Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29667)
*Danae Natalie Savvidi,H. Çağrı Bilgi,Kubilay Atasu,Arie van Deursen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TCEE 用冻结的时序基础模型（TSFM）总结账户交易历史，为交易边注入时序上下文表示，提升金融犯罪检测性能并显著降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融犯罪检测中可疑行为稀少且随时间演化；现有图方法虽能建模账户间关系，但普遍低估了账户交易历史中蕴含的时序行为信息，存在时序信号利用不足的缺口。

Method: 提出 Temporal-Context Edge Enrichment（TCEE）框架：用冻结的时序基础模型对每个账户的交易历史做摘要，并以相关时序上下文丰富每条交易的表示；该丰富表示可直接用于边分类打分，也可作为边特征输入 GNN 以捕捉更广的结构信息。

Result: 在六个 AMLWorld 数据集上，TCEE 在其中五个相对最强基线平均提升 PR-AUC 10.8%，端到端训练快 6.0 倍；将表示输入 GNN 后，在四个较小数据集上 PR-AUC 再提升 4.8%；在以太坊钓鱼数据集上，TSFM 生成的账户表示输入 GNN 用于节点分类，PR-AUC 较最强基线提升 14.5%。

Conclusion: TSFM 能捕捉互补于图方法的时序信号，可有效增强金融犯罪检测；随着 TSFM 持续进步，该路线具备进一步扩展潜力。

Abstract: Financial crime detection requires identifying illicit activity within large transaction networks, where suspicious behaviour is rare and unfolds over time. Existing graph-based methods capture relationships among accounts but typically underuse the temporal behaviour encoded in account transaction histories. We introduce Temporal-Context Edge Enrichment (TCEE), a framework that addresses this gap by summarising each account's transaction history with a frozen time-series foundation model (TSFM), and enriching each transaction's representation with the relevant temporal context from these summaries. These enriched representations can be scored directly for edge classification, or fed into a graph neural network (GNN) as edge features to capture broader structural information. Across six AMLWorld datasets, TCEE improves PR-AUC over the strongest evaluated baseline on five by a relative 10.8% on average, while training 6.0x faster end-to-end. Feeding TCEE's representations into a GNN raises PR-AUC a further 4.8% on the four smaller datasets. On an Ethereum phishing dataset, we show that TSFM-generated account representations fed into a GNN are also effective for node classification, improving PR-AUC by 14.5% over the strongest baseline. As TSFMs continue to advance, our results suggest they can strengthen financial crime detection by capturing temporal signals that complement graph-based approaches.

</details>


### [218] [BatchBench: Benchmarking AI Robustness to Batch Effects and Domain Shift in Biomedical Images - A JUMP Cell Painting Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30104)
*Sonja Topf,Ana Sanchez-Fernandez,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BatchBench——一个用于评估和比较AI系统对生物医学图像中未见实验批次与数据源鲁棒性的排行榜基准，基于大规模公开数据集JUMP-CP构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统对域偏移高度敏感，生物医学成像因图像采集实验条件差异巨大尤为突出；尽管域适应方法已有进展，域偏移下的性能差距依然显著，亟需可量化追踪进展的评估工具。

Method: 构建BatchBench排行榜基准，基于JUMP-CP（最大规模公开生物医学成像数据集之一）构建测试平台；该数据由Cell Painting assay生成，具有显著批次效应与域偏移，用于评测模型对未见实验批次及数据源的鲁棒性。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数值结果，主要贡献为提供可比较、可追踪的鲁棒性评估基准与排行榜（BatchBench）。

Conclusion: BatchBench为生物医学图像域偏移鲁棒性研究提供了标准化、挑战性的评测平台，有助于量化并推动该方向进展。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, current AI systems remain highly susceptible to domain shifts, particularly in biomedical imaging, where images are usually acquired under substantially different experimental conditions. Although recent domain adaptation methods have made substantial progress, significant performance gaps under domain shift persist. To measure progress in this regard, we introduce BatchBench, a leaderboard that evaluates and compares the robustness of AI systems on biomedical images from unseen experimental batches and data sources. BatchBench is built on JUMP-CP, one of the largest publicly available biomedical imaging datasets. Generated using the Cell Painting assay, JUMP-CP exhibits pronounced batch effects and domain shifts, making it a challenging testbed for evaluating robustness to distributional changes.

</details>


### [219] [Characterizing Oblivious Common-Reward Multi-Player Bandits: Universal Stationarity, Coordination Traps, and Certified Global Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29673)
*Mingxiao Li,Wenhai Lai,Hongyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对"无意识共同奖励多人多臂老虎机"(OC-MPMAB)进行理论分析：证明玩家各自的独立梯度-老虎机更新联合构成隐藏乘积策略目标的无偏随机梯度估计，从而给出普适的有限时间一阶平稳性保证；证明平稳性一般无法加强为全局最优（乘积策略陷阱 obstruction），并在奖励表结构假设下通过"有限表证书"恢复全局最优，得到条件化的策略差距与伪遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一个极简的多人 bandit 模型：多玩家同时选臂、仅观测共享奖励，且玩家完全无意识——看不到他人动作、甚至不知道其他玩家是否存在。此类设定缺乏对现有收敛/最优性理论的刻画，作者希望厘清在这种极弱信息条件下，独立学习动态究竟能得到何种保证（平稳性还是全局最优）。

Method: 理论分析。核心是证明各玩家独立执行的梯度-老虎机更新在联合层面等价于某个隐藏乘积策略目标的无偏随机梯度估计，由此推出有限时间的一阶平稳性界；随后构造"乘积策略陷阱"反例说明全局最优不可保证；再引入有限表证书，分别刻画乘积梯度占优与自约束随机噪声，建立证书条件下的策略差距与伪遗憾界。

Result: (1) 得到对所有实例成立的普适有限时间一阶平稳性保证；(2) 证明平稳性一般不能加强为全局最优，给出乘积策略陷阱 obstruction；(3) 在奖励表结构假设与有限表证书下恢复全局最优，给出策略差距与伪遗憾的证书条件化界；当证书一致时，得到标准 O(1/t) 策略差距率与 O(log T) 伪遗憾率。

Conclusion: 在完全无意识的共享奖励多人 bandit 中，独立梯度式学习的普适保证止步于一阶平稳性；全局最优需要奖励表的结构条件，可通过有限表证书刻画，并在一致证书下达到标准收敛速率。该工作为该极简模型建立了从平稳性到最优性的完整理论图景。

Abstract: This paper presents a theoretical analysis of the oblivious common-reward multi-player multi-armed bandit (OC-MPMAB), a minimal multi-player bandit model in which multiple players select arms simultaneously and observe only a shared reward. Crucially, the players are fully oblivious: they do not observe the actions of other players and do not even know whether any other players exist. The contributions of this work are threefold. First, we prove that the independent gradient-bandit updates applied by the players jointly form an unbiased stochastic gradient estimator of a hidden product-policy objective. This property then yields a universal finite-time first-order stationarity guarantee for every instance. Second, we show that stationarity cannot, in general, be strengthened to global optimality. To illustrate this, we identify a product-policy trap obstruction that rules out the guarantee of global optimality. Third, we show that global optimality can be recovered under structural assumptions on the reward table. To make global optimality achievable, we introduce finite-table certificates that separately characterize product-gradient dominance and self-bounding stochastic noise. We then establish certificate-conditioned bounds on the product-policy gap and pseudo-regret. Consequently, the standard $O(1/t)$ product-policy gap and $O(\log T)$ pseudo-regret rates hold when the certificates are uniform.

</details>


### [220] [DriveMLP: When Training Strategy Outperforms Model Complexity in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29676)
*Alessandro Canevaro,Julian Schmidt,Hang Yu,Ye Jin,Sajad Marvi,Georg Martius,Julian Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以RL作为纯优化工具、由专家轨迹距离奖励引导的闭环模仿学习框架EGRL，并据此得到轻量MLP策略DriveMLP，在nuPlan Val14 Reactive上达86.51分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环执行下的运动规划策略可靠性是自动驾驶核心难题：IL可扩展但存在分布偏移、因果混淆与复合误差；RL依赖手工规则奖励与大规模仿真。

Method: 简单的闭环IL框架EGRL：将RL仅作为优化工具，以基于日志专家数据构造的轨迹距离奖励进行引导；通过受控实证研究分析性能来源，并提出轻量MLP策略DriveMLP作为该发现的简单实例化。

Result: 实证表明训练流程而非模型规模与复杂度贡献了大部分性能增益；DriveMLP在nuPlan Val14 Reactive基准取得86.51分，较此前最佳IL规划器Flow Planner提升3.2分，推理速度提升15倍以上。

Conclusion: 训练范式的重要性优于模型容量，轻量MLP配合EGRL即可在闭环规划基准上取得领先且高效率的表现。

Abstract: Learning motion planning policies that remain reliable under closed-loop execution is a central challenge in Autonomous Driving (AD). Imitation Learning (IL) scales well to large AD datasets but suffers from distribution shift, causal confusion, and compounding errors under closed-loop execution. In contrast, Reinforcement Learning (RL) typically requires handcrafted rule-based rewards and massive simulation. We study a simple closed-loop IL framework that uses RL purely as an optimization tool, guided by a trajectory-distance reward derived from logged expert data. We refer to this training strategy as Expert-Guided RL (EGRL). Through a controlled empirical study we find evidence that the training procedure, rather than model size and complexity, accounts for a substantial part of the performance gains. We then introduce DriveMLP, a lightweight MLP policy trained with EGRL, as a simple instantiation of these findings. It achieves a score of 86.51 on the nuPlan Val14 Reactive benchmark, a +3.2 point improvement over the previous best IL planner (Flow Planner), with >15x faster inference. Code is available at: https://github.com/alessandro-canevaro/DriveMLP .

</details>


### [221] [Monitorability Is Trainable: Post-Training for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29734)
*Usman Anwar,Tim Bakker,Christos Louizos,David Krueger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督后训练目标，通过最大化CoT与输出之间的互信息来提升CoT可监控性，可恢复被退化策略的大部分监控能力并进一步增强原始模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控是监督强大AI系统的少数实用手段之一，但很脆弱：只有当推理与模型实际行为保持耦合时才有用。该耦合会在训练中被CoT压缩、针对监控器的优化、以及合成文档的域外泛化等因素削弱。作者因此希望将可监控性视为策略的一种可训练属性。

Method: 提出基于最大化思维链（CoT）与输出之间互信息的无监督后训练目标，以此改善CoT可监控性；在三个具有退化CoT的模型organism上验证，并应用于未退化策略。

Result: 在三个CoT退化的模型organism上，该方法恢复了大部或全部丢失的可监控性；应用于未退化策略时，进一步提升了经评判的可监控性、改善了CoT监控器与激活探针对不安全行为的预测、并增加了关键性（consequential）推理的出现频率。

Conclusion: 可监控性应被视为策略的一种可训练属性；专门的可监控性后训练阶段可作为现有"保护CoT免受直接优化压力"实践的有益延伸。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is one of the few practical tools available for overseeing capable AI systems, but it is fragile: a model's reasoning is only useful to a monitor if it remains coupled to what the model actually does. This coupling can be degraded during training through, for example, CoT compression, optimization against a monitor, and out-of-context generalization from synthetic documents. In this work, we propose an unsupervised post-training objective based on maximizing mutual information between CoT and the output for improving CoT monitorability. Across three model organisms with degraded CoT, it recovers most or all of the lost monitorability. Applied to undegraded policies, it further improves judged monitorability, improves prediction of unsafe behavior by CoT monitors and activation probes, and increases the prevalence of consequential reasoning. These results suggest that monitorability should be treated as a trainable attribute of the policy. A dedicated monitorability post-training stage can extend current practices that protect CoT from direct optimization pressure.

</details>


### [222] [Asymmetric Likelihood-Free Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29798)
*Gunjan Kumar,Yash Pote,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非对称无似然假设检验（asymmetric LFHT），刻画可行三元组 $(s_P,s_Q,s_D)$ 至多对数因子，证明不等参考预算可带来多项式级节省；并应用于非自适应分布 2-聚类，给出匹配上下界及 $\widetilde\Theta(k^{1/4})$ 的最坏情形自适应性间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 LFHT 假设 $P,Q$ 的样本预算相同，但实际场景中两参考分布的可用样本往往不等。作者希望刻画在预算不对称时何时仍可检验，并量化不对称带来的收益，同时将此刻画用于样本分配必须预先固定的非自适应聚类问题。

Method: 以全变差距离 $\mathrm{d_{TV}}(P,Q)\ge\varepsilon$ 为分离条件，在 $[n]$ 上的三个分布 $P,Q,D$ 中，研究非对称参考预算 $s_P\neq s_Q$ 下识别 $D\in\{P,Q\}$ 的可行性，刻画可行三元组 $(s_P,s_Q,s_D)$ 至多对数因子；再将结果推广到 $k$ 个分布的非自适应 2-聚类（样本分配须在观测数据前固定），给出基于域大小 $n$、分布数 $k$、较小簇规模与分离度的上下界构造。

Result: 非对称预算可实现多项式级节省：常数分离下，$P$ 取 $n$ 个样本、$Q$ 取 $n^{3/4}$ 个样本时，仅需 $\widetilde O(n^{1/4})$ 个 $D$ 样本即可检验，而对称 LFHT 与不等样本 closeness testing 两类基线均需 $\Omega(\sqrt n)$ 个 $D$ 样本。对非自适应 2-聚类，得到对数因子内匹配的上下界（单簇已知与双簇未知两种设定），与自适应分配已知界相比，最坏情形自适应性间隙为 $\widetilde\Theta(k^{1/4})$。

Conclusion: 参考分布样本预算的不对称性是一种有效但常被忽视的资源，可显著降低检验样本复杂度；非自适应样本分配相对自适应分配在最坏情形下存在 $\widetilde\Theta(k^{1/4})$ 的固有损失，该间隙由本文匹配界所确定。

Abstract: In likelihood-free hypothesis testing (LFHT), given samples from two unknown distributions $P,Q$ on $[n]$ with $\mathrm{d_{TV}}(P,Q)\geq\varepsilon$, and from a third distribution $D\in\{P,Q\}$, the goal is to identify whether $D=P$ or $D=Q$. We study asymmetric likelihood-free hypothesis testing, where the sample budgets $s_P$ and $s_Q$ for $P$ and $Q$ may differ, and characterize the feasible triples $(s_P,s_Q,s_D)$ up to logarithmic factors. We establish that unequal reference budgets can give a polynomial saving. For instance, for constant separation, $n$ samples from $P$ and $n^{3/4}$ from $Q$ allow testing with $\widetilde O(n^{1/4})$ samples from $D$, whereas both existing baselines---symmetric LFHT and unequal-sample closeness testing---require $Ω(\sqrt n)$ samples from $D$.
  We further apply our results to non-adaptive distribution 2-clustering, where the sample allocation among $k$ distributions must be fixed before observing any data. We obtain matching upper and lower bounds for this problem up to logarithmic factors in terms of the domain size, number of distributions, smaller cluster size, and separation, both with one cluster distribution known and with both unknown. Comparing with known bounds for adaptive sample allocation gives a worst-case adaptivity gap of $\widetildeΘ(k^{1/4})$.

</details>


### [223] [A Sharp Dynamic Haussler Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29898)
*Shihao Shao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在有界实值函数类上证明了归一化 ℓ_p 度量下的尖锐全局动态覆盖引理：以缩放尺度 d=sfat_{ε/32}(F) 计，存在与实例序列无关的单一因果专家族，覆盖所有序列上所有目标，规模上界为 (C/ε^p)^d；并给出匹配的 (c/ε)^{pd} 最坏情形下界（二元类、单条固定序列即成立），常数与 p 无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haussler 覆盖引理只给出静态、ℓ_1 下的度量熵界，而序列学习/在线学习需要随序列自适应、且覆盖所有时间跨度与所有目标的"全局动态"覆盖。Rakhlin–Sridharan–Tewari (PTRF 2015) 提出的 horizon-independent 序贯 ℓ_2 覆盖问题，以及 Alon 等 (STOC 2021) 与 Malliaris–Moran (Trans. AMS 2024) 关于二元类全局动态覆盖的公开问题均未解决。

Method: 将问题推广至所有 1≤p<∞ 的归一化 ℓ_p；证明技术由三部分构成：(1) 秩递减的修正调度（rank-decreasing correction schedules）；(2) 精确采样的前缀（accurate sampled prefixes）；(3) 目标识别后的精确延续（exact continuations after target identification），并通过"共享父节点路由"（shared-parent routing）把这些延续在合格前缀之间分配，从而消除对数级开销。上下界均以缩放尺度 sfat 刻画，常数统一且与 p 无关。

Result: 上界：对任意时间跨度与 0<ε<1/2，存在大小不超过 (C/ε^p)^d 的全局动态覆盖，其中 d=sfat_{ε/32}(F)，且专家族不依赖实例序列。下界：对 0<ε<1/4，即使专家为实值，二元类在单条固定序列上即达到 (c/ε)^{pd}。p=2 时以更强的全局形式解决了 RS Tewari 的 horizon-independent 序贯 ℓ_2 覆盖问题；p=1 时给出二元类尖锐的 (C/δ)^{Ldim(H)} 全局动态覆盖界，回答了 STOC 2021 与 Trans. AMS 2024 的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Haussler 的静态度量熵引理统一推广为 ℓ_p 下的尖锐全局动态覆盖定理，覆盖与下界在 ε 与 d（sfat/Ldim）意义下均紧，且常数与 p 无关；由此统一解决了序贯 ℓ_2 覆盖与二元类动态覆盖两个公开问题，为序贯/在线学习的覆盖数刻画提供了完整框架。

Abstract: Haussler's covering lemma (JCTA 1995) gives the sharp $(C/δ)^d$ metric-entropy bound for binary VC classes under normalized $\ell_1$ distance. We prove a sharp global dynamic analogue for bounded real-valued classes in normalized $\ell_p$, for every $1\le p<\infty$. Writing $d=\operatorname{sfat}_{\varepsilon/32}(\mathcal F)$, we obtain a global dynamic cover of size at most $(C/\varepsilon^p)^d$ for every horizon and $0<\varepsilon<1/2$. Here a single family of deterministic causal experts, fixed independently of the instance sequence, covers every target on every sequence. For $0<\varepsilon<1/4$, a matching worst-case lower bound $(c/\varepsilon)^{pd}$ holds already for binary classes on one fixed sequence, even with real-valued experts. The constants are universal, independent of $p$.
  For $p=2$, this resolves the horizon-independent sequential $\ell_2$ covering problem of Rakhlin, Sridharan, and Tewari (PTRF 2015) in a stronger global form. For $p=1$, it gives the sharp $(C/δ)^{\operatorname{Ldim}(\mathcal H)}$ global dynamic covering bound for binary classes, resolving questions of Alon, Ben-Eliezer, Dagan, Moran, Naor, and Yogev (STOC 2021) and Malliaris and Moran (Trans. AMS 2024). Our proof combines rank-decreasing correction schedules and accurate sampled prefixes with exact continuations after target identification. Shared-parent routing distributes these continuations among eligible prefixes, eliminating the logarithmic overhead.

</details>


### [224] [Preventing Model Extraction in Contextual Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29918)
*Adam N. Elmachtoub,Tianyi Lin,Wenxuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究PTO系统的模型窃取风险与防御：对线性预测模型给出攻击者可学到模型（至正缩放）的充分条件，提出逆优化+边界采样的自适应攻击，并提出仅在预测成本向量接近决策边界时扰动决策的防御机制，给出关于扰动半径的regret界及对最优拟合型攻击者的期望提取误差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTO系统只返回线性规划的最优决策而不公开成本向量，但攻击者可通过提交上下文查询来反推底层预测模型；成本到决策是多对一映射，然而跨上下文的观测仍可能泄露模型，因此需要刻画可提取性并设计防御。

Method: 1) 对线性预测模型推导攻击者仅凭决策观测即可将模型学习到正缩放意义下的充分条件；2) 据此设计自适应攻击，交替进行逆优化与边界采样（用指向当前攻击者模型决策边界的查询）；3) 提出防御机制：仅当预测成本向量接近决策边界时对决策施加扰动；4) 建立以扰动半径为参数的regret上界，并针对将防御响应拟合为评估分布下最优决策的攻击者证明期望提取误差下界。

Result: 理论上给出可提取性的充分条件、regret随扰动半径的界以及期望提取误差下界；实验在合成实例与真实数据上量化了安全—性能权衡，并表明该防御能提高攻击者的提取误差。

Conclusion: PTO系统的决策输出确实可被自适应查询用于提取预测模型，而基于决策边界的稀疏扰动能在可控性能损失下有效提升提取难度，形成可调的安全—性能权衡。

Abstract: Consider a contextual linear optimization problem, where a predict-then-optimize (PTO) system returns optimal decisions without revealing the cost vectors used in its downstream linear program. Our focus is on how to prevent attackers, who can submit context queries, from extracting the underlying prediction model. Although the cost-to-decision map is many-to-one, observations across contexts can reveal the underlying prediction model. For linear models, we provide sufficient conditions where the attacker can learn the model up to positive scaling by only observing the decision. These conditions motivate an adaptive attack that alternates inverse optimization with boundary sampling, i.e., using context queries leading to decision boundaries of the current attacker model. To prevent extraction, we propose a mechanism that perturbs decisions only when the predicted cost vector is near a decision boundary. We establish a regret bound in terms of the perturbation radius. For the attackers that fit the defended responses as optimal decisions under the evaluation distribution, we also prove a lower bound on expected extraction error. Experiments on synthetic instances and real data quantify the security-performance trade-off of the defense mechanism and illustrate that our defense can increase the extraction error.

</details>


### [225] [FrontierCS 2: A Benchmark for Computer Science R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29932)
*Qiuyang Mang,Wenhao Chai,Runyuan He,Dongruixuan Li,Zihan Zheng,Yujun He,Xuanyi Zhou,Hao Wang,Siddhant Ray,Bo Peng,Ruyi Ji,Shuo Yang,Haocheng Xi,Boru Chen,Xinyang David Han,Melissa Pan,Mayank Mishra,Boxi Yu,Sijia Liu,Kerem Akillioglu,Yi Pan,Zirui Wang,Mert Cemri,Diogo Antunes,Yichuan Wang,Aiwei Ivy Zhang,Zhenyang Chen,Qizheng Zhang,Kaiyuan Liu,Wenyuan Huang,Sherry Ruan,Shu Liu,Dacheng Li,Zerui Cheng,Zeyu Shen,Shang Zhou,Hanfei Chen,Yuzhong Zhang,Bohan Lyu,Qu Ao,Han Zheng,Chonghe Jiang,Jingzhuo Hu,Xiaokun Chen,Kunjun Li,Zhiqing Zhong,Haoye Tian,Tong Zhao,Yunzhong He,Hanchen Li,Ofir Press,Jiantao Jiao,Kunle Olukotun,Karthik R. Narasimhan,Jingbo Shang,Koushik Sen,Matei Zaharia,Alex Dimakis,Ion Stoica,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FrontierCS 2 基准，用 48 个开放式前沿计算机科学研发任务评估 LLM agent 的科研贡献能力；任务由作者原论文代码库构成，需 agent 提交补丁与人类参考实现竞争，最佳 agent Claude Opus 5.5 通过率 58%，16 个任务无一运行通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放式基准只给问题陈述、跑完评一个程序，无法反映真实研发过程；本文旨在检验 LLM agent 能否在前沿计算机科学研发中做出实质性贡献。

Method: 构造 48 个开放式任务（覆盖 13 个领域：ML 系统、高性能计算、数据库、安全、机器人等），并保留 100+ 私有任务后续公布。流程为：取论文及其作者开源代码，去除论文所述贡献点作为待完成目标，删去的代码作为人类参考基线。Agent 被置于研究环境中，继承可运行仓库，可随时提交 patch；评审对每个 patch 返回开发集分数，但隐藏评测器、留出最终测试集与参考实现，最终提交在最终测试集上与参考实现对比评分。共评测 12 个 agent。

Result: 最佳 agent 为 Claude Opus 5.5，58% 的运行通过；有 16 个任务无任何运行通过。得分高不等于通过：多数在综合最终分数上超过参考实现的运行，仍在某些单项指标或工作负载上不达标。在 agent 与任务的共享能力标度上，中位任务难度大致相当于最强 agent 的能力上限。

Conclusion: 开放式研发基准应同时考察过程性提交与多指标综合表现，而非单一程序得分；当前最强 LLM agent 仍有相当比例任务完全无法攻克，且"分数超越参考"与"真正通过"存在显著差距。

Abstract: Can LLM agents make contributions to frontier computer science R&D? We introduce FrontierCS 2, a benchmark of 48 open-ended tasks. We take a paper and the code its authors released and delete the paper's contribution. The deleted code becomes the human reference that sets the bar. Earlier open-ended benchmarks give each agent a problem statement and grade one program after the run. FrontierCS 2 places the agent inside a research environment. The agent inherits a working repository and may submit a patch at any point. For each patch, a judge returns a development-suite score but keeps the evaluator, the held-out final suite and the reference private, and grades the final submission on that suite against the reference. The tasks span 13 domains, from machine-learning systems and high-performance computing to databases, security and robotics. Beyond these 48 tasks, FrontierCS 2 keeps a private set of more than 100 tasks, to be announced in the future. We evaluate 12 agents. The best one is Claude Opus 5.5, passing 58% of its runs. No run passes on 16 tasks. A higher score is not the same as a pass: most runs that beat the reference on the combined final score still fall short on some metric or workload. On a shared capability scale for agents and tasks, the median task is roughly as difficult as what the strongest agent can handle.

</details>


### [226] [CORD: Conflict-Aware Optimization for Joint RL and Distillation in Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29965)
*Wenhao Yu,Zhengcheng Wang,Jiahong Liu,Zexuan Qiu,Minda Hu,Shaohang Wei,Jiafei Lyu,Junyan Lyu,Haobo Fu,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORD：一种面向双目标冲突的梯度组合框架，将"保护哪个目标"与"何时投影"解耦，以最近点闭式解实现选择性修正，可即插即用地嵌入不同联合训练配方，在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上稳定提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合强化学习与自蒸馏中，任务奖励梯度与密集教师引导梯度会部分相互抵消；现有做法仅用混合系数调节相对尺度，无法选择性剔除方向相反的分量，导致局部优化进展损失。

Method: CORD 框架：分离"被保护目标"的选择与"触发投影"的条件；按每次优化器 mini-batch 进行一次梯度组合；采用最近点（nearest-point）形式化得到闭式更新，并刻画在优化器变换前对保护目标的一阶影响；用梯度诊断把智能体训练中的冲突与局部优化进展损失联系起来，从而支持选择性修正；作为梯度组合机制可叠加于多种联合训练配方并保留其原有目标。

Result: 在三种联合训练配方上，ALFWorld 成功率提升 1.5–15.9 个百分点；WebShop 上三组中的两组得到提升，其中 SDAR 由 60.7% 提升至 64.6%。

Conclusion: 保护方向与投影门控必须协同选择；即使经投影修正，蒸馏目标本身及其混合权重仍具实质影响。

Abstract: Joint reinforcement learning and self-distillation combines task rewards with dense teacher guidance for agent training, but their gradients can partly cancel. A mixing coefficient controls relative scale without selectively removing opposing components. We present CORD, a general framework for conflict-aware optimization of two objectives. CORD separates the choice of protected objective from the condition that triggers projection, and combines the gradients once per optimizer mini-batch. A nearest-point formulation yields a closed-form update and characterizes its first-order effect on the protected objective before optimizer transformations. Gradient diagnostics connect conflict in agent training to lost local optimization progress and motivate selective correction. As a gradient-combination mechanism, CORD can be added to different joint training recipes while retaining their objectives. Across three such recipes, it improves ALFWorld success by 1.5-15.9 percentage points. On WebShop, it improves two of the three pairs, with SDAR rising from 60.7% to 64.6% success. Our analyses explain why protection direction and projection gate must be chosen together, and why the distillation objective and its mixing weight remain consequential after projection.

</details>


### [227] [SCDM: Scalable Causal Discovery in Nonlinear Temporal Systems with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29992)
*Jingbo Wang,Kegeng Tang,Zihao Wang,Shaogang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCDM：共享参数元学习框架，将每个目标变量视为一个任务，学习共享时间预测器与共享因果强度矩阵，实现可扩展的非线性时序因果发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非线性时序因果发现中候选有向关系数随变量数平方增长；现有方法受限于逐目标建模、重复条件检验或昂贵的图搜索。

Method: 将每个目标变量视为任务，跨任务学习共享时间预测器与共享因果强度矩阵；每个元回合仅更新部分目标变量任务以累积全系统结构证据；训练后通过图读出将预测器转为连续有向因果评分，支持阈值无关评估；并给出有限误差恢复原理。

Result: 在完全观测延迟系统假设与正风险间隙条件下，当统计误差、元优化误差与读出误差低于总体间隔时，对SCDM评分阈值化可恢复真实图；在合成、类神经影像、高维及真实时序基准上取得有竞争力的因果恢复效果并提升大规模系统可扩展性。

Conclusion: SCDM通过共享参数元学习避免了逐目标优化与图搜索，兼具理论恢复保证与大规模时序系统上的可扩展性。

Abstract: Causal discovery from nonlinear multivariate time series is challenging in high-dimensional systems, where the number of candidate directed relations grows quadratically with the number of variables. Existing methods are often limited by target-wise model fitting, repeated conditional testing, or expensive graph-search procedures. We propose SCDM, a shared-parameter meta-learning framework for scalable temporal causal discovery. SCDM treats each target variable as a task while learning a shared temporal predictor and a shared causal-strength matrix across tasks. Each meta-episode updates only a subset of target-variable tasks, allowing the model to accumulate structural evidence across the full system without exhaustive target-wise optimization. After training, a post-training graph readout converts the learned predictor into continuous directed causal scores for threshold-independent evaluation. We also provide a finite-error recovery principle showing that, under fully observed delayed-system assumptions and a positive risk-gap condition, thresholding SCDM scores recovers the graph when statistical, meta-optimization, and readout errors are below the population margin. Experiments on controlled synthetic, neuroimaging-inspired, high-dimensional, and real-world temporal benchmarks demonstrate competitive causal recovery and improved scalability in large temporal systems.

</details>


### [228] [EPIC: Rethinking Trust Regions for Autoregressive LLM Policies via Exact-KL Prefix Importance-Ratio Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29999)
*Chengxi Li,Hanlin Wu,Zhicheng Cai,Huan-ang Gao,Wei-Ying Ma,Jingjing Liu,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PPO式token级裁剪仅约束当前token条件概率、忽略前缀由策略生成的问题，本文将自回归生成重构为有限时域MDP，推导性能差恒等式，识别出前缀累积似然比为精确重要性权重，并基于trust-region单调改进原则用逆KL坐标构造前缀级信任域，提出EPIC序列裁剪方法，在稀疏MoE的离策略强化学习中优于标准和序列级裁剪基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准token级裁剪仅通过当前token的条件概率度量策略变化，而前缀条件上下文本身由策略生成，存在度量错配；该问题在长回答和离策略训练中尤为突出，导致策略漂移约束不准确。

Method: 将自回归生成重构为有限时域MDP，在行为策略采样下推导性能差恒等式，指出生成前缀的累积似然比是每步策略改进项的精确重要性权重；从信任域策略优化的单调改进原则出发，用精确逆KL坐标 r−1−log r 构造前缀级信任域（其行为策略期望等于前缀分布KL散度），得到EPIC——一种直接调控前缀级策略漂移的PPO式序列裁剪方法，不依赖局部二次近似或专家路由回放。

Result: 在稀疏混合专家语言模型的离策略强化学习实验中，EPIC在数学推理基准上持续优于标准和序列级裁剪基线，并与基于路由回放的替代方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 前缀级信任域是实现更精确策略漂移控制的有效途径，EPIC以精确逆KL坐标实现单调改进原则的序列级裁剪，无需二次近似或路由回放即可在离策略MoE场景取得更优的数学推理表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) based on PPO-style clipping has become an effective approach to improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, standard token-level clipping measures policy change only through the conditional probability of the current token, while the prefix conditioning context is itself generated by the policy. This mismatch becomes especially consequential in long-response and off-policy training. We reformulate autoregressive generation as a finite-horizon Markov decision process and derive its performance-difference identity under behavior-policy sampling. The resulting change of measure identifies the cumulative likelihood ratio of the generated prefix as the exact importance weight for each stepwise policy-improvement term. Starting from the monotonic-improvement principle of trust-region policy optimization, we then construct a prefix-level trust region using the exact reverse-KL coordinate $r-1-\log r$, whose behavior-policy expectation equals the corresponding prefix-distribution KL divergence. This yields EPIC, a PPO-style sequence-clipping method that directly regulates prefix-level policy drift without relying on a local quadratic approximation or expert-routing replay. Experiments on off-policy reinforcement learning with a sparse mixture-of-experts language model show that EPIC consistently outperforms standard and sequence-level clipping baselines across mathematical-reasoning benchmarks, while remaining competitive with routing-replay-based alternatives. Code is available at https://github.com/LiChengxi666/EPIC.

</details>


### [229] [Mitigating Model Collapse through Dreaming Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30009)
*Matteo Benati,Alessandro Londei,Denise Lanzieri,Raffaello Mastromarino,Vittorio Loreto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从谱角度研究合成数据训练生成模型导致的"模型坍缩"，指出低温采样($T_s<1$)相当于对转移算子反复"锐化"，使次主特征值趋近单位圆、谱隙收窄；而高温"Dreaming Learning"正则项可保持算子元素不消失，使特征值远离单位圆，从而缓解坍缩，并在LSTM与124M GPT-2上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据训练的生成模型会出现多样性丧失与性能下降（模型坍缩），但缺乏对退化过程的机理刻画。作者希望在可控的马尔可夫设定下，从学习到的转移算子的特征值谱这一可直接观测的角度，揭示坍缩的动力学本质，并据此设计缓解手段。

Method: 采用受控马尔可夫链设定，将训练过程视为对转移算子谱结构的作用。分析温度采样$T_s<1$对算子的影响（类比反复锐化），推导次主特征值与谱隙的变化；再引入Dreaming Learning——并行地对模型自身以升温$T_d>1$采样得到的样本加一项损失，抑制算子元素趋于零。通过5000代训练的谱测量进行验证，并推广到LSTM语言模型与124M参数GPT-2。

Result: $T_s<1$时次主特征值逼近单位圆、谱隙收窄，算子趋于确定性系统，且即使积累数据也无法阻止该漂移；Dreaming Learning因高温样本展平预测分布，保持算子元素非零，使特征值有界地远离单位圆。5000代谱测量与理论一致，缓解效果在LSTM和124M GPT-2等复杂模型中依然存在。

Conclusion: 模型坍缩可被理解为学习算子谱结构的系统性漂移；采样温度是控制该漂移的关键因素，低温加剧坍缩而升温的自我采样正则化可有效抑制。谱分析为理解与缓解生成模型退化提供了机理化框架，并具跨模型规模的可迁移性。

Abstract: Training generative models on synthetic data causes loss of diversity and a decrease in performance, a phenomenon known as model collapse. We study this degeneration spectrally in a controlled Markov setting. The eigenvalue spectrum of the learned transition operator offers a direct view of the process, since the subdominant eigenvalues set how fast the chain mixes and therefore how much of the state space it continues to explore. We find that sampling at temperature $T_s < 1$ acts on the operator like repeated sharpening, pushing it toward a deterministic system: the subdominant eigenvalues approach the unit circle and the spectral gap narrows. When $T_s < 1$, even data accumulation does not appear to arrest this drift. We then analyze Dreaming Learning, a regulariser that, in parallel with regular training, adds a loss term on the model's own samples drawn at a raised temperature $T_d > 1$. Because high-temperature samples flatten the predicted distribution, training on them keeps the entries of the learned operator from becoming vanishingly small, which in turn keeps the eigenvalues bounded away from the unit circle. Measurements of the spectra over 5000 generations are consistent with this picture, and the mitigation effect persists in more complex settings such as LSTM language models and a 124M-parameter GPT-2.

</details>


### [230] [ExGrad: Beyond Suppression - Explicit Clean-Gradient Subtraction in Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30018)
*Zhiyuan Ye,Xiangyu Zhou,Ji Qi,Hao Zhang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExGrad通过重组合干净梯度、对抗梯度与AWP梯度，在仿射半径约束下求解三变量系数，发现负的干净梯度系数随半径增大而占主导，并据此在多个数据集上超越AWP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练依赖代理损失的下降，但源损失的下降并不能直接刻画某个更新对对抗间隔的影响，因此需要一种以"间隔响应"为依据来构造更新的方法。

Method: 构建干净输入梯度、对抗输入梯度与对抗权重扰动(AWP)梯度；用虚拟步测量各自对间隔的响应；在无负约束的有界仿射域上求解一个全局可解的三变量系数选择问题；并设计跨零干预、投影诊断、范数匹配控制等消融实验。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上负干净系数随半径迅速占主导且幅值增大；将其置零不足，适度减法提升PGD-20，更强减法以干净精度换更多鲁棒增益；WRN-28-4下三种候选移除变体均不及完整ExGrad；负系数常伴随正净干净轴投影；范数匹配表明增益非仅由更新幅值解释。CIFAR-100(R=0.8)五项指标全升，干净与AutoAttack分别+3.84、+1.75个百分点；ImageNet-100四项鲁棒指标均优于AWP与PGD-AT。

Conclusion: 干净梯度减法是一种由间隔响应揭示的更新偏好，其强度与干净-鲁棒权衡操作点由仿射半径控制。

Abstract: Adversarial training derives updates from surrogate losses, but source-loss descent does not directly characterize an update's effect on adversarial margins. We introduce ExGrad (extrapolative gradient recomposition), which constructs clean-input, adversarial-input, and adversarial-weight-perturbed (AWP) gradients. Virtual steps measure their margin responses, and a globally solvable three-variable problem selects coefficients over a bounded affine domain without nonnegativity constraints. Across affine radii on CIFAR-10, negative clean coefficients rapidly predominate and increase in magnitude with the radius. Cross-zero interventions show that setting the clean coefficient to zero is insufficient: mild subtraction improves PGD-20, while stronger subtraction trades clean accuracy for further PGD-20 gains. In CIFAR-10 WRN-28-4 controls, none of the three candidate-removal variants matches full ExGrad. Projection diagnostics show that negative clean coefficients usually coexist with positive net clean-axis projections, although both decrease as the radius grows. Norm-matched controls indicate that update magnitude alone does not explain the moderate-radius gains. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-100 show radius-dependent improvements over AWP. On CIFAR-100, ExGrad at $R=0.8$ improves all five reported metrics, including clean and AutoAttack accuracy by 3.84 and 1.75 percentage points. On ImageNet-100, the same radius improves all four robust metrics over both AWP and PGD-AT. These results identify clean-gradient subtraction as a response-revealed update preference whose strength and clean-robust operating point are controlled by the affine radius.

</details>


### [231] [Loopholing All the Way: Language Modeling with pure Self-Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30068)
*Adrien Deschenaux,Nadezhda Ilieva,Justin Deschenaux,Manuel Madeira,Dorina Thanou,Aurélien Lucchi,Antonio Orvieto,Caglar Gulcehre,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LAW（Loopholing All the Way），将自条件化（Self-Conditioning）从辅助启发式提升为连续生成模型的核心机制，通过在去噪器预测下逐步传播期望嵌入、仅在最后一次性解码token，在Sudoku、Maze、GSM8K及OpenWebText上超越离散/连续扩散模型并逼近自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期扩散语言模型（DLM）使用自条件化（SC，又称Loopholing）提升性能，但SC传统上被视为辅助启发式。其经验成功引出一个问题：连续生成模型能否完全基于自条件化构建？论文对此给出肯定回答。

Method: 提出LAW框架：沿去噪步骤传播去噪器预测下的期望嵌入（Expected Embedding），在全部步骤中保持连续表示，仅在最终阶段进行一次token解码，从而摒弃传统逐步离散解码与辅助启发式。

Result: Sudoku上优于此前离散与连续扩散模型（含Loopholing Discrete Diffusion）；Maze上在2–64 NFE范围内最准确；TinyGSM训练、温度1、1024 NFE采样下GSM8K解出42.2%（对比uniform Loopholing Discrete Diffusion的34.5%）；微调预训练Duo将准确率从17.2%提升至48.1%；OpenWebText上推进Pareto前沿，64 NFE下逼近自回归模型。

Conclusion: 自条件化本身足以支撑连续生成模型，LAW以纯连续传播加末端解码的简洁设计，在结构化推理与文本生成任务上取得竞争力表现，并显示对预训练扩散模型微调的有效性。

Abstract: Recent Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) use Self-Conditioning (SC), or Loopholing, to improve their performance. While SC is traditionally seen as an auxiliary heuristic, its empirical success raises the question: can a continuous generative model be based purely on Self-Conditioning? We answer affirmatively with LAW (``Loopholing All the Way''), which propagates the Expected Embedding under the denoiser's prediction across steps, decoding the tokens once, at the end. On Sudoku, LAW outperforms previous Discrete and Continuous Diffusion Models, including Loopholing Discrete Diffusion. On Maze, LAW is the most accurate from 2 to 64 NFE. After training on TinyGSM and sampling at temperature 1 with 1024 NFE, LAW solves 42.2% of GSM8K problems against 34.5% for uniform Loopholing Discrete Diffusion. Additionally, fine-tuning a pre-trained Uniform Diffusion Model (Duo) with LAW increases its accuracy from 17.2% to 48.1%. Finally, on OpenWebText, LAW pushes the Pareto frontier and approaches Autoregressive Models (ARMs) with 64 NFE. Find our code at https://github.com/ilievanadezhda/law.

</details>


### [232] [From Classifier Heads to Encoder Prototypes: Personalized Federated Learning with Head-Derived Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30075)
*Amirardalan Dehghanpour,Arian Noori,Ehsan Tanghatari,Naimeh Omidvar,Hamid R. Rabiee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HDC-FL 利用共享编码器训练中已产生的客户端分类头作为聚类信号，周期性将缓存的分类头投影到低秩描述子空间并分组，为每组构造编码器原型，客户端最终获得原型软混合 + 自身不变分类头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习需要决定哪些客户端共享表示，但额外引入聚类或二次优化轨迹会带来开销并可能破坏共享编码器训练。作者希望从训练过程已有的信号中直接读出该决策。

Method: 在周期性刷新轮次中，服务器将缓存的客户端分类头投影到归一化低秩描述子空间，对描述子聚类，用缓存的客户端编码器为每组构建一个编码器原型，并向每个客户端返回这些原型的软混合与其自身未改动的分类头。分类头从不被平均或分发，共享编码器轨迹保持不变，无需第二优化轨迹、额外本地目标或训练后微调。

Result: 在 11 组 CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100 配置（标签子集、Dirichlet、旋转异质性；ResNet-18 与 MobileNetV2 编码器）上，宏平均平均准确率与最强个性化/聚类基线相当，并在所评估方法中取得数值最高的宏平均最差客户端准确率与末位 20% 准确率。捐赠者交换诊断显示三种设定下头相似度与编码器兼容性均呈正秩相关，协变量偏移下最明显。

Conclusion: 分类头可作为可靠的客户端分组代理信号，尾部分布指标能区分均值相近的方法；该方法在保持共享编码器训练不变的前提下提升个性化联邦学习的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning must decide which clients should share representations. We introduce HDC-FL (Head-Derived Clustering for Personalized Federated Learning), which reads that decision off a signal shared-encoder training already produces: the client-specific classifi- er heads. At periodic refresh rounds the server projects the cached heads into a normalized low-rank descriptor space, groups the descriptors, builds one encoder prototype per group from the cached client encoders, and returns to each client a soft mixture of those prototypes paired with its own unchanged head. Client-specific heads are never averaged or distributed to oth- er clients; the shared-encoder trajectory is left intact; and no second optimization trajectory, additional local objective, or post-training fine-tuning stage is required. Across 11 CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 configurations covering label-subset, Dirichlet and rotation heterogeneity with ResNet-18 and MobileNetV2 encoders, HDC-FL is competitive with the strongest personalized and clustered baselines in macro-averaged mean accuracy and attains the numerically highest macro-averaged worst-client and bottom-20% accuracy among the evaluated methods. The tail results distinguish methods that look similar when only their means are compared. A controlled donor-swap diagnostic further finds positive donor-ranking associations between head similarity and encoder compatibility in all three settings tested, most clearly under covariate shift.

</details>


### [233] [Circuits of LLM Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30076)
*Hadi Khalaf,Sajani Vithana,Kunal Talreja,Haewon Jeong,Viveck R. Cadambe,Flavio Calmon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LLM分类器视为对潜在标签的噪声测量仪器，把多个分类器组织成自适应查询-判定"电路"，在给定平均查询成本下最大化平均分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用提示词让LLM做分类很便捷，但其预测既非确定性、可靠性也因样本而异。已有的路由（routing）与级联（cascading）方案未能充分利用多模型预测的组合信息，亟需在成本-准确率权衡上取得更优的可靠性。

Method: 把LLM分类器建模为对潜在真实标签的含噪测量工具；构造"电路"（circuits），将多个LLM分类器组织为自适应的查询与决策序列；刻画最优电路结构，推导可达准确率的基本极限，并给出实现最优电路的实用建议。

Result: 在覆盖安全、健康、政治科学、人类偏好、智能体评估、决策与推理的七个分类任务上，该方法在成本-准确率前沿上优于路由与级联基线。

Conclusion: 自适应查询电路为整合多LLM分类器提供了有理论支撑的框架，可在同等平均查询成本下提升平均分类准确率，并具备实际可部署性。

Abstract: Language models make it easy to build classifiers by simply prompting a model with the sample to classify. However, their predictions are neither deterministic nor uniformly reliable across samples. How can we combine predictions from different models to improve reliability? We view LLM classifiers as noisy measurement instruments of an underlying target label. Our goal is to maximize average classification accuracy at a given average query cost. To this end, we introduce circuits that organize LLM classifiers into an adaptive sequence of queries and decisions. We then characterize the optimal circuit, obtain the fundamental limits of achievable accuracy, and provide practical insights on realizing the optimal circuit. Across seven classification tasks spanning safety, health, political science, human preference, agent evaluation, decision-making and reasoning, our method improves the cost-accuracy frontier over routing and cascading baselines.

</details>


### [234] [Orbitol: Crystal Generation on Wyckoff Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30086)
*Ivan Žugec,Joshua Rosenthal,Ivor Lončarić,Jan-Willem van de Meent,Tin Hadži Veljković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Crystalite扩散Transformer的晶体生成器Orbitol，以Wyckoff轨道token替代原子token，在给定空间群与占据Wyckoff位置条件下生成原子类型、自由坐标与兼容晶格，保证结构满足目标群对称性，并引入SPARC评分与压电材料筛选应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无条件生成模型无法直接控制空间群，而空间群决定了压电性等功能响应是否被对称性允许，因此需要可指定空间群的对称性可控晶体生成方法。

Method: Orbitol构建于Crystalite扩散Transformer之上，将原子token替换为Wyckoff轨道token；输入空间群及占据的Wyckoff位置集合，输出每个轨道的原子类型、自由坐标以及与之兼容的晶格，使生成结构在目标群操作下不变。同时提出SPARC指标，融合原始检出空间群的保持率与保持群和训练分布的一致性。

Result: 在评估设置下生成速度比对称性感知基线快2–3个数量级；MP-20上13.8%的尝试得到弛豫后仍保持目标空间群的稳定/亚稳、唯一且新颖结构，优于次优模型SGFM的10.8%；用于压电晶类生成时，内部筛选所得结构中89%通过DFT检验，79%在Materials Project与Alexandria中无同空间群条目，品质因数中位数为MP压电材料的9倍，24%进入该集合前10%。

Conclusion: 以Wyckoff轨道为生成单元可高效实现空间群精确可控的晶体生成，在稳定性、新颖性与速度上优于对称性感知基线，并在压电材料定向发现中展现显著的筛选通过率与性能优势。

Abstract: Generative models propose crystalline materials by learning composition and geometry, but unconditional sampling gives no direct control over the space group, which constrains whether responses such as piezoelectricity are symmetry-allowed. We present Orbitol, a crystal generator built on the Crystalite diffusion Transformer that replaces atom tokens with Wyckoff-orbit tokens. Given a space group and a collection of occupied Wyckoff positions, Orbitol generates the atom type and free coordinates of each orbit together with a compatible lattice, so every valid structure is invariant under the operations of the requested group. It generates structures two to three orders of magnitude faster than the symmetry-aware baselines at the evaluated settings. On MP-20, 13.8% of Orbitol's attempts yielded stable or metastable, unique and novel structures that retained the requested space group after relaxation, compared with 10.8% for SGFM, the next best model. We also introduce SPARC, a score that combines retention of the raw detected space group with agreement between the retained-group and training distributions. Finally, we used Orbitol to generate candidates in piezoelectric crystal classes. Of the structures selected by internal screening, 89% passed our density functional theory checks, and 79% also matched no Materials Project or Alexandria entry with the same space group. Their median figure of merit was nine times that of the piezoelectric materials computed in the Materials Project, and 24% of the new candidates ranked in that set's top 10%.

</details>


### [235] [Velocity Ambiguity Profiles: Time-Resolved Bayes-Risk Diagnostics for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30107)
*Yunpeng Mei,Xiaowen Zhu,Chenyu Wang,Chenbo Xin,Hongjie Cao,Jiamin Wang,Jie Chen,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出速度模糊度剖面VAP 𝒜_v(t)，将流匹配时间分辨损失分解为不可约贝叶斯风险地板与可约超额项，并给出统计基准与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配通常只报告在插值时间上平均的损失，掩盖了哪些时间区域受内在模糊性限制、哪些受数据量限制，缺乏时间分辨的诊断工具。

Method: 定义VAP 𝒜_v(t)为速度预测的时间局部贝叶斯风险地板，将损失分解为L_N(t)=𝒜_v(t)+R(t;N)；在结构化混合模型下证明两个统计基准：(1)内在局部回归基准中𝒜_v(t)进入非参数速率的时间依赖噪声因子；(2)分离各向同性高斯混合中贝叶斯速度局部仿射，标签预言机残差回归超额风险尺度为K𝒜_v(t)/N。配合高斯混合受控实验、预言机—盲测—经验代理审计与时采样压力测试，并在CIFAR-100 DINOv2特征流上验证。

Result: 高斯混合实验通过稠密过渡扫描、校准审计与局部时采样压力测试验证诊断有效性，冻结的可约误差代理可降低可约损失，跨单元敏感性界定激进采样边界。CIFAR-100 DINOv2特征流上，引导时间采样使均匀评估速度损失降6.78%、全局特征Fréchet距离降23.6%，但类条件KID无改善。

Conclusion: VAP将平均化的流匹配目标转化为时间分辨诊断，可定位模糊性限制与数据限制区域；实践上指导时间采样以降低可约误差与全局分布距离，但对类条件生成质量提升有限。

Abstract: Flow matching trains a continuum of velocity regression problems but usually reports a loss averaged over interpolation time, obscuring which regions are limited by intrinsic ambiguity and which remain data-limited. We introduce the Velocity Ambiguity Profile (VAP), $\mathcal{A}_v(t)$, the time-local Bayes-risk floor of velocity prediction, and decompose time-resolved loss as $L_N(t)=\mathcal{A}_v(t)+R(t;N)$, where only the excess term $R(t;N)$ is reducible by more data or a different estimator. For $N$ training samples and a $K$-component mixture in the structured example, we prove two statistical benchmarks. In an intrinsic local-regression benchmark, $\mathcal{A}_v(t)$ enters the time-dependent noise factor of the standard nonparametric rate. In separated isotropic Gaussian mixtures, the Bayes velocity becomes locally affine, and a label-oracle residual-regression benchmark has parametric excess-risk scale $K\mathcal{A}_v(t)/N$, matched within the same separated residual Gaussian-location subproblem. Controlled Gaussian-mixture experiments validate this diagnostic through a dense transition sweep, calibrated oracle-to-blind and empirical-proxy audits, and a local controlled time-sampler stress test in which a frozen reducible-error proxy reduces reducible loss while cross-cell sensitivity delimits aggressive sampling. On CIFAR-100 DINOv2 feature flow, guided time sampling reduces uniformly evaluated velocity loss by $6.78\%$ and global feature Fréchet distance (FD) by $23.6\%$, while class-conditional kernel inception distance (KID) shows no improvement. VAP turns an averaged flow-matching objective into a time-resolved diagnostic for locating ambiguity-limited and data-limited regions.

</details>


### [236] [The role of locality and weight sharing in convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30118)
*Francesco Mori,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用师生模型解析地比较了卷积与局部连接网络的学习动态，揭示权重共享与局部性如何分别塑造学习，且其收益依赖自然图像的空间相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积网络的成功常归因于局部性与权重共享，但二者各自如何影响学习、以及其效果如何依赖自然图像的空间相关性，仍缺乏清晰理解。

Method: 构建师生模型：卷积教师在位置间相关性为 c 的 P 个输入位置上工作；比较卷积学生与局部连接学生，用在线随机梯度下降训练，并在特征维度 D→∞ 极限下推导精确描述其动力学的常微分方程。

Result: c=0 时两学生学习轨迹相同，卷积学生快 P 倍；局部性影响样本复杂度：核大小与教师匹配需 O_D(D) 样本，核更大需 O_D(D log D)。c>0 时空间相关性削弱权重共享优势：卷积学生渐近误差随 c 增大，局部连接学生误差与 c 无关且同学习率下更低；若按训练时程调优学习率，早期局部连接学生占优、后期卷积学生占优，并给出解析预测的转变点。

Conclusion: 局部性与权重共享的作用可分离且可量化：权重共享带来收敛速度增益，局部性决定样本复杂度，而空间相关性调节二者权衡，学习率策略决定谁在早期/后期更优。

Abstract: The success of convolutional neural networks is often attributed to locality and weight sharing. However, how these two properties separately shape learning, and how their effects depend on the spatial correlations of natural images, remain poorly understood. We address these questions in a teacher-student model with a convolutional teacher acting on $P$ input positions with correlation $c$ between positions. We compare convolutional and locally connected students trained by online stochastic gradient descent, and derive ordinary differential equations that describe their dynamics exactly in the limit of large feature dimension $D$. For uncorrelated positions ($c = 0$), the two students follow the same learning trajectory, with the convolutional student learning exactly $P$ times faster. We also find that locality affects the sample complexity: a student that matches the kernel size of the teacher recovers it from $\mathcal{O}_{D}(D)$ samples, whereas $\mathcal{O}_{D}(D\log(D))$ samples are needed with a larger kernel. For $c > 0$, spatial correlations can reduce the advantage of weight sharing. The asymptotic error of the convolutional student grows with $c$, whereas that of the locally connected student is independent of $c$ and overall lower when evaluated at the same learning rate. When the learning rate is instead tuned to the training horizon, however, the locally connected student wins at early times and the convolutional student at late times, with a transition that we predict analytically.

</details>


### [237] [Self-normalized Bernstein Inequalities for Regenerative Processes and Applications to Learning Without Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30119)
*Imon Banerjee,Pierre Alquier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为具有独立同分布标记周期（有限平均周期长度）的再生过程的有界函数，证明了自归一化 Bernstein 不等式，并将其应用于机器学习中的泛化误差分析与高斯近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Bernstein 不等式依赖独立同分布假设，而实际数据（如时间序列、马尔可夫链）常呈非独立同分布且具再生结构；同时希望界中各项尽量可经验观测、可用于构造学习目标，并刻画其对样本量与期望再生时间的依赖是否最优。

Method: 以再生过程的标记周期为基本单元，利用观测到的周期长度构造自归一化 Bernstein 型偏差界，显式保留截断项与观测水平线后"末周期"项；从有限确定性集合中选取调优参数，得到除与再生时间尾部相关的项外均为经验可计算的形式，并对该剩余项在多类情形下进行阶数分析；进一步结合再生强逼近，采用可观测的块方差估计量构造高斯近似。

Result: 得到的界中绝大部分项可由数据经验计算，剩余尾部项在多种关注情形下为二阶小量；由此建立非独立同分布再生数据分类的泛化误差界，可仅最小化界的一阶项定义 agnostic 分类器并仍满足 oracle 不等式；同时证明其平均偏差界在样本量与期望再生时间两个维度上均达到 minimax 最优；并给出基于可观测块方差估计的高斯近似。

Conclusion: 该工作将自归一化 Bernstein 不等式推广至再生过程，提供经验可用的偏差与泛化误差界，兼具最优性与可操作性，为依赖数据的非独立同分布机器学习提供了理论基础与实用工具。

Abstract: We prove a self-normalized Bernstein inequality for bounded functions of a regenerative process with independent, identically distributed marked cycles of finite mean length. The bound uses observed cycle lengths and retains explicit terms for truncation and the cycle ending after the observation horizon. Selecting a tuning parameter from a finite deterministic collection gives a version of Bernstein inequality where all terms are empirical but one, related to the tails of the regeneration times. We then study this term in many regimes: it turns out that it is of second-order in many cases of interest. This is important for applications in machine learning: we prove generalization error bounds for classification with non-i.i.d., regenerative data. These bounds can be used to define learning objectives: by minimizing the first-order term only in the bound, we define agnostic classifiers that still satisfy oracle inequalities. We also show that the bound on the average deviation provided by our results is minimax-optimal in the sample size and in the expected regeneration time. Finally, we combine the Bernstein inequality with a regenerative strong approximation to obtain a Gaussian approximation using an observable block variance estimator.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [RESEARCHHUB: A Personalized Literature Discovery System for Biomedical Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28636)
*Viswonathan Manoranjan,Taeyoon Kim,Nicholas Do,Sang Woo Kim,Dawei Li,Zhen Tan,Tianlong Chen,Yunmei Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ResearchHub：面向生物医学预印本的个性化文献发现交互系统，核心为经GPT-4o合成查询与混合排序目标适配的prism双编码器检索器，并支持新颖性/多样性控制与课程式排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统易陷入近重复推荐，且通用稠密检索器对生物医学预印本领域适配不足，用户难以从主流文献平滑过渡到专精文献并理解推荐理由。

Method: 构建MedRxiv领域语料库；prism双编码器检索器以GPT-4o生成的查询进行适配，训练采用Multiple Negatives Ranking与MarginMSE混合目标（含cross-encoder软标签）；系统层面引入基于阅读历史的前沿检索、DPP多样性重排、基于局部内在维度（LID）的从主流到专精的课程式排序，以及轻量归因卡片解释。

Result: 在2023–2025年44,937篇MedRxiv论文上，prism优于现成强稠密检索器，Recall@10较SPECTER2-Search提升12.4个百分点；全流程低延迟运行。

Conclusion: 领域适配检索+可控新颖性与多样性+可解释排序可有效提升个性化文献发现体验，系统具备实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: We present ResearchHub, an interactive system for personalized literature discovery over biomedical preprints. Given a researcher profile or a free-form query, ResearchHub retrieves relevant papers from a domain-specific MedRxiv corpus and helps users move beyond near-duplicate recommendations through controllable novelty, diversity, and curriculum ordering. At the core of the system is prism, a bi-encoder retriever adapted to biomedical preprints using GPT-4o-generated natural language queries and a hybrid Multiple Negatives Ranking plus MarginMSE objective with cross-encoder soft labels. On 44,937 MedRxiv papers from 2023--2025, prism outperforms strong off-the-shelf dense retrievers, improving Recall@10 over SPECTER2-Search by 12.4 percentage points. ResearchHub further supports frontier retrieval based on a user's reading history, DPP-based diversity re-ranking, Local Intrinsic Dimensionality ordering from mainstream to specialized papers, and lightweight attribution cards for result explanation. The full pipeline runs with low latency, making the system suitable

</details>


### [239] [TAGR: Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval across Heterogeneous Urban Delivery Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28638)
*Zhi Liu,Yurun Cai,Xiao Han,Zhi Zhen,Yuran Li,Guojiang Shen,Xiangjie Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TAGR：面向城市配送异质任务的"任务自适应地理-语义检索"框架，用LLM离线构建地理语义共享先验，并为每个任务建立独立检索库与任务自适应检索增强模块，在三个城市数据集的多任务上稳定优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市即时配送中，需求预测、ETA预测、下一站点预测三任务所依赖的历史证据在结构与粒度上差异显著，输出形式也不同；单一统一的检索-融合机制易造成证据失配与负迁移，导致预测误差上升与资源错配（部分区域需求未满足、部分区域资源闲置），降低整体效率与服务质量。

Method: 提出TAGR框架：(1) 基于LLM构建地理-语义共享先验，生成编码区域地理上下文与区域间语义关系的区域表示，为三任务提供可迁移知识，且无需在线LLM推理；(2) 为各任务构建任务特定检索库，在共享区域知识的同时保留异质任务证据；(3) 引入任务自适应检索增强模块，适配不同输出结构，缓解统一检索融合策略带来的信息失配与负迁移。

Result: 在三个城市数据集上，TAGR在多个任务上一致优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过"共享地理语义先验+任务特定检索库+任务自适应检索增强"的解耦设计，可有效缓解异质城市配送任务间的证据失配与负迁移，提升多任务预测性能；该框架具备无需在线LLM推理的部署友好性。

Abstract: As urban residents demand higher-quality instant delivery services, demand forecasting, estimated time of arrival prediction, and next-stop prediction have become critical tasks in urban delivery systems. However, the three tasks rely on historical evidence with substantially different structures and granularities. Meanwhile, their prediction targets differ in output form, making it difficult to integrate retrieved evidence through a single fusion mechanism without causing mismatch or negative transfer. These issues can increase prediction errors and subsequently lead to imbalanced resource allocation, where some regions experience unmet demand.
